===== FILE START: app/admin/avatars/add/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { AdminPageHeader, AdminPrimaryButton, AdminSecondaryButton } from "@/components/admin/AdminPageHeader";
import { AdminCard } from "@/components/admin/AdminCard";
import type { OwnerType, BodyType } from "@/lib/admin/types";

// Deliberately the plain counterpart to the avatar-wizard at
// /admin/avatars/new: that wizard (now linked from the Dashboard as
// "Create simulation") creates an avatar + persona + knowledge + the
// avatar_combinations row that ties them together. This page only calls
// POST /api/admin/avatars — no persona, no knowledge, no combination.
const OWNER_TYPES: OwnerType[] = ["Organisation", "Individual"];
const BODY_TYPES: BodyType[] = ["Full body", "Half body", "Head only"];

export default function AddAvatarPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [owner, setOwner] = useState("");
  const [ownerType, setOwnerType] = useState<OwnerType>("Organisation");
  const [bodyType, setBodyType] = useState<BodyType>("Half body");
  const [imageUrl, setImageUrl] = useState("");
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function handleSave() {
    if (!name.trim()) {
      setError("Name is required.");
      return;
    }
    if (!owner.trim()) {
      setError("Owner is required.");
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/admin/avatars", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name,
          owner,
          ownerType,
          bodyType,
          imageUrl: imageUrl.trim() || undefined,
          status: "Draft",
        }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to create avatar");
      router.push("/admin/avatars");
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to create avatar");
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <AdminPageHeader
        title="Add avatar"
        subtitle="Just the digital human itself — no persona or knowledge attached yet."
      />

      <div className="mt-8 max-w-2xl">
        <AdminCard>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Name</span>
                <input
                  value={name}
                  onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. Aura"
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                />
              </label>
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Owner</span>
                <input
                  value={owner}
                  onChange={(e) => setOwner(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. PwC Tunisia"
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                />
              </label>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Owner type</span>
                <select
                  value={ownerType}
                  onChange={(e) => setOwnerType(e.target.value as OwnerType)}
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                >
                  {OWNER_TYPES.map((t) => (
                    <option key={t} value={t}>
                      {t}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </label>
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Body type</span>
                <select
                  value={bodyType}
                  onChange={(e) => setBodyType(e.target.value as BodyType)}
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                >
                  {BODY_TYPES.map((t) => (
                    <option key={t} value={t}>
                      {t}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </label>
            </div>

            <label className="block">
              <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Image URL (optional)</span>
              <input
                value={imageUrl}
                onChange={(e) => setImageUrl(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="https://…"
                className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
              />
            </label>
          </div>
        </AdminCard>

        {error ? (
          <p className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">{error}</p>
        ) : null}

        <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-between">
          <AdminSecondaryButton onClick={() => router.push("/admin/avatars")}>Cancel</AdminSecondaryButton>
          <AdminPrimaryButton onClick={handleSave} disabled={saving}>
            {saving ? "Creating…" : "Create avatar"}
          </AdminPrimaryButton>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

===== FILE START: app/admin/avatars/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { AdminPageHeader, AdminPrimaryButton } from "@/components/admin/AdminPageHeader";
import { Table, THead, Th, Tr, Td, StatusBadge } from "@/components/admin/table/Table";
import { ActionsMenu } from "@/components/admin/table/ActionsMenu";
import { publishTone } from "@/lib/admin/mock-data"; // keep the helper, drop the array
import type { Avatar } from "@/lib/admin/types";

export default function AvatarsIndexPage() {
  const [avatars, setAvatars] = useState<Avatar[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Pulled out (was inline in a useEffect) so ActionsMenu's Publish/Archive/
  // Delete actions have something to call via onChanged after they PATCH —
  // the table had no way to refresh itself after a row action before.
  const refetch = useCallback(() => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    return fetch("/api/admin/avatars")
      .then(async (res) => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to load avatars");
        return res.json();
      })
      .then((data: Avatar[]) => setAvatars(data))
      .catch((err) => setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load avatars"))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    refetch();
  }, [refetch]);

  return (
    <>
      <AdminPageHeader
        title="Avatars"
        subtitle="Every digital human you've created, and their current status."
        action={
          <Link href="/admin/avatars/add">
            <AdminPrimaryButton>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Plus className="h-4 w-4" /> Add avatar
              </span>
            </AdminPrimaryButton>
          </Link>
        }
      />

      <div className="mt-8">
        {error ? (
          <p className="rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">{error}</p>
        ) : loading ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">Loading avatars…</p>
        ) : avatars.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">No avatars yet — create one to get started.</p>
        ) : (
          <Table>
            <THead>
              <tr>
                <Th>Image</Th>
                <Th>Name</Th>
                <Th>Owner</Th>
                <Th>Owner type</Th>
                <Th>Body type</Th>
                <Th>Status</Th>
                <Th className="text-right">Actions</Th>
              </tr>
            </THead>
            <tbody>
              {avatars.map((avatar) => (
                <Tr key={avatar.id}>
                  <Td>
                    <Link
                      href={`/admin/avatars/${avatar.id}/persona`}
                      className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-accent/10 font-serif text-lg font-semibold text-accent"
                    >
                      {avatar.name[0]}
                    </Link>
                  </Td>
                  <Td className="font-medium text-stone-900">
                    <Link href={`/admin/avatars/${avatar.id}/persona`}>{avatar.name}</Link>
                  </Td>
                  <Td>{avatar.owner}</Td>
                  <Td>{avatar.ownerType}</Td>
                  <Td>{avatar.bodyType}</Td>
                  <Td>
                    <StatusBadge label={avatar.status} tone={publishTone(avatar.status)} />
                  </Td>
                  <Td className="text-right">
                    <ActionsMenu variant="avatar" avatar={avatar} onChanged={refetch} />
                  </Td>
                </Tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </Table>
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

===== FILE END: app/admin/avatars/page.tsx =====

===== FILE START: app/admin/avatars/[avatarId]/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { AdminPageHeader, AdminPrimaryButton, AdminSecondaryButton } from "@/components/admin/AdminPageHeader";
import { AdminCard } from "@/components/admin/AdminCard";
import type { Avatar, OwnerType, BodyType, PublishState } from "@/lib/admin/types";

const OWNER_TYPES: OwnerType[] = ["Organisation", "Individual"];
const BODY_TYPES: BodyType[] = ["Full body", "Half body", "Head only"];
const STATUSES: PublishState[] = ["Private", "Public", "Archived"];

type FormState = {
  name: string;
  owner: string;
  ownerType: OwnerType;
  bodyType: BodyType;
  imageUrl: string;
  status: PublishState;
};

const EMPTY_FORM: FormState = {
  name: "",
  owner: "",
  ownerType: "Organisation",
  bodyType: "Half body",
  imageUrl: "",
  status: "Private",
};

export default function AvatarEditPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useParams<{ avatarId: string }>();

  const [form, setForm] = useState<FormState>(EMPTY_FORM);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [saveError, setSaveError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch(`/api/admin/avatars/${params.avatarId}`)
      .then(async (res) => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to load avatar");
        return res.json();
      })
      .then((data: Avatar) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setForm({
          name: data.name,
          owner: data.owner,
          ownerType: data.ownerType,
          bodyType: data.bodyType,
          imageUrl: data.imageUrl ?? "",
          status: data.status,
        });
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoadError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load avatar");
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [params.avatarId]);

  function update<K extends keyof FormState>(key: K, value: FormState[K]) {
    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: value }));
  }

  async function handleSave() {
    if (!form.name.trim()) {
      setSaveError("Name is required.");
      return;
    }
    if (!form.owner.trim()) {
      setSaveError("Owner is required.");
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    setSaveError(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/avatars/${params.avatarId}`, {
        method: "PATCH",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: form.name,
          owner: form.owner,
          ownerType: form.ownerType,
          bodyType: form.bodyType,
          imageUrl: form.imageUrl.trim() || null,
          status: form.status,
        }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to save avatar");
      router.push("/admin/avatars");
    } catch (err) {
      setSaveError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save avatar");
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <>
        <AdminPageHeader title="Edit avatar" />
        <p className="mt-8 text-sm text-stone-500">Loading…</p>
      </>
    );
  }

  if (loadError) {
    return (
      <>
        <AdminPageHeader title="Edit avatar" />
        <p className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">{loadError}</p>
      </>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <AdminPageHeader title={`Edit ${form.name}`} subtitle="Update this avatar's details." />

      <div className="mt-8 max-w-2xl">
        <AdminCard>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Name</span>
                <input
                  value={form.name}
                  onChange={(e) => update("name", e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. Aura"
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                />
              </label>
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Owner</span>
                <input
                  value={form.owner}
                  onChange={(e) => update("owner", e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. PwC Tunisia"
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                />
              </label>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Owner type</span>
                <select
                  value={form.ownerType}
                  onChange={(e) => update("ownerType", e.target.value as OwnerType)}
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                >
                  {OWNER_TYPES.map((t) => (
                    <option key={t} value={t}>{t}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </label>
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Body type</span>
                <select
                  value={form.bodyType}
                  onChange={(e) => update("bodyType", e.target.value as BodyType)}
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                >
                  {BODY_TYPES.map((t) => (
                    <option key={t} value={t}>{t}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </label>
            </div>

            <label className="block">
              <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Image URL</span>
              <input
                value={form.imageUrl}
                onChange={(e) => update("imageUrl", e.target.value)}
                placeholder="https://…"
                className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
              />
            </label>

            <label className="block sm:w-1/2">
              <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Status</span>
              <select
                value={form.status}
                onChange={(e) => update("status", e.target.value as PublishState)}
                className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
              >
                {STATUSES.map((s) => (
                  <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>
          </div>
        </AdminCard>

        {saveError ? (
          <p className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">{saveError}</p>
        ) : null}

        <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-between">
          <AdminSecondaryButton onClick={() => router.push("/admin/avatars")}>Cancel</AdminSecondaryButton>
          <AdminPrimaryButton onClick={handleSave} disabled={saving}>
            {saving ? "Saving…" : "Save changes"}
          </AdminPrimaryButton>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

===== FILE END: app/admin/avatars/[avatarId]/page.tsx =====

===== FILE START: app/admin/combinations/[id]/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { AdminPageHeader, AdminPrimaryButton, AdminSecondaryButton } from "@/components/admin/AdminPageHeader";
import { AdminCard } from "@/components/admin/AdminCard";
import { WizardStepper } from "@/components/admin/avatar-wizard/WizardStepper";
import { StepAvatar } from "@/components/admin/avatar-wizard/steps/StepAvatar";
import { StepKnowledge } from "@/components/admin/avatar-wizard/steps/StepKnowledge";
import { PersonaFieldsForm, type PersonaFieldsData } from "@/components/admin/avatar-wizard/steps/PersonaFieldsForm";
import type { WizardData } from "@/components/admin/avatar-wizard/wizard-types";
import type { ResolvedCombination } from "@/lib/admin/types";

const EDIT_STEPS = [
  { id: "avatar", label: "Avatar" },
  { id: "persona", label: "Persona" },
  { id: "knowledge", label: "Knowledge" },
] as const;

export default function CombinationEditPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useParams<{ id: string }>();

  const [combo, setCombo] = useState<ResolvedCombination | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [stepIndex, setStepIndex] = useState(0);
  const [avatarDraft, setAvatarDraft] = useState<WizardData["avatar"] | null>(null);
  const [personaDraft, setPersonaDraft] = useState<PersonaFieldsData | null>(null);
  const [knowledgeDraft, setKnowledgeDraft] = useState<WizardData["knowledge"] | null>(null);

  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [saveError, setSaveError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch(`/api/admin/combinations/${params.id}`)
      .then(async (res) => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to load combination");
        return res.json();
      })
      .then((data: ResolvedCombination) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setCombo(data);
        setAvatarDraft({
          imagePreviewUrl: data.avatar.imageUrl,
          name: data.avatar.name,
          owner: data.avatar.owner,
          ownerType: data.avatar.ownerType,
          bodyType: data.avatar.bodyType,
          status: data.avatar.status,
        });
        setPersonaDraft({
          name: data.persona.name,
          type: data.persona.type,
          prompt: data.persona.prompt,
          qnaModel: data.persona.qnaModel,
          creativity: data.persona.creativity,
        });
        setKnowledgeDraft({
          mode: "existing",
          selectedIds: data.knowledgeSources.map((k) => k.id),
          newSource: { name: "", description: "", files: [] },
        });
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoadError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load combination");
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [params.id]);

  const isLastStep = stepIndex === EDIT_STEPS.length - 1;

  function goBack() {
    if (stepIndex === 0) {
      router.push("/admin");
      return;
    }
    setStepIndex((i) => Math.max(i - 1, 0));
  }

  function goNext() {
    if (isLastStep) {
      handleSave();
      return;
    }
    setStepIndex((i) => Math.min(i + 1, EDIT_STEPS.length - 1));
  }

  async function handleSave() {
    if (!combo || !avatarDraft || !personaDraft || !knowledgeDraft) return;
    setSaving(true);
    setSaveError(null);
    try {
      let knowledgeSourceIds = knowledgeDraft.selectedIds;

      if (knowledgeDraft.mode === "new" && knowledgeDraft.newSource.name.trim()) {
        const createRes = await fetch("/api/admin/knowledge", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            name: knowledgeDraft.newSource.name,
            description: knowledgeDraft.newSource.description,
          }),
        });
        if (!createRes.ok) throw new Error((await createRes.json()).error ?? "Failed to create knowledge source");
        const created = await createRes.json();
        knowledgeSourceIds = [...knowledgeDraft.selectedIds, created.id];
      }

      const [avatarRes, personaRes, comboRes] = await Promise.all([
        fetch(`/api/admin/avatars/${combo.avatar.id}`, {
          method: "PATCH",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            name: avatarDraft.name,
            owner: avatarDraft.owner,
            ownerType: avatarDraft.ownerType,
            bodyType: avatarDraft.bodyType,
          }),
        }),
        fetch(`/api/admin/personas/${combo.persona.id}`, {
          method: "PATCH",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            name: personaDraft.name,
            type: personaDraft.type,
            prompt: personaDraft.prompt,
            qnaModel: personaDraft.qnaModel,
            creativity: personaDraft.creativity,
          }),
        }),
        fetch(`/api/admin/combinations/${combo.id}`, {
          method: "PATCH",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ knowledgeSourceIds }),
        }),
      ]);

      if (!avatarRes.ok) throw new Error((await avatarRes.json()).error ?? "Failed to save avatar");
      if (!personaRes.ok) throw new Error((await personaRes.json()).error ?? "Failed to save persona");
      if (!comboRes.ok) throw new Error((await comboRes.json()).error ?? "Failed to save knowledge links");

      router.push("/admin");
    } catch (err) {
      setSaveError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save changes");
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  if (loading) return <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">Loading combination…</p>;
  if (loadError || !combo || !avatarDraft || !personaDraft || !knowledgeDraft) {
    return (
      <p className="rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">
        {loadError ?? "Combination not found"}
      </p>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <AdminPageHeader
        title={`Edit ${combo.avatar.name}`}
        subtitle="Update the avatar, its persona, and its linked knowledge in one place — same flow as creation."
      />

      <div className="mt-8">
        <WizardStepper activeIndex={stepIndex} steps={EDIT_STEPS} />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-8">
        {EDIT_STEPS[stepIndex].id === "avatar" ? (
          <StepAvatar data={avatarDraft} onChange={setAvatarDraft} />
        ) : null}
        {EDIT_STEPS[stepIndex].id === "persona" ? (
          <AdminCard>
            <PersonaFieldsForm data={personaDraft} onChange={setPersonaDraft} />
          </AdminCard>
        ) : null}
        {EDIT_STEPS[stepIndex].id === "knowledge" ? (
          <StepKnowledge data={knowledgeDraft} onChange={setKnowledgeDraft} />
        ) : null}
      </div>

      {saveError ? (
        <p className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">
          {saveError}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <div className="mt-8 flex items-center justify-between">
        <AdminSecondaryButton onClick={goBack}>{stepIndex === 0 ? "Cancel" : "Back"}</AdminSecondaryButton>
        <AdminPrimaryButton onClick={goNext} disabled={saving}>
          {isLastStep ? (saving ? "Saving…" : "Save changes") : "Next"}
        </AdminPrimaryButton>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

===== FILE END: app/admin/combinations/[id]/page.tsx =====

===== FILE START: app/admin/conversations/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { AdminPageHeader } from "@/components/admin/AdminPageHeader";
import { Table, THead, Th, Tr, Td, StatusBadge, BooleanBadge } from "@/components/admin/table/Table";
import { ActionsMenu } from "@/components/admin/table/ActionsMenu";
import { conversationTone } from "@/lib/admin/mock-data";
import type { Conversation, Avatar, Persona } from "@/lib/admin/types";

export default function ConversationCenterPage() {
  const [conversations, setConversations] = useState<Conversation[]>([]);
  const [avatarNames, setAvatarNames] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [personaNames, setPersonaNames] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    Promise.all([
      fetch("/api/admin/conversations").then((res) => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Failed to load conversations");
        return res.json();
      }),
      fetch("/api/admin/avatars").then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : [])),
      fetch("/api/admin/personas").then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : [])),
    ])
      .then(([convData, avatarData, personaData]: [Conversation[], Avatar[], Persona[]]) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setConversations(convData);
        setAvatarNames(Object.fromEntries(avatarData.map((a) => [a.id, a.name])));
        setPersonaNames(Object.fromEntries(personaData.map((p) => [p.id, p.name])));
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (!cancelled) setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load conversations");
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <>
      <AdminPageHeader
        title="Conversation center"
        subtitle="Monitor, review, and export live and past conversations."
      />

      <div className="mt-8">
        {error ? (
          <p className="rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">{error}</p>
        ) : loading ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">Loading conversations…</p>
        ) : conversations.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">No conversations yet.</p>
        ) : (
          <Table>
            <THead>
              <tr>
                <Th>Name</Th>
                <Th>Public</Th>
                <Th>Status</Th>
                <Th>Personal avatar request</Th>
                <Th>Active avatar</Th>
                <Th>Persona name</Th>
                <Th>Conversation type</Th>
                <Th>Language</Th>
                <Th>Creator user</Th>
                <Th className="text-right">Actions</Th>
              </tr>
            </THead>
            <tbody>
              {conversations.map((conv) => (
                <Tr key={conv.id}>
                  <Td className="font-medium text-stone-900">{conv.name}</Td>
                  <Td>
                    <BooleanBadge value={conv.isPublic} />
                  </Td>
                  <Td>
                    <StatusBadge label={conv.status} tone={conversationTone(conv.status)} />
                  </Td>
                  <Td>
                    <BooleanBadge value={conv.personalAvatarRequest} />
                  </Td>
                  <Td>{(conv.activeAvatarId && avatarNames[conv.activeAvatarId]) || "—"}</Td>
                  <Td>{(conv.personaId && personaNames[conv.personaId]) || "—"}</Td>
                  <Td>{conv.conversationType}</Td>
                  <Td>{conv.language}</Td>
                  <Td>{conv.creatorUser}</Td>
                  <Td className="text-right">
                    <ActionsMenu name={conv.name} />
                  </Td>
                </Tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </Table>
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

===== FILE END: app/admin/conversations/page.tsx =====

===== FILE START: app/admin/knowledge/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Plus, FolderPlus } from "lucide-react";
import { AdminPageHeader, AdminPrimaryButton } from "@/components/admin/AdminPageHeader";
import { AdminCard } from "@/components/admin/AdminCard";
import { KnowledgeFolderCard } from "@/components/admin/knowledge/KnowledgeFolderCard";
import { KnowledgeFolderModal } from "@/components/admin/knowledge/KnowledgeFolderModal";
import type { KnowledgeSource } from "@/lib/admin/types";

export default function KnowledgeCenterPage() {
  const [sources, setSources] = useState<KnowledgeSource[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const refetch = useCallback(() => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    return fetch("/api/admin/knowledge")
      .then(async (res) => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to load knowledge sources");
        return res.json();
      })
      .then((data: KnowledgeSource[]) => setSources(data))
      .catch((err) => setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load knowledge sources"))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    refetch();
  }, [refetch]);

  // Look the open source up fresh from `sources` on every render (rather than
  // holding a snapshot from the moment it was clicked) so the popup reflects
  // any refetch — e.g. its file count after an upload — and closes itself
  // cleanly if the source gets deleted while open.
  const openSource = openId ? (sources.find((s) => s.id === openId) ?? null) : null;

  return (
    <>
      <AdminPageHeader
        title="Knowledge center"
        subtitle="Sources powering your avatars' answers, indexed and continuously verified."
        action={
          <Link href="/admin/knowledge/new">
            <AdminPrimaryButton>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Plus className="h-4 w-4" /> Add knowledge
              </span>
            </AdminPrimaryButton>
          </Link>
        }
      />

      <div className="mt-8">
        {error ? (
          <p className="rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">{error}</p>
        ) : loading ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">Loading knowledge sources…</p>
        ) : sources.length === 0 ? (
          <AdminCard className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4 px-10 py-20 text-center">
            <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-accent/10 text-accent">
              <FolderPlus className="h-6 w-6" strokeWidth={1.75} />
            </div>
            <div className="max-w-md">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-stone-900">No knowledge folders yet</h2>
              <p className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-stone-500">
                Add a source and drop in some files — your avatars will pull answers from it once it's indexed.
              </p>
            </div>
            <Link href="/admin/knowledge/new">
              <AdminPrimaryButton>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Plus className="h-4 w-4" /> Add knowledge
                </span>
              </AdminPrimaryButton>
            </Link>
          </AdminCard>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4">
            {sources.map((source) => (
              <KnowledgeFolderCard
                key={source.id}
                source={source}
                onOpen={() => setOpenId(source.id)}
                onChanged={refetch}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <KnowledgeFolderModal source={openSource} onClose={() => setOpenId(null)} onChanged={refetch} />
    </>
  );
}

===== FILE END: app/admin/knowledge/page.tsx =====

===== FILE START: app/admin/knowledge/[knowledgeId]/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { File as FileIcon, Link as LinkIcon, Upload, X } from "lucide-react";
import { AdminPageHeader, AdminPrimaryButton, AdminSecondaryButton } from "@/components/admin/AdminPageHeader";
import { AdminCard } from "@/components/admin/AdminCard";
import type { KnowledgeSource, KnowledgeFile, KnowledgeStatus } from "@/lib/admin/types";

// Replaces what would otherwise be four near-identical pages
// (knowledge/new, knowledge/[id]/edit, and their persona equivalents) down
// to one dynamic route per resource. `knowledgeId` is either a real source
// UUID (edit mode) or the literal "new" (create mode) — matches the existing
// /admin/knowledge/new link, so nothing else needs to change.
const STATUSES: KnowledgeStatus[] = ["Processing", "Indexed", "Error"];

// In create mode nothing can be persisted yet — there's no source row for
// it to belong to — so pending items stay client-side until the source is
// saved. In edit mode the source already exists, so add/remove hit the API
// immediately. Same UI, two different backing stores.
//
// A pending item is either a real File (uploaded) or a URL string (fetched
// server-side on save) — same list, different payload shape and different
// API route each hits once the source exists.
type PendingItem = { id: string; kind: "file"; file: File } | { id: string; kind: "url"; url: string };

function formatBytes(bytes: number) {
  if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`;
  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`;
  return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`;
}

function isValidHttpUrl(value: string) {
  try {
    const url = new URL(value);
    return url.protocol === "http:" || url.protocol === "https:";
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export default function KnowledgeSourceFormPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useParams<{ knowledgeId: string }>();
  const isNew = params.knowledgeId === "new";
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<KnowledgeStatus>("Processing");

  const [pendingItems, setPendingItems] = useState<PendingItem[]>([]); // create mode only
  const [savedFiles, setSavedFiles] = useState<KnowledgeFile[]>([]); // edit mode only
  const [dragActive, setDragActive] = useState(false);

  const [urlInput, setUrlInput] = useState("");
  const [urlError, setUrlError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [addingUrl, setAddingUrl] = useState(false);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(!isNew);
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [saveError, setSaveError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  function loadSavedFiles() {
    fetch(`/api/admin/knowledge/${params.knowledgeId}/files`)
      .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : []))
      .then((rows: KnowledgeFile[]) => setSavedFiles(rows))
      .catch(() => {});
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isNew) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch(`/api/admin/knowledge/${params.knowledgeId}`)
      .then(async (res) => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to load knowledge source");
        return res.json();
      })
      .then((data: KnowledgeSource) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setName(data.name);
        setDescription(data.description);
        setStatus(data.status);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoadError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load knowledge source");
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      });

    loadSavedFiles();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [isNew, params.knowledgeId]);

  function addPendingFiles(fileList: FileList | null) {
    if (!fileList || fileList.length === 0) return;
    const incoming: PendingItem[] = Array.from(fileList).map((f) => ({
      id: `file-${f.name}-${f.size}-${f.lastModified}`,
      kind: "file",
      file: f,
    }));
    setPendingItems((prev) => {
      const existingIds = new Set(prev.map((item) => item.id));
      return [...prev, ...incoming.filter((item) => !existingIds.has(item.id))];
    });
  }

  async function addSavedFiles(fileList: FileList | null) {
    if (!fileList || fileList.length === 0) return;
    for (const file of Array.from(fileList)) {
      try {
        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append("file", file);
        // NOTE: no explicit Content-Type header — the browser sets
        // "multipart/form-data; boundary=..." itself from the FormData body.
        // Setting it manually strips the boundary and breaks req.formData()
        // on the server.
        const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/knowledge/${params.knowledgeId}/files`, {
          method: "POST",
          body: formData,
        });
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? `Failed to add "${file.name}"`);
      } catch (err) {
        alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to add file");
      }
    }
    loadSavedFiles();
  }

  function addPendingUrl() {
    const url = urlInput.trim();
    if (!url) return;
    if (!isValidHttpUrl(url)) {
      setUrlError("Enter a valid http(s) URL.");
      return;
    }
    setUrlError(null);
    setPendingItems((prev) => {
      if (prev.some((item) => item.kind === "url" && item.url === url)) return prev; // no duplicates
      return [...prev, { id: `url-${url}`, kind: "url", url }];
    });
    setUrlInput("");
  }

  async function addSavedUrl() {
    const url = urlInput.trim();
    if (!url) return;
    if (!isValidHttpUrl(url)) {
      setUrlError("Enter a valid http(s) URL.");
      return;
    }
    setUrlError(null);
    setAddingUrl(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/knowledge/${params.knowledgeId}/urls`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ url }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? `Failed to add "${url}"`);
      setUrlInput("");
      loadSavedFiles();
    } catch (err) {
      setUrlError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to add URL");
    } finally {
      setAddingUrl(false);
    }
  }

  async function removeSavedFile(fileId: string) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/knowledge/${params.knowledgeId}/files/${fileId}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to remove file");
      loadSavedFiles();
    } catch (err) {
      alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to remove file");
    }
  }

  function handleFilesPicked(fileList: FileList | null) {
    if (isNew) addPendingFiles(fileList);
    else void addSavedFiles(fileList);
  }

  function handleUrlSubmit() {
    if (isNew) addPendingUrl();
    else void addSavedUrl();
  }

  async function handleSave() {
    if (!name.trim()) {
      setSaveError("Name is required.");
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    setSaveError(null);
    try {
      if (isNew) {
        const sourceRes = await fetch("/api/admin/knowledge", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ name, description, status: "Processing" }),
        });
        if (!sourceRes.ok) throw new Error((await sourceRes.json()).error ?? "Failed to create knowledge source");
        const source = await sourceRes.json();

        for (const item of pendingItems) {
          if (item.kind === "file") {
            const formData = new FormData();
            formData.append("file", item.file);
            const fileRes = await fetch(`/api/admin/knowledge/${source.id}/files`, {
              method: "POST",
              body: formData,
            });
            if (!fileRes.ok) {
              throw new Error((await fileRes.json()).error ?? `Failed to upload file "${item.file.name}"`);
            }
          } else {
            const urlRes = await fetch(`/api/admin/knowledge/${source.id}/urls`, {
              method: "POST",
              headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
              body: JSON.stringify({ url: item.url }),
            });
            if (!urlRes.ok) {
              throw new Error((await urlRes.json()).error ?? `Failed to add URL "${item.url}"`);
            }
          }
        }
      } else {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/knowledge/${params.knowledgeId}`, {
          method: "PATCH",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ name, description, status }),
        });
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to save knowledge source");
      }
      router.push("/admin/knowledge");
    } catch (err) {
      setSaveError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : `Failed to ${isNew ? "create" : "save"} knowledge source`);
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <>
        <AdminPageHeader title="Edit knowledge source" />
        <p className="mt-8 text-sm text-stone-500">Loading…</p>
      </>
    );
  }

  if (loadError) {
    return (
      <>
        <AdminPageHeader title="Edit knowledge source" />
        <p className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">{loadError}</p>
      </>
    );
  }

  const fileCount = isNew ? pendingItems.length : savedFiles.length;

  return (
    <>
      <AdminPageHeader
        title={isNew ? "Add knowledge source" : `Edit ${name}`}
        subtitle={
          isNew ? "Register a new source your avatars can pull answers from." : "Update this source's details and files."
        }
      />

      <div className="mt-8 max-w-2xl">
        <AdminCard>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Source name</span>
                <input
                  value={name}
                  onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. Onboarding guides"
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                />
              </label>
              {isNew ? (
                <label className="block">
                  <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Item count</span>
                  <div className="flex h-[46px] items-center rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 bg-stone-50 px-4 text-sm text-stone-500">
                    {fileCount} item{fileCount === 1 ? "" : "s"}
                  </div>
                </label>
              ) : (
                <label className="block">
                  <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Status</span>
                  <select
                    value={status}
                    onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value as KnowledgeStatus)}
                    className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                  >
                    {STATUSES.map((s) => (
                      <option key={s} value={s}>
                        {s}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </label>
              )}
            </div>

            <label className="block">
              <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Description</span>
              <textarea
                value={description}
                onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
                rows={3}
                placeholder="What does this source cover?"
                className="w-full resize-none rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
              />
            </label>

            <div>
              <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Files & links ({fileCount})</span>

              {isNew
                ? pendingItems.length > 0 && (
                    <ul className="mb-3 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                      {pendingItems.map((item) => (
                        <li
                          key={item.id}
                          className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-stone-100 bg-stone-50/60 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                        >
                          <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2 text-stone-700">
                            {item.kind === "file" ? (
                              <FileIcon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-stone-400" />
                            ) : (
                              <LinkIcon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-stone-400" />
                            )}
                            <span className="truncate">{item.kind === "file" ? item.file.name : item.url}</span>
                          </span>
                          <span className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-3">
                            {item.kind === "file" ? (
                              <span className="text-xs text-stone-400">{formatBytes(item.file.size)}</span>
                            ) : (
                              <span className="text-xs text-stone-400">Link</span>
                            )}
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => setPendingItems((prev) => prev.filter((i) => i.id !== item.id))}
                              aria-label={`Remove ${item.kind === "file" ? item.file.name : item.url}`}
                              className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-stone-400 hover:bg-stone-200 hover:text-stone-700"
                            >
                              <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                            </button>
                          </span>
                        </li>
                      ))}
                    </ul>
                  )
                : savedFiles.length > 0 && (
                    <ul className="mb-3 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                      {savedFiles.map((file) => (
                        <li
                          key={file.id}
                          className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-stone-100 bg-stone-50/60 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                        >
                          <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2 text-stone-700">
                            {file.sourceUrl ? (
                              <LinkIcon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-stone-400" />
                            ) : (
                              <FileIcon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-stone-400" />
                            )}
                            {file.sourceUrl ? (
                              <a
                                href={file.sourceUrl}
                                target="_blank"
                                rel="noreferrer"
                                className="truncate text-accent hover:underline"
                                title={file.sourceUrl}
                              >
                                {file.name}
                              </a>
                            ) : (
                              <span className="truncate">{file.name}</span>
                            )}
                          </span>
                          <span className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-3">
                            <span className="text-xs text-stone-400">{formatBytes(file.sizeBytes)}</span>
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => removeSavedFile(file.id)}
                              aria-label={`Remove ${file.name}`}
                              className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-stone-400 hover:bg-stone-200 hover:text-stone-700"
                            >
                              <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                            </button>
                          </span>
                        </li>
                      ))}
                    </ul>
                  )}

              <div
                onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                onDragOver={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  setDragActive(true);
                }}
                onDragLeave={() => setDragActive(false)}
                onDrop={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  setDragActive(false);
                  handleFilesPicked(e.dataTransfer.files);
                }}
                className={`flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed px-6 py-8 text-center transition-colors ${
                  dragActive ? "border-accent bg-accent/5" : "border-stone-300 hover:border-stone-400"
                }`}
              >
                <Upload className="h-6 w-6 text-stone-400" />
                <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">
                  <span className="font-medium text-accent">Click to upload</span> or drag and drop
                </p>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-400">PDF, DOCX, TXT, CSV — up to 20 files</p>
                <input
                  ref={fileInputRef}
                  type="file"
                  multiple
                  accept=".pdf,.doc,.docx,.txt,.csv,.md"
                  className="hidden"
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    handleFilesPicked(e.target.files);
                    e.target.value = "";
                  }}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-stone-500">Or add a web page by URL</span>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <input
                    value={urlInput}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      setUrlInput(e.target.value);
                      if (urlError) setUrlError(null);
                    }}
                    onKeyDown={(e) => {
                      if (e.key === "Enter") {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        handleUrlSubmit();
                      }
                    }}
                    placeholder="https://example.com/policy-page"
                    className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                  />
                  <AdminSecondaryButton onClick={handleUrlSubmit} disabled={addingUrl}>
                    {addingUrl ? "Adding…" : "Add link"}
                  </AdminSecondaryButton>
                </div>
                {urlError ? <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs text-red-600">{urlError}</p> : null}
                <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs text-stone-400">
                  We fetch the page and extract its main article content — not the full page markup.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </AdminCard>

        {saveError ? (
          <p className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">{saveError}</p>
        ) : null}

        <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-between">
          <AdminSecondaryButton onClick={() => router.push("/admin/knowledge")}>Cancel</AdminSecondaryButton>
          <AdminPrimaryButton onClick={handleSave} disabled={saving}>
            {saving ? (isNew ? "Creating…" : "Saving…") : isNew ? "Create source" : "Save changes"}
          </AdminPrimaryButton>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

===== FILE START: app/admin/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Plus } from "lucide-react";

import { AdminPageHeader, AdminPrimaryButton } from "@/components/admin/AdminPageHeader";
import { Table, THead, Th, Tr, Td, StatusBadge } from "@/components/admin/table/Table";
import { ActionsMenu } from "@/components/admin/table/ActionsMenu";
import { publishTone } from "@/lib/admin/mock-data";
import type { ResolvedCombination } from "@/lib/admin/types";

export default function AdminDashboardPage() {
  const [combinations, setCombinations] = useState<ResolvedCombination[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const fetchCombinations = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/admin/combinations");
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to load combinations");
      }
      setCombinations(await res.json());
      setError(null);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load combinations");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchCombinations();
  }, [fetchCombinations]);


  return (
    <>
      <AdminPageHeader
        title="Agents"
        subtitle="Active avatars, conversation volume, and knowledge health at a glance."
        action={
          <Link href="/admin/avatars/new">
            <AdminPrimaryButton>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
                Create agent
              </span>
            </AdminPrimaryButton>
          </Link>
        }
      />

      <div className="mt-8">
        <h2 className="mb-3 text-sm font-semibold text-stone-500">
          Agents available
        </h2>

        {error ? (
          <p className="rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">
            {error}
          </p>
        ) : loading ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">Loading agents...</p>
        ) : combinations.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">
            No combinations yet — use "Create agent" to pair an avatar with a
            persona and knowledge.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <Table>
            <THead>
              <tr>
                <Th>Avatar</Th>
                <Th>Persona</Th>
                <Th>Knowledge sources</Th>
                <Th>Status</Th>
                <Th className="text-right">Actions</Th>
              </tr>
            </THead>

            <tbody>
              {combinations.map((combo) => (
                <Tr key={combo.id}>
                  <Td className="font-medium text-stone-900">
                    {combo.avatar.name}
                  </Td>

                  <Td>
                    {combo.persona.name}
                  </Td>

                  <Td className="text-stone-500">
                    {combo.knowledgeSources.length > 0
                      ? combo.knowledgeSources.map((k) => k.name).join(", ")
                      : "—"}
                  </Td>

                  <Td>
                    <StatusBadge
                      label={combo.avatar.status}
                      tone={publishTone(combo.avatar.status)}
                    />
                  </Td>

                  <Td className="text-right">
                    <ActionsMenu variant="combination" combination={combo} onChanged={fetchCombinations} />
                  </Td>
                </Tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </Table>
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

===== FILE START: app/admin/personas/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { AdminPageHeader, AdminPrimaryButton } from "@/components/admin/AdminPageHeader";
import { Table, THead, Th, Tr, Td, StatusBadge } from "@/components/admin/table/Table";
import { ActionsMenu } from "@/components/admin/table/ActionsMenu";
import { publishTone } from "@/lib/admin/mock-data";
import type { Persona } from "@/lib/admin/types";

export default function PersonasIndexPage() {
  const [personas, setPersonas] = useState<Persona[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const refetch = useCallback(() => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    return fetch("/api/admin/personas")
      .then(async (res) => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to load personas");
        return res.json();
      })
      .then((data: Persona[]) => setPersonas(data))
      .catch((err) => setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load personas"))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    refetch();
  }, [refetch]);

  return (
    <>
      <AdminPageHeader
        title="Personas"
        subtitle="How each avatar thinks, speaks, and represents PwC."
        action={
          <Link href="/admin/personas/new">
            <AdminPrimaryButton>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Plus className="h-4 w-4" /> Add persona
              </span>
            </AdminPrimaryButton>
          </Link>
        }
      />

      <div className="mt-8">
        {error ? (
          <p className="rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">{error}</p>
        ) : loading ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">Loading personas…</p>
        ) : personas.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">No personas yet.</p>
        ) : (
          <Table>
            <THead>
              <tr>
                <Th>Type</Th>
                <Th>Name</Th>
                <Th className="min-w-[280px]">Prompt</Th>
                <Th>Q&amp;A model</Th>
                <Th>Creativity</Th>
                <Th>Status</Th>
                <Th>Version</Th>
                <Th className="text-right">Actions</Th>
              </tr>
            </THead>
            <tbody>
              {personas.map((persona) => (
                <Tr key={persona.id}>
                  <Td>{persona.type}</Td>
                  <Td className="font-medium text-stone-900">{persona.name}</Td>
                  <Td className="max-w-xs">
                    <span className="block truncate text-stone-500" title={persona.prompt}>
                      {persona.prompt}
                    </span>
                  </Td>
                  <Td>{persona.qnaModel}</Td>
                  <Td>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <div className="h-1.5 w-16 rounded-full bg-stone-100">
                        <div className="h-1.5 rounded-full bg-accent" style={{ width: `${persona.creativity}%` }} />
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-xs text-stone-500">{persona.creativity}%</span>
                    </div>
                  </Td>
                  <Td>
                    <StatusBadge label={persona.status} tone={publishTone(persona.status)} />
                  </Td>
                  <Td>{persona.version}</Td>
                  <Td className="text-right">
                    <ActionsMenu variant="persona" persona={persona} onChanged={refetch} />
                  </Td>
                </Tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </Table>
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

===== FILE END: app/admin/personas/page.tsx =====

===== FILE START: app/admin/personas/[personaId]/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { AdminPageHeader, AdminPrimaryButton, AdminSecondaryButton } from "@/components/admin/AdminPageHeader";
import { AdminCard } from "@/components/admin/AdminCard";
import { QNA_MODELS } from "@/lib/admin/mock-data";
import type { Persona, QnaModel, PublishState } from "@/lib/admin/types";

// This single route replaces the previous app/admin/personas/new/page.tsx —
// delete that file/folder once this is in place. `personaId` is either a
// real persona UUID (edit mode) or the literal string "new" (create mode),
// which is what /admin/personas/new already links to, so no other link
// needs to change.
const STATUSES: PublishState[] = ["Private", "Public", "Archived"];

type FormState = {
  name: string;
  type: string;
  roleTitle: string;
  department: string;
  prompt: string;
  qnaModel: QnaModel;
  creativity: number;
  status: PublishState;
  version: string;
};

const EMPTY_FORM: FormState = {
  name: "",
  type: "",
  roleTitle: "",
  department: "",
  prompt: "",
  qnaModel: "Claude Sonnet",
  creativity: 35,
  status: "Private",
  version: "v1.0",
};

export default function PersonaFormPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useParams<{ personaId: string }>();
  const isNew = params.personaId === "new";

  const [form, setForm] = useState<FormState>(EMPTY_FORM);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(!isNew);
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [saveError, setSaveError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isNew) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch(`/api/admin/personas/${params.personaId}`)
      .then(async (res) => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to load persona");
        return res.json();
      })
      .then((data: Persona) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setForm({
          name: data.name,
          type: data.type,
          roleTitle: data.roleTitle,
          department: data.department,
          prompt: data.prompt,
          qnaModel: data.qnaModel,
          creativity: data.creativity,
          status: data.status,
          version: data.version,
        });
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoadError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load persona");
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [isNew, params.personaId]);

  function update<K extends keyof FormState>(key: K, value: FormState[K]) {
    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: value }));
  }

  async function handleSave() {
    if (!form.name.trim()) {
      setSaveError("Name is required.");
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    setSaveError(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(isNew ? "/api/admin/personas" : `/api/admin/personas/${params.personaId}`, {
        method: isNew ? "POST" : "PATCH",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(isNew ? { ...form, status: "Draft" } : form),
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? `Failed to ${isNew ? "create" : "save"} persona`);
      router.push("/admin/personas");
    } catch (err) {
      setSaveError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : `Failed to ${isNew ? "create" : "save"} persona`);
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <>
        <AdminPageHeader title="Edit persona" />
        <p className="mt-8 text-sm text-stone-500">Loading…</p>
      </>
    );
  }

  if (loadError) {
    return (
      <>
        <AdminPageHeader title="Edit persona" />
        <p className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">{loadError}</p>
      </>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <AdminPageHeader
        title={isNew ? "Add persona" : `Edit ${form.name}`}
        subtitle={
          isNew
            ? "Create a standalone persona — link it to an avatar later from the avatar wizard."
            : "Update this persona's details."
        }
      />

      <div className="mt-8 max-w-2xl">
        <AdminCard>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Name</span>
                <input
                  value={form.name}
                  onChange={(e) => update("name", e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. Sarra"
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                />
              </label>
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Type</span>
                <input
                  value={form.type}
                  onChange={(e) => update("type", e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. HR assistant"
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                />
              </label>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Role title</span>
                <input
                  value={form.roleTitle}
                  onChange={(e) => update("roleTitle", e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. Senior HR Advisor"
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                />
              </label>
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Department</span>
                <input
                  value={form.department}
                  onChange={(e) => update("department", e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. People & Culture"
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                />
              </label>
            </div>

            <label className="block">
              <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Prompt</span>
              <textarea
                value={form.prompt}
                onChange={(e) => update("prompt", e.target.value)}
                rows={5}
                placeholder="You are ... Answer employee questions about ... with a ... tone."
                className="w-full resize-none rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
              />
            </label>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Q&amp;A model</span>
                <select
                  value={form.qnaModel}
                  onChange={(e) => update("qnaModel", e.target.value as QnaModel)}
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                >
                  {QNA_MODELS.map((model) => (
                    <option key={model} value={model}>
                      {model}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </label>
              <div>
                <span className="mb-1.5 flex items-center justify-between text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">
                  Creativity <span className="text-stone-400">{form.creativity}%</span>
                </span>
                <input
                  type="range"
                  min={0}
                  max={100}
                  value={form.creativity}
                  onChange={(e) => update("creativity", Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="mt-3.5 w-full accent-accent"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Status/version only make sense once a persona actually exists —
                a brand-new one is always created as Draft v1.0 server-side. */}
            {!isNew ? (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <label className="block">
                  <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Status</span>
                  <select
                    value={form.status}
                    onChange={(e) => update("status", e.target.value as PublishState)}
                    className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                  >
                    {STATUSES.map((s) => (
                      <option key={s} value={s}>
                        {s}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </label>
                <label className="block">
                  <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Version</span>
                  <input
                    value={form.version}
                    onChange={(e) => update("version", e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                  />
                </label>
              </div>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </AdminCard>

        {saveError ? (
          <p className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">{saveError}</p>
        ) : null}

        <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-between">
          <AdminSecondaryButton onClick={() => router.push("/admin/personas")}>Cancel</AdminSecondaryButton>
          <AdminPrimaryButton onClick={handleSave} disabled={saving}>
            {saving ? (isNew ? "Creating…" : "Saving…") : isNew ? "Create persona" : "Save changes"}
          </AdminPrimaryButton>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}
