===== FILE START: app/admin/testing/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { AdminPageHeader } from "@/components/admin/AdminPageHeader";
import { Table, THead, Th, Tr, Td, StatusBadge, formatTunisTime } from "@/components/admin/table/Table";
import { ActionsMenu } from "@/components/admin/table/ActionsMenu";
import { testTone } from "@/lib/admin/mock-data";
import type { TestCase, Conversation } from "@/lib/admin/types";

export default function TestingLabPage() {
  const [tests, setTests] = useState<TestCase[]>([]);
  const [conversationNames, setConversationNames] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    Promise.all([
      fetch("/api/admin/tests").then((res) => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Failed to load tests");
        return res.json();
      }),
      fetch("/api/admin/conversations").then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : [])),
    ])
      .then(([testData, convData]: [TestCase[], Conversation[]]) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setTests(testData);
        setConversationNames(Object.fromEntries(convData.map((c) => [c.id, c.name])));
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (!cancelled) setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load tests");
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <>
      <AdminPageHeader
        title="Testing lab"
        subtitle="Simulate conversations, compare versions, and catch hallucinations before launch."
      />

      <div className="mt-8">
        {error ? (
          <p className="rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">{error}</p>
        ) : loading ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">Loading tests…</p>
        ) : tests.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">No tests yet.</p>
        ) : (
          <Table>
            <THead>
              <tr>
                <Th>Name</Th>
                <Th>Conversation</Th>
                <Th>Created (GMT+1)</Th>
                <Th>Type</Th>
                <Th>Total questions</Th>
                <Th>Status</Th>
                <Th className="text-right">Actions</Th>
              </tr>
            </THead>
            <tbody>
              {tests.map((test) => (
                <Tr key={test.id}>
                  <Td className="font-medium text-stone-900">{test.name}</Td>
                  <Td className="text-stone-500">
                    {(test.conversationId && conversationNames[test.conversationId]) || "—"}
                  </Td>
                  <Td>{formatTunisTime(test.createdAt)}</Td>
                  <Td>{test.type}</Td>
                  <Td>{test.totalQuestions}</Td>
                  <Td>
                    <StatusBadge label={test.status} tone={testTone(test.status)} />
                  </Td>
                  <Td className="text-right">
                    <ActionsMenu name={test.name} />
                  </Td>
                </Tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </Table>
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

===== FILE END: app/admin/testing/page.tsx =====

===== FILE START: app/api/admin/avatars/publish/route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { badRequest, serverError, asRecord } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const cleanup: Array<() => Promise<void>> = [];

  try {
    const body = asRecord(await req.json());
    const avatarInput = asRecord(body.avatar);
    const personaInput = asRecord(body.persona);
    const knowledgeInput = asRecord(body.knowledge);

    const avatarName = avatarInput.name;
    if (typeof avatarName !== "string" || !avatarName.trim()) {
      return badRequest("`avatar.name` is required");
    }

    const { data: avatarRow, error: avatarError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("avatars")
      .insert({
        name: avatarName,
        owner: typeof avatarInput.owner === "string" ? avatarInput.owner : "",
        owner_type: avatarInput.ownerType === "Individual" ? "Individual" : "Organisation",
        body_type: ["Full body", "Half body", "Head only"].includes(avatarInput.bodyType as string)
          ? avatarInput.bodyType
          : "Half body",
        image_url: typeof avatarInput.imagePreviewUrl === "string" ? avatarInput.imagePreviewUrl : null,
        status: typeof avatarInput.status === "string" ? avatarInput.status : "Private",
      })
      .select("id")
      .single();

    if (avatarError) throw avatarError;
    const avatarId = avatarRow.id as string;
    cleanup.push(async () => {
      await supabaseAdmin.from("avatars").delete().eq("id", avatarId);
    });

    let personaId: string;

    if (personaInput.mode === "existing") {
      if (typeof personaInput.existingPersonaId !== "string" || !personaInput.existingPersonaId) {
        return badRequest("`persona.existingPersonaId` is required when persona.mode is 'existing'");
      }
      personaId = personaInput.existingPersonaId;

      const { error: linkError } = await supabaseAdmin
        .from("personas")
        .update({ avatar_id: avatarId })
        .eq("id", personaId);
      if (linkError) throw linkError;
    } else {
      const { data: personaRow, error: personaError } = await supabaseAdmin
        .from("personas")
        .insert({
          avatar_id: avatarId,
          type: typeof personaInput.type === "string" ? personaInput.type : "",
          name: typeof personaInput.name === "string" ? personaInput.name : avatarName,
          prompt: typeof personaInput.prompt === "string" ? personaInput.prompt : "",
          qna_model: typeof personaInput.qnaModel === "string" ? personaInput.qnaModel : "Claude Sonnet",
          creativity: typeof personaInput.creativity === "number" ? personaInput.creativity : 35,
          status: "Private",
          // Drives TTS voice selection - see app/api/conversations/route.ts
          // and supabase/migrations/0004_persona_gender.sql.
          gender: personaInput.gender === "male" ? "male" : "female",
        })
        .select("id")
        .single();

      if (personaError) throw personaError;
      personaId = personaRow.id as string;
      cleanup.push(async () => {
        await supabaseAdmin.from("personas").delete().eq("id", personaId);
      });
    }

    let knowledgeSourceIds: string[] = [];

    if (knowledgeInput.mode === "existing") {
      knowledgeSourceIds = Array.isArray(knowledgeInput.selectedIds) ? (knowledgeInput.selectedIds as string[]) : [];
    } else {
      const newSource = asRecord(knowledgeInput.newSource);
      const files = Array.isArray(newSource.files) ? (newSource.files as Record<string, unknown>[]) : [];

      const { data: sourceRow, error: sourceError } = await supabaseAdmin
        .from("knowledge_sources")
        .insert({
          name: typeof newSource.name === "string" && newSource.name ? newSource.name : `${avatarName} knowledge`,
          description: typeof newSource.description === "string" ? newSource.description : "",
          status: "Processing",
        })
        .select("id")
        .single();

      if (sourceError) throw sourceError;
      const sourceId = sourceRow.id as string;
      cleanup.push(async () => {
        await supabaseAdmin.from("knowledge_sources").delete().eq("id", sourceId);
      });
      knowledgeSourceIds = [sourceId];

      if (files.length > 0) {
        const { error: filesError } = await supabaseAdmin.from("knowledge_files").insert(
          files.map((file) => ({
            knowledge_source_id: sourceId,
            name: typeof file.name === "string" ? file.name : "untitled",
            size_bytes: 0,
          }))
        );
        if (filesError) throw filesError;
      }
    }

    const { data: comboRow, error: comboError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("avatar_combinations")
      .insert({ avatar_id: avatarId, persona_id: personaId, knowledge_source_ids: knowledgeSourceIds })
      .select("id")
      .single();

    if (comboError) throw comboError;

    return NextResponse.json(
      { avatarId, personaId, knowledgeSourceIds, combinationId: comboRow.id },
      { status: 201 }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    for (const undo of cleanup.reverse()) {
      try {
        await undo();
      } catch (cleanupError) {
        console.error("Cleanup step failed during publish rollback:", cleanupError);
      }
    }
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { toAvatarDTO, type AvatarRow } from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { badRequest, serverError, asRecord } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin.from("avatars").select("*").order("created_at", { ascending: false });
    if (error) throw error;
    return NextResponse.json((data as AvatarRow[]).map(toAvatarDTO));
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = asRecord(await req.json());

    const name = body.name;
    const owner = body.owner;
    const ownerType = body.ownerType;
    const bodyType = body.bodyType;

    if (typeof name !== "string" || !name.trim()) return badRequest("`name` is required");
    if (typeof owner !== "string" || !owner.trim()) return badRequest("`owner` is required");
    if (ownerType !== "Organisation" && ownerType !== "Individual") {
      return badRequest("`ownerType` must be 'Organisation' or 'Individual'");
    }
    if (bodyType !== "Full body" && bodyType !== "Half body" && bodyType !== "Head only") {
      return badRequest("`bodyType` must be 'Full body', 'Half body', or 'Head only'");
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("avatars")
      .insert({
        name,
        owner,
        owner_type: ownerType,
        body_type: bodyType,
        image_url: typeof body.imageUrl === "string" ? body.imageUrl : null,
        // Fixed default: "Draft" isn't a valid PublishState, so new rows
        // rendered with a red/danger badge instead of neutral.
        status: typeof body.status === "string" ? body.status : "Private",
      })
      .select("*")
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;

    return NextResponse.json(toAvatarDTO(data as AvatarRow), { status: 201 });
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

===== FILE START: app/api/admin/avatars/[id]/route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { toAvatarDTO, type AvatarRow } from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { asRecord, notFound, serverError } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";

type Params = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

export async function GET(_req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin.from("avatars").select("*").eq("id", id).maybeSingle();
    if (error) throw error;
    if (!data) return notFound(`Avatar ${id} not found`);
    return NextResponse.json(toAvatarDTO(data as AvatarRow));
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

export async function PATCH(req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const body = asRecord(await req.json());

    const patch: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    if (typeof body.name === "string") patch.name = body.name;
    if (typeof body.owner === "string") patch.owner = body.owner;
    if (typeof body.imageUrl === "string" || body.imageUrl === null) patch.image_url = body.imageUrl;
    if (body.ownerType === "Organisation" || body.ownerType === "Individual") patch.owner_type = body.ownerType;
    if (["Full body", "Half body", "Head only"].includes(body.bodyType as string)) {
      patch.body_type = body.bodyType;
    }
    if (["Private", "Public", "Archived"].includes(body.status as string)) {
      patch.status = body.status;
    }

    if (Object.keys(patch).length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "No updatable fields provided" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("avatars")
      .update(patch)
      .eq("id", id)
      .select("*")
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error) throw error;
    if (!data) return notFound(`Avatar ${id} not found`);

    return NextResponse.json(toAvatarDTO(data as AvatarRow));
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

export async function DELETE(_req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const { error, count } = await supabaseAdmin.from("avatars").delete({ count: "exact" }).eq("id", id);
    if (error) throw error;
    if (!count) return notFound(`Avatar ${id} not found`);
    return NextResponse.json({ deleted: true });
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

===== FILE START: app/api/admin/combinations/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import {
  toAvatarDTO,
  toKnowledgeSourceDTOFromJoin,
  toPersonaDTO,
  KNOWLEDGE_SOURCE_WITH_COUNT_SELECT,
  type AvatarCombinationRow,
  type AvatarRow,
  type KnowledgeSourceRowWithCount,
  type PersonaRow,
  type ResolvedCombination,
} from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { serverError } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";

// GET /api/admin/combinations
//
// Admin-facing sibling of /api/experience/combinations. Same shape
// (ResolvedCombination[]) and same join logic, but deliberately WITHOUT the
// "avatar AND persona must both be Published" filter that route applies —
// the dashboard needs to show Draft/Archived combinations too, since seeing
// what you're still working on is the point of an overview page. Keep that
// filter only in the /experience route; employees should never see
// unpublished pairs.
export async function GET() {
  try {
    const { data: combos, error: comboError } = await supabaseAdmin.from("avatar_combinations").select("*");
    if (comboError) throw comboError;

    const rows = combos as AvatarCombinationRow[];
    if (rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json([]);

    const avatarIds = [...new Set(rows.map((c) => c.avatar_id))];
    const personaIds = [...new Set(rows.map((c) => c.persona_id))];
    const knowledgeIds = [...new Set(rows.flatMap((c) => c.knowledge_source_ids))];

    const [avatarsRes, personasRes, knowledgeRes] = await Promise.all([
      supabaseAdmin.from("avatars").select("*").in("id", avatarIds),
      supabaseAdmin.from("personas").select("*").in("id", personaIds),
      knowledgeIds.length > 0
        ? supabaseAdmin.from("knowledge_sources").select(KNOWLEDGE_SOURCE_WITH_COUNT_SELECT).in("id", knowledgeIds)
        : Promise.resolve({ data: [], error: null }),
    ]);

    if (avatarsRes.error) throw avatarsRes.error;
    if (personasRes.error) throw personasRes.error;
    if (knowledgeRes.error) throw knowledgeRes.error;

    const avatarsById = new Map((avatarsRes.data as AvatarRow[]).map((a) => [a.id, a]));
    const personasById = new Map((personasRes.data as PersonaRow[]).map((p) => [p.id, p]));
    const knowledgeById = new Map(
      (knowledgeRes.data as KnowledgeSourceRowWithCount[]).map((k) => [k.id, toKnowledgeSourceDTOFromJoin(k)])
    );

    const resolved: ResolvedCombination[] = [];
    for (const combo of rows) {
      const avatar = avatarsById.get(combo.avatar_id);
      const persona = personasById.get(combo.persona_id);
      if (!avatar || !persona) continue; // orphaned row — shouldn't happen given the FKs, skip defensively

      resolved.push({
        id: combo.id,
        avatar: toAvatarDTO(avatar),
        persona: toPersonaDTO(persona),
        knowledgeSources: combo.knowledge_source_ids
          .map((id) => knowledgeById.get(id))
          .filter((k): k is NonNullable<typeof k> => Boolean(k)),
      });
    }

    return NextResponse.json(resolved);
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

===== FILE END: app/api/admin/combinations/route.ts =====

===== FILE START: app/api/admin/combinations/[id]/route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import {
  toAvatarDTO,
  toKnowledgeSourceDTOFromJoin,
  toPersonaDTO,
  KNOWLEDGE_SOURCE_WITH_COUNT_SELECT,
  type AvatarCombinationRow,
  type AvatarRow,
  type KnowledgeSourceRowWithCount,
  type PersonaRow,
  type ResolvedCombination,
} from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { asRecord, notFound, serverError } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";

// Next.js 15+ dynamic params are async
type Params = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

async function resolveCombination(id: string): Promise<ResolvedCombination | null> {
  const { data: combo, error: comboError } = await supabaseAdmin
    .from("avatar_combinations")
    .select("*")
    .eq("id", id)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (comboError) throw comboError;
  if (!combo) return null;

  const row = combo as AvatarCombinationRow;

  const [avatarRes, personaRes, knowledgeRes] = await Promise.all([
    supabaseAdmin.from("avatars").select("*").eq("id", row.avatar_id).maybeSingle(),

    supabaseAdmin.from("personas").select("*").eq("id", row.persona_id).maybeSingle(),

    row.knowledge_source_ids.length > 0
      ? supabaseAdmin
          .from("knowledge_sources")
          .select(KNOWLEDGE_SOURCE_WITH_COUNT_SELECT)
          .in("id", row.knowledge_source_ids)
      : Promise.resolve({ data: [], error: null }),
  ]);

  if (avatarRes.error) throw avatarRes.error;
  if (personaRes.error) throw personaRes.error;
  if (knowledgeRes.error) throw knowledgeRes.error;

  if (!avatarRes.data || !personaRes.data) return null;

  const knowledgeSources = (knowledgeRes.data as KnowledgeSourceRowWithCount[]).map(
    (k) => toKnowledgeSourceDTOFromJoin(k)
  );

  return {
    id: row.id,
    avatar: toAvatarDTO(avatarRes.data as AvatarRow),
    persona: toPersonaDTO(personaRes.data as PersonaRow),
    knowledgeSources,
  };
}


// GET /api/admin/combinations/:id
export async function GET(_req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;

    const combination = await resolveCombination(id);

    if (!combination) {
      return notFound(`Combination ${id} not found`);
    }

    return NextResponse.json(combination);
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}


// PATCH /api/admin/combinations/:id
export async function PATCH(req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;

    const body = asRecord(await req.json());

    const patch: Record<string, unknown> = {};

    if (typeof body.avatarId === "string") {
      patch.avatar_id = body.avatarId;
    }

    if (typeof body.personaId === "string") {
      patch.persona_id = body.personaId;
    }

    if (Array.isArray(body.knowledgeSourceIds)) {
      patch.knowledge_source_ids = body.knowledgeSourceIds.filter(
        (value): value is string => typeof value === "string"
      );
    }


    if (Object.keys(patch).length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "No updatable fields provided" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }


    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("avatar_combinations")
      .update(patch)
      .eq("id", id)
      .select("*")
      .maybeSingle();


    if (error) throw error;

    if (!data) {
      return notFound(`Combination ${id} not found`);
    }


    const resolved = await resolveCombination(id);

    if (!resolved) {
      return notFound(`Combination ${id} not found after update`);
    }

    return NextResponse.json(resolved);

  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}


// DELETE /api/admin/combinations/:id
export async function DELETE(_req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;

    const { error, count } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("avatar_combinations")
      .delete({ count: "exact" })
      .eq("id", id);


    if (error) throw error;

    if (!count) {
      return notFound(`Combination ${id} not found`);
    }


    return NextResponse.json({ deleted: true });

  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import {
  toKnowledgeSourceDTO,
  toKnowledgeSourceDTOFromJoin,
  KNOWLEDGE_SOURCE_WITH_COUNT_SELECT,
  type KnowledgeSourceRowWithCount,
  type KnowledgeSourceRow,
} from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { badRequest, serverError, asRecord } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";

// GET /api/admin/knowledge — list every knowledge source with its file count
// (fileCount is derived via a count aggregate, never stored directly).
export async function GET() {
  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("knowledge_sources")
      .select(KNOWLEDGE_SOURCE_WITH_COUNT_SELECT)
      .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

    if (error) throw error;

    const rows = data as KnowledgeSourceRowWithCount[];
    return NextResponse.json(rows.map(toKnowledgeSourceDTOFromJoin));
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

// POST /api/admin/knowledge — create a new knowledge source's metadata.
// Actual files are registered separately via
// POST /api/admin/knowledge/:id/files (see that route for why upload is
// split from source creation).
export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = asRecord(await req.json());

    const name = body.name;
    if (typeof name !== "string" || !name.trim()) return badRequest("`name` is required");

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("knowledge_sources")
      .insert({
        name,
        description: typeof body.description === "string" ? body.description : "",
        status: typeof body.status === "string" ? body.status : "Processing",
      })
      .select("*")
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;

    // Brand new — no files can exist for it yet, so fileCount is trivially 0.
    return NextResponse.json(toKnowledgeSourceDTO(data as KnowledgeSourceRow, 0), { status: 201 });
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

===== FILE START: app/api/admin/knowledge/[id]/files/route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { toKnowledgeFileDTO, type KnowledgeFileRow } from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { badRequest, notFound, serverError } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";
import { ingestKnowledgeFile } from "@/lib/ingestion/pipeline";

type Params = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

const STORAGE_BUCKET = "knowledge";

// GET /api/admin/knowledge/:id/files — list files registered under a source.
export async function GET(_req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("knowledge_files")
      .select("*")
      .eq("knowledge_source_id", id)
      .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

    if (error) throw error;

    return NextResponse.json((data as KnowledgeFileRow[]).map(toKnowledgeFileDTO));
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

// POST /api/admin/knowledge/:id/files — multipart/form-data upload.
// Uploads the file's bytes to Supabase Storage, registers its metadata row,
// then runs the RAG ingestion pipeline for it (extract → chunk → embed →
// store in knowledge_chunks — see lib/ingestion/pipeline.ts). Ingestion runs
// before responding so the client gets the source's final status
// ("Indexed"/"Error") in the same round trip as the upload.
export async function POST(req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;

    const form = await req.formData();
    const file = form.get("file");
    if (!(file instanceof File)) return badRequest("`file` is required (multipart/form-data)");

    // Confirm the parent source exists first for a clean 404 instead of an
    // FK violation.
    const { data: source, error: sourceError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("knowledge_sources")
      .select("id")
      .eq("id", id)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (sourceError) throw sourceError;
    if (!source) return notFound(`Knowledge source ${id} not found`);

    const storagePath = `${id}/${crypto.randomUUID()}-${file.name}`;
    const bytes = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer());

    const { error: uploadError } = await supabaseAdmin.storage
      .from(STORAGE_BUCKET)
      .upload(storagePath, bytes, { contentType: file.type || undefined, upsert: false });
    if (uploadError) throw uploadError;

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("knowledge_files")
      .insert({
        knowledge_source_id: id,
        name: file.name,
        size_bytes: file.size,
        storage_path: storagePath,
      })
      .select("*")
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;

    const fileRow = data as KnowledgeFileRow;

    try {
      await ingestKnowledgeFile(fileRow.id);
    } catch (ingestError) {
      // File stays registered even if ingestion fails — the source's status
      // is already flipped to "Error" inside ingestKnowledgeFile, so the
      // admin UI surfaces it without losing the uploaded file.
      console.error(`Ingestion failed for file ${fileRow.id}:`, ingestError);
    }

    return NextResponse.json(toKnowledgeFileDTO(fileRow), { status: 201 });
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

===== FILE END: app/api/admin/knowledge/[id]/files/route.ts =====

===== FILE START: app/api/admin/knowledge/[id]/files/[fileId]/route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { notFound, serverError } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";
import type { KnowledgeFileRow } from "@/lib/admin/types";

type Params = { params: Promise<{ id: string; fileId: string }> };

const STORAGE_BUCKET = "knowledge";

// DELETE /api/admin/knowledge/:id/files/:fileId
// Removes the metadata row, the underlying Storage object, and its
// knowledge_chunks (cascaded via FK — see 0002_knowledge_chunks.sql).
export async function DELETE(_req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id, fileId } = await params;

    const { data: fileData, error: fetchError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("knowledge_files")
      .select("*")
      .eq("id", fileId)
      .eq("knowledge_source_id", id)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (fetchError) throw fetchError;
    if (!fileData) return notFound(`File ${fileId} not found on source ${id}`);

    const storagePath = (fileData as KnowledgeFileRow).storage_path;
    if (storagePath) {
      const { error: removeError } = await supabaseAdmin.storage.from(STORAGE_BUCKET).remove([storagePath]);
      if (removeError) console.error(`Failed to remove storage object ${storagePath}:`, removeError);
    }

    const { error, count } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("knowledge_files")
      .delete({ count: "exact" })
      .eq("id", fileId)
      .eq("knowledge_source_id", id);

    if (error) throw error;
    if (!count) return notFound(`File ${fileId} not found on source ${id}`);

    return NextResponse.json({ deleted: true });
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

===== FILE END: app/api/admin/knowledge/[id]/files/[fileId]/route.ts =====

===== FILE START: app/api/admin/knowledge/[id]/ingest/route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { ingestKnowledgeSource } from "@/lib/ingestion/pipeline";
import { notFound, serverError } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";

type Params = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

// POST /api/admin/knowledge/:id/ingest — (re)runs the RAG pipeline for every
// file registered under this source. Files also ingest automatically as
// they're uploaded (see files/route.ts); this route is for manual retry
// after an "Error" status or a bulk reindex.
export async function POST(_req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;

    const { data: source, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("knowledge_sources")
      .select("id")
      .eq("id", id)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (error) throw error;
    if (!source) return notFound(`Knowledge source ${id} not found`);

    await ingestKnowledgeSource(id);

    return NextResponse.json({ ingested: true });
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import {
  toKnowledgeSourceDTOFromJoin,
  KNOWLEDGE_SOURCE_WITH_COUNT_SELECT,
  type KnowledgeSourceRowWithCount,
} from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { asRecord, notFound, serverError } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";

type Params = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

// GET /api/admin/knowledge/:id
export async function GET(_req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("knowledge_sources")
      .select(KNOWLEDGE_SOURCE_WITH_COUNT_SELECT)
      .eq("id", id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error) throw error;
    if (!data) return notFound(`Knowledge source ${id} not found`);

    return NextResponse.json(toKnowledgeSourceDTOFromJoin(data as KnowledgeSourceRowWithCount));
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

// PATCH /api/admin/knowledge/:id — rename/redescribe, or flip status once
// ingestion (the AI part) finishes indexing.
export async function PATCH(req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const body = asRecord(await req.json());

    const patch: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    if (typeof body.name === "string") patch.name = body.name;
    if (typeof body.description === "string") patch.description = body.description;
    if (["Indexed", "Processing", "Error"].includes(body.status as string)) {
      patch.status = body.status;
    }

    if (Object.keys(patch).length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "No updatable fields provided" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("knowledge_sources")
      .update(patch)
      .eq("id", id)
      .select(KNOWLEDGE_SOURCE_WITH_COUNT_SELECT)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error) throw error;
    if (!data) return notFound(`Knowledge source ${id} not found`);

    return NextResponse.json(toKnowledgeSourceDTOFromJoin(data as KnowledgeSourceRowWithCount));
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

// DELETE /api/admin/knowledge/:id — cascades to its knowledge_files rows.
export async function DELETE(_req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;

    const { error, count } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("knowledge_sources")
      .delete({ count: "exact" })
      .eq("id", id);

    if (error) throw error;
    if (!count) return notFound(`Knowledge source ${id} not found`);

    return NextResponse.json({ deleted: true });
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

===== FILE START: app/api/admin/knowledge/[id]/urls/route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { toKnowledgeFileDTO, type KnowledgeFileRow } from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { badRequest, notFound, serverError } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";
import { ingestKnowledgeFile } from "@/lib/ingestion/pipeline";
import { fetchAndExtractUrl } from "@/lib/ingestion/fetchUrl";

type Params = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

const STORAGE_BUCKET = "knowledge";

/**
 * Supabase Storage keys reject spaces, accented characters, and various
 * punctuation. This only sanitizes the *storage path* — the human-readable
 * title (with accents, spaces, etc. intact) still goes into the `name`
 * column for display, so French/Arabic/etc. titles show correctly in the UI
 * even though the underlying file key is ASCII-safe.
 */
function slugifyForStorageKey(input: string): string {
  const withoutDiacritics = input.normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, ""); // é -> e, ç -> c, etc.
  const slug = withoutDiacritics
    .replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/-+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 150);
  return slug || "webpage";
}

// POST /api/admin/knowledge/:id/urls — register a web page as a knowledge
// file. Fetches the URL server-side, extracts its main readable content,
// stores that extracted text as a .md file in Storage, then runs it through
// the exact same ingestion pipeline as an uploaded file (see files/route.ts)
// — extract.ts/chunk.ts/embed.ts/pipeline.ts don't know or care that this
// content originated from a URL rather than an upload.
export async function POST(req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;

    const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
    const url = typeof body?.url === "string" ? body.url.trim() : "";
    if (!url) return badRequest("`url` is required (JSON body: { \"url\": \"https://...\" })");

    const { data: source, error: sourceError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("knowledge_sources")
      .select("id")
      .eq("id", id)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (sourceError) throw sourceError;
    if (!source) return notFound(`Knowledge source ${id} not found`);

    // Let fetch/extraction errors (bad URL, unreachable, non-HTML, no
    // readable content) surface as a 400 rather than a generic 500 — these
    // are user-input problems ("that link doesn't work"), not server bugs.
    let page;
    try {
      page = await fetchAndExtractUrl(url);
    } catch (fetchErr) {
      return badRequest(fetchErr instanceof Error ? fetchErr.message : `Could not process "${url}"`);
    }

    const safeName = `${slugifyForStorageKey(page.title || "webpage")}.md`;
    const storagePath = `${id}/${crypto.randomUUID()}-${safeName}`;
    const bytes = Buffer.from(page.textContent, "utf-8");

    const { error: uploadError } = await supabaseAdmin.storage
      .from(STORAGE_BUCKET)
      .upload(storagePath, bytes, { contentType: "text/markdown", upsert: false });
    if (uploadError) throw uploadError;

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("knowledge_files")
      .insert({
        knowledge_source_id: id,
        name: page.title || url,
        size_bytes: bytes.byteLength,
        storage_path: storagePath,
        source_url: url,
      })
      .select("*")
      .single();
    if (error) throw error;

    const fileRow = data as KnowledgeFileRow;

    try {
      await ingestKnowledgeFile(fileRow.id);
    } catch (ingestError) {
      // Same pattern as files/route.ts — the file/URL stays registered even
      // if ingestion fails; the source's status flips to "Error" inside
      // ingestKnowledgeFile so the admin UI surfaces it.
      console.error(`Ingestion failed for file ${fileRow.id} (from URL ${url}):`, ingestError);
    }

    return NextResponse.json(toKnowledgeFileDTO(fileRow), { status: 201 });
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { toPersonaDTO, type PersonaRow } from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { badRequest, serverError, asRecord } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin.from("personas").select("*").order("created_at", { ascending: false });
    if (error) throw error;
    return NextResponse.json((data as PersonaRow[]).map(toPersonaDTO));
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = asRecord(await req.json());

    const name = body.name;
    if (typeof name !== "string" || !name.trim()) return badRequest("`name` is required");

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("personas")
      .insert({
        avatar_id: typeof body.avatarId === "string" ? body.avatarId : null,
        type: typeof body.type === "string" ? body.type : "",
        name,
        prompt: typeof body.prompt === "string" ? body.prompt : "",
        qna_model: typeof body.qnaModel === "string" ? body.qnaModel : "Claude Sonnet",
        creativity: typeof body.creativity === "number" ? body.creativity : 35,
        status: typeof body.status === "string" ? body.status : "Private",
        version: typeof body.version === "string" ? body.version : "v1.0",
        role_title: typeof body.roleTitle === "string" ? body.roleTitle : "",
        department: typeof body.department === "string" ? body.department : "",
        // Drives TTS voice selection - see app/api/conversations/route.ts.
        // Defaults to "female" (matches the DB column default) if omitted.
        gender: body.gender === "male" ? "male" : "female",
      })
      .select("*")
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;

    return NextResponse.json(toPersonaDTO(data as PersonaRow), { status: 201 });
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

===== FILE START: app/api/admin/personas/[id]/route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { toPersonaDTO, type PersonaRow } from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { asRecord, notFound, serverError } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";

type Params = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

export async function GET(_req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin.from("personas").select("*").eq("id", id).maybeSingle();
    if (error) throw error;
    if (!data) return notFound(`Persona ${id} not found`);
    return NextResponse.json(toPersonaDTO(data as PersonaRow));
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

export async function PATCH(req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const body = asRecord(await req.json());

    const patch: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    const stringFields = ["type", "name", "prompt", "roleTitle", "department", "version"] as const;
    const stringColumns: Record<(typeof stringFields)[number], string> = {
      type: "type",
      name: "name",
      prompt: "prompt",
      roleTitle: "role_title",
      department: "department",
      version: "version",
    };
    for (const field of stringFields) {
      if (typeof body[field] === "string") patch[stringColumns[field]] = body[field];
    }

    if (["Claude Opus", "Claude Sonnet", "Claude Haiku"].includes(body.qnaModel as string)) {
      patch.qna_model = body.qnaModel;
    }
    if (["Private", "Public", "Archived"].includes(body.status as string)) {
      patch.status = body.status;
    }
    if (typeof body.avatarId === "string" || body.avatarId === null) {
      patch.avatar_id = body.avatarId;
    }
    if (typeof body.creativity === "number" && body.creativity >= 0 && body.creativity <= 100) {
      patch.creativity = body.creativity;
    }
    // Drives TTS voice selection - see app/api/conversations/route.ts and
    // supabase/migrations/0004_persona_gender.sql.
    if (body.gender === "male" || body.gender === "female") {
      patch.gender = body.gender;
    }

    if (Object.keys(patch).length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "No updatable fields provided" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("personas")
      .update(patch)
      .eq("id", id)
      .select("*")
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error) throw error;
    if (!data) return notFound(`Persona ${id} not found`);

    return NextResponse.json(toPersonaDTO(data as PersonaRow));
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

export async function DELETE(_req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const { error, count } = await supabaseAdmin.from("personas").delete({ count: "exact" }).eq("id", id);
    if (error) throw error;
    if (!count) return notFound(`Persona ${id} not found`);
    return NextResponse.json({ deleted: true });
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

===== FILE START: app/api/chat/route.ts =====
import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { asRecord, badRequest } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";
import { resolveChatContext } from "@/lib/llm/resolveContext";
import { runChatPipeline, type ChatPipelinePersona } from "@/lib/llm/chatPipeline";
import type { ChatMessage } from "@/lib/llm/realtimeClient";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

// POST /api/chat — the real-time LLM turn from the architecture diagram:
// takes one user message, resolves the active persona + its knowledge
// source(s), runs RAG retrieval, and streams back the Claude completion as
// SSE frames (see chatPipeline.ts for the frame shapes).
//
// Two ways to call it:
//
//  1) Persisted flow — the employee-facing /conversation screen.
//     Body: { personaId, avatarId?, combinationId?, conversationId?, message, history? }
//     Persona + knowledge source(s) are resolved from Supabase via
//     avatar_combinations, and the turn is logged if conversationId is set.
//
//  2) Preview flow — the admin avatar wizard's "Test" step, before Publish.
//     Body: { personaOverride, avatarName?, knowledgeSourceIds?, message, history? }
//     `personaOverride` is whatever's currently in the wizard's in-memory
//     draft (WizardData["persona"]) — nothing is persisted or logged, and
//     no personaId exists yet to look one up by.
export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = asRecord(await req.json());

    const message = body.message;
    if (typeof message !== "string" || !message.trim()) {
      return badRequest("`message` is required");
    }

    const history: ChatMessage[] = Array.isArray(body.history)
      ? (body.history as ChatMessage[]).filter(
          (m) => m && (m.role === "user" || m.role === "assistant") && typeof m.content === "string"
        )
      : [];

    let avatarName: string;
    let persona: ChatPipelinePersona;
    let knowledgeSourceIds: string[];
    let conversationId: string | null = null;

    if (body.personaOverride && typeof body.personaOverride === "object" && !Array.isArray(body.personaOverride)) {
      persona = body.personaOverride as ChatPipelinePersona;
      avatarName = typeof body.avatarName === "string" ? body.avatarName : persona.name || "Avatar";
      knowledgeSourceIds = Array.isArray(body.knowledgeSourceIds) ? (body.knowledgeSourceIds as string[]) : [];
    } else {
      const personaId = typeof body.personaId === "string" ? body.personaId : undefined;
      const avatarId = typeof body.avatarId === "string" ? body.avatarId : undefined;
      const combinationId = typeof body.combinationId === "string" ? body.combinationId : undefined;

      if (!personaId && !combinationId) {
        return badRequest("`personaId` (or `combinationId`) is required when not using `personaOverride`");
      }

      const resolved = await resolveChatContext({ personaId, avatarId, combinationId });
      avatarName = resolved.avatarName;
      persona = resolved.persona;
      knowledgeSourceIds = resolved.knowledgeSourceIds;
      conversationId = typeof body.conversationId === "string" ? body.conversationId : null;
    }

    const stream = runChatPipeline({
      avatarName,
      persona,
      knowledgeSourceIds,
      message: message.trim(),
      history,
      conversationId,
    });

    return new Response(stream, {
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "text/event-stream; charset=utf-8",
        "Cache-Control": "no-cache, no-transform",
        Connection: "keep-alive",
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unexpected server error";
    // Same SSE shape as a mid-stream error, so the client's frame parser
    // (see hooks/useChatStream.ts) can handle both with one code path.
    return new Response(`data: ${JSON.stringify({ type: "error", message })}\n\n`, {
      status: 500,
      headers: { "Content-Type": "text/event-stream; charset=utf-8" },
    });
  }
}

===== FILE END: app/api/chat/route.ts =====

===== FILE START: app/api/conversations/route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { toConversationDTO, type ConversationRow, type PersonaRow } from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { asRecord, badRequest, notFound, serverError } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";

// POST /api/conversations — called when an employee picks an avatar on
// /experience and lands on /conversation. Creates the session row and
// returns everything VoiceConversationScreen needs as initial props
// (assistantName, assistantRole) — no STT/LLM/avatar-stream setup happens
// here, that's wired separately per the earlier architecture discussion
// (Deepgram/HeyGen tokens minted by their own routes).
export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = asRecord(await req.json());

    const personaId = body.personaId;
    if (typeof personaId !== "string" || !personaId) return badRequest("`personaId` is required");

    const { data: persona, error: personaError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("personas")
      .select("*")
      .eq("id", personaId)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (personaError) throw personaError;
    if (!persona) return notFound(`Persona ${personaId} not found`);

    const personaRow = persona as PersonaRow;

    const { data: conversation, error: conversationError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("conversations")
      .insert({
        name: typeof body.name === "string" ? body.name : `${personaRow.name} session`,
        is_public: Boolean(body.isPublic),
        personal_avatar_request: Boolean(body.personalAvatarRequest),
        active_avatar_id: personaRow.avatar_id,
        persona_id: personaRow.id,
        conversation_type: body.conversationType === "Text" ? "Text" : "Voice",
        language: typeof body.language === "string" ? body.language : "English",
        creator_user: typeof body.creatorUser === "string" ? body.creatorUser : "anonymous",
        status: "Active",
      })
      .select("*")
      .single();

    if (conversationError) throw conversationError;

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        conversation: toConversationDTO(conversation as ConversationRow),
        // Ready-to-use props for VoiceConversationScreen — brandName is a
        // fixed org constant, everything else comes from the persona row.
        // Tone sliders are included so the client can shape the ElevenLabs
        // voice for this persona via /api/tts (see lib/tts/voiceMapping.ts) —
        // same sliders already used server-side for the system prompt.
        screenProps: {
          brandName: "PWC",
          assistantName: personaRow.name,
          assistantRole: personaRow.role_title || personaRow.type || "Assistant",
          voice: {
            formality: personaRow.formality,
            empathy: personaRow.empathy,
            humour: personaRow.humour,
            confidence: personaRow.confidence,
          },
        },
      },
      { status: 201 }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

===== FILE END: app/api/conversations/route.ts =====

===== FILE START: app/api/conversations/[id]/end/route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { toConversationDTO, type ConversationRow } from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { asRecord, notFound, serverError } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";

// Next.js 16 route handlers receive `params` as a Promise (must be
// awaited) — this was previously accessed synchronously here, which meant
// `params.id` was `undefined` and every call 404'd. `await`-ing a plain
// object still just returns it unchanged, so this is safe either way.
type Params = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

// POST /api/conversations/:id/end — called from the "End session" (X) button
// in VoiceConversationScreen's onEndSession handler (wired up by
// components/chat/ConversationChat.tsx). Only closes out the session row;
// it does not touch or save the transcript itself — that's handled by
// lib/llm/chatPipeline.ts writing to conversation_messages per turn.
export async function POST(req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const body = asRecord(await req.json().catch(() => ({})));

    const { data: existing, error: fetchError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("conversations")
      .select("started_at")
      .eq("id", id)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (fetchError) throw fetchError;
    if (!existing) return notFound(`Conversation ${id} not found`);

    const startedAt = new Date(existing.started_at as string).getTime();
    const endedAt = Date.now();
    const durationSeconds =
      typeof body.durationSeconds === "number" ? body.durationSeconds : Math.round((endedAt - startedAt) / 1000);

    const status = body.status === "Escalated" ? "Escalated" : "Resolved";

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("conversations")
      .update({
        status,
        ended_at: new Date(endedAt).toISOString(),
        duration_seconds: durationSeconds,
      })
      .eq("id", id)
      .select("*")
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error) throw error;
    if (!data) return notFound(`Conversation ${id} not found`);

    return NextResponse.json(toConversationDTO(data as ConversationRow));
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

===== FILE END: app/api/conversations/[id]/end/route.ts =====

===== FILE START: app/api/conversations/[id]/messages/route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { notFound, serverError } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";

type Params = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

type ConversationMessageRow = {
  id: string;
  conversation_id: string;
  role: "user" | "assistant";
  content: string;
  created_at: string;
};

// GET /api/conversations/:id/messages — the read side of the transcript
// logging lib/llm/chatPipeline.ts writes on every turn (conversation_messages
// table, see supabase/migrations/0003_conversation_messages.sql). Returns an
// empty array (not a 404) if the migration hasn't been run yet or nothing's
// been logged for this session — a missing table is a setup gap, not this
// conversation not existing.
export async function GET(_req: NextRequest, { params }: Params) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;

    const { data: conversation, error: conversationError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("conversations")
      .select("id")
      .eq("id", id)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (conversationError) throw conversationError;
    if (!conversation) return notFound(`Conversation ${id} not found`);

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("conversation_messages")
      .select("*")
      .eq("conversation_id", id)
      .order("created_at", { ascending: true });

    if (error) {
      console.error("Failed to read conversation_messages (migration 0003 not run?):", error);
      return NextResponse.json([]);
    }

    const messages = (data as ConversationMessageRow[]).map((row) => ({
      id: row.id,
      role: row.role,
      content: row.content,
      createdAt: row.created_at,
    }));

    return NextResponse.json(messages);
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

===== FILE END: app/api/conversations/[id]/messages/route.ts =====

===== FILE START: app/api/experience/combinations/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import {
  toAvatarDTO,
  toKnowledgeSourceDTO,
  toPersonaDTO,
  type AvatarCombinationRow,
  type AvatarRow,
  type KnowledgeSourceRow,
  type PersonaRow,
  type ResolvedCombination,
} from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { serverError } from "@/lib/admin/api-utils";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const { data: combos, error: comboError } = await supabaseAdmin.from("avatar_combinations").select("*");
    if (comboError) throw comboError;

    const rows = combos as AvatarCombinationRow[];
    if (rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json([]);

    const avatarIds = [...new Set(rows.map((c) => c.avatar_id))];
    const personaIds = [...new Set(rows.map((c) => c.persona_id))];
    const knowledgeIds = [...new Set(rows.flatMap((c) => c.knowledge_source_ids))];

    const [avatarsRes, personasRes, knowledgeRes] = await Promise.all([
      // Fixed: was .eq("status", "Published") — never matched real rows,
      // so a fully-published combination could never appear on /experience.
      supabaseAdmin.from("avatars").select("*").in("id", avatarIds).eq("status", "Public"),
      supabaseAdmin.from("personas").select("*").in("id", personaIds).eq("status", "Public"),
      knowledgeIds.length > 0
        ? supabaseAdmin.from("knowledge_sources").select("*, knowledge_files(count)").in("id", knowledgeIds)
        : Promise.resolve({ data: [], error: null }),
    ]);

    if (avatarsRes.error) throw avatarsRes.error;
    if (personasRes.error) throw personasRes.error;
    if (knowledgeRes.error) throw knowledgeRes.error;

    const avatarsById = new Map((avatarsRes.data as AvatarRow[]).map((a) => [a.id, a]));
    const personasById = new Map((personasRes.data as PersonaRow[]).map((p) => [p.id, p]));
    const knowledgeById = new Map(
      (knowledgeRes.data as (KnowledgeSourceRow & { knowledge_files: { count: number }[] })[]).map((k) => [
        k.id,
        toKnowledgeSourceDTO(k, k.knowledge_files?.[0]?.count ?? 0),
      ])
    );

    const resolved: ResolvedCombination[] = [];
    for (const combo of rows) {
      const avatar = avatarsById.get(combo.avatar_id);
      const persona = personasById.get(combo.persona_id);
      if (!avatar || !persona) continue;

      resolved.push({
        id: combo.id,
        avatar: toAvatarDTO(avatar),
        persona: toPersonaDTO(persona),
        knowledgeSources: combo.knowledge_source_ids
          .map((id) => knowledgeById.get(id))
          .filter((k): k is NonNullable<typeof k> => Boolean(k)),
      });
    }

    return NextResponse.json(resolved);
  } catch (error) {
    return serverError(error);
  }
}

===== FILE END: app/api/experience/combinations/route.ts =====

===== FILE START: app/api/tts/route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { synthesizeSpeech } from "@/lib/llm/ttsClient";

// POST /api/tts - thin wrapper around synthesizeSpeech() so the browser
// plays real generated audio instead of relying on the OS's installed
// SpeechSynthesis voices (see hooks/useTextToSpeech.ts's rewrite - the
// old browser-based approach had no reliable language or gender mapping,
// which is why French replies were playing back in English with a random
// male voice).
export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const text = typeof body.text === "string" ? body.text.trim() : "";
    if (!text) return NextResponse.json({ error: "`text` is required" }, { status: 400 });

    const voice = typeof body.voice === "string" ? body.voice : undefined;

    const audioBuffer = await synthesizeSpeech(text, { voice });

    return new NextResponse(audioBuffer, {
      status: 200,
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "audio/mpeg",
        "Cache-Control": "no-store",
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("TTS route error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "TTS failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

"use client";

import { Suspense } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { ConversationChat } from "@/components/chat/ConversationChat";

// Reads the ?avatar=&persona= query params AvatarPicker links here with
// (see components/home/AvatarPicker.tsx) and hands them to ConversationChat,
// which creates the real conversation row and drives the live chat.
function ConversationRoute() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();

  return (
    <ConversationChat
      avatarId={searchParams.get("avatar") ?? undefined}
      personaId={searchParams.get("persona") ?? undefined}
    />
  );
}

// useSearchParams() opts this route out of static rendering unless wrapped
// in Suspense — this page is inherently dynamic (per-visitor chat session)
// anyway, but wrapping avoids the Next.js build-time warning.
export default function ConversationPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={null}>
      <ConversationRoute />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { AnimatePresence, motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import IntroSequence from "@/components/home/intro/IntroSequence";
import HomepageContent from "@/components/home/HomepageContent";

export default function HomePage() {
  const [introDone, setIntroDone] = useState(false);
  // Respect prefers-reduced-motion: skip straight to the homepage.
  const prefersReducedMotion = useReducedMotion();

  const showIntro = !introDone && !prefersReducedMotion;

  return (
    <main className="relative">
      <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
        {showIntro ? (
          <IntroSequence key="intro" onComplete={() => setIntroDone(true)} />
        ) : (
          <motion.div
            key="home"
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.6 }}
          >
            <HomepageContent />
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </main>
  );
}

===== FILE START: bundle.js =====
// bundle.js — walks the current project folder and writes every source
// file into ONE plain-text file (project-bundle.txt), with markers around
// each file so it can be split back into a real folder tree later.
//
// Why: no zip, no git, no USB, no cloud drive allowed — but a .txt file
// (or pasting its contents into email/Teams/Slack) almost always is,
// since corporate filters target archives/executables, not plain text.
//
// Usage (on the OLD laptop, from the project root):
//   node bundle.js
// This creates ./project-bundle.txt — send that file however you're
// allowed to (email attachment, Teams, Slack, paste into a chat box).

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

const ROOT = process.cwd();
const OUTPUT_FILE = path.join(ROOT, "project-bundle.txt");

// Anything regenerated by npm install / next dev, or that shouldn't travel.
const SKIP_DIRS = new Set(["node_modules", ".next", ".git", "dist", "build", "out"]);
const SKIP_FILES = new Set(["project-bundle.txt", ".DS_Store"]);

// Binary/image extensions can't round-trip safely through plain text — skip them
// and just list them so you know what's missing (fonts/images you'll re-add manually).
const BINARY_EXT = new Set([
  ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".gif", ".webp", ".ico", ".woff", ".woff2", ".ttf", ".otf",
  ".mp4", ".mp3", ".pdf", ".zip",
]);

const START = (p) => `\n===== FILE START: ${p} =====\n`;
const END = (p) => `\n===== FILE END: ${p} =====\n`;

let fileCount = 0;
const skippedBinaries = [];

function walk(dir) {
  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    if (entry.name.startsWith(".") && entry.name !== ".env.local") continue;
    const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name);
    const relPath = path.relative(ROOT, fullPath).split(path.sep).join("/");

    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
      if (SKIP_DIRS.has(entry.name)) continue;
      walk(fullPath);
    } else {
      if (SKIP_FILES.has(entry.name)) continue;
      const ext = path.extname(entry.name).toLowerCase();
      if (BINARY_EXT.has(ext)) {
        skippedBinaries.push(relPath);
        continue;
      }
      appendFile(relPath, fullPath);
    }
  }
}

const out = fs.createWriteStream(OUTPUT_FILE, { encoding: "utf8" });

function appendFile(relPath, fullPath) {
  const content = fs.readFileSync(fullPath, "utf8");
  out.write(START(relPath));
  out.write(content);
  if (!content.endsWith("\n")) out.write("\n");
  out.write(END(relPath));
  fileCount++;
}

walk(ROOT);
out.end(() => {
  console.log(`Wrote ${fileCount} files into ${OUTPUT_FILE}`);
  if (skippedBinaries.length) {
    console.log("\nSkipped binary files (copy these separately, e.g. via email one-by-one):");
    skippedBinaries.forEach((p) => console.log("  -", p));
  }
  console.log("\nSend project-bundle.txt to the new laptop, then run unbundle.js there.");
});

import type { ReactNode, HTMLAttributes } from "react";

type DivProps = HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>;

/** White rounded card on the cream page background. Used for stat tiles, list rows, form sections. */
export function AdminCard({ className = "", children, ...rest }: DivProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`rounded-2xl border border-stone-200/70 bg-white p-6 shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)] ${className}`}
      {...rest}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * Near-black "live" panel — the signature device used for anything real-time
 * or AI-generated: the live test session transcript, the session audio +
 * transcript viewer, the behaviour/appearance preview. Keeps the light admin
 * chrome quiet while making AI output feel distinct and alive.
 */
export function AdminLivePanel({ className = "", children, ...rest }: DivProps) {
  return (
    <div className={`rounded-3xl bg-ink p-6 text-white ${className}`} {...rest}>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

export function AdminStat({
  label,
  value,
  tone = "default",
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  tone?: "default" | "accent" | "warn";
}) {
  const toneClass =
    tone === "accent" ? "text-accent" : tone === "warn" ? "text-red-500" : "text-emerald-600";

  return (
    <AdminCard className="p-5">
      <div className={`text-3xl font-semibold ${toneClass}`}>{value}</div>
      <div className="mt-1 text-sm text-stone-500">{label}</div>
    </AdminCard>
  );
}

type AdminPageHeaderProps = {

  subtitle?: string;
  action?: ReactNode;
  breadcrumb?: ReactNode;

export function AdminPageHeader({ title, subtitle, action, breadcrumb }: AdminPageHeaderProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-6">
      <div>
        {breadcrumb ? <div className="mb-2 text-sm text-stone-500">{breadcrumb}</div> : null}
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-stone-900">{title}</h1>
        {subtitle ? <p className="mt-2 text-[15px] text-stone-500">{subtitle}</p> : null}
      </div>
      {action ? <div className="shrink-0">{action}</div> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

type AdminPrimaryButtonProps = {
  children: ReactNode;
  onClick?: () => void;
  type?: "button" | "submit";
  disabled?: boolean;
};

export function AdminPrimaryButton({ children, onClick, type = "button", disabled = false }: AdminPrimaryButtonProps) {
  return (
    <button
      type={type}
      onClick={onClick}
      disabled={disabled}
      className="rounded-full bg-accent px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow-[0_8px_24px_-6px_rgba(255,109,0,0.55)] transition-transform hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-[0.98] focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent/60 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-panel disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 disabled:hover:scale-100"
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

export function AdminSecondaryButton({ children, onClick, type = "button", disabled = false }: AdminPrimaryButtonProps) {
  return (
    <button
      type={type}
      onClick={onClick}
      disabled={disabled}
      className="rounded-full border border-stone-300 bg-white px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-stone-700 transition-colors hover:bg-stone-50 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent/50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

      aria-label="Admin navigation"

        className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-accent font-serif text-xl font-semibold text-white shadow-[0_0_24px_-4px_rgba(255,109,0,0.65)] transition-transform hover:scale-105"

      >
        P
      </Link>

      <nav className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center gap-2">

          const active = isNavItemActive(pathname, item.href);
          const Icon = item.icon;

          return (
            <Link
              key={item.id}
              href={item.href}
              aria-label={item.label}
              aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
              className={`group relative flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent/70 ${
                active

                  : "text-white/40 hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-white/80"

              <Icon className="h-5 w-5" strokeWidth={active ? 2.25 : 1.75} />

              {/* Tooltip on hover — icon-only rail needs a visible label */}

                {item.label}
              </span>
            </Link>
          );
        })}
      </nav>
    </aside>
  );
}

===== FILE END: components/admin/AdminSidebar.tsx =====

===== FILE START: components/admin/avatar-wizard/AvatarWizard.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { AdminPageHeader, AdminPrimaryButton, AdminSecondaryButton } from "@/components/admin/AdminPageHeader";
import { WizardStepper, wizardSteps } from "./WizardStepper";
import { initialWizardData } from "./wizard-types";
import { StepAvatar } from "./steps/StepAvatar";
import { StepPersona } from "./steps/StepPersona";
import { StepKnowledge } from "./steps/StepKnowledge";
import { StepTest } from "./steps/StepTest";

export function AvatarWizard() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [stepIndex, setStepIndex] = useState(0);
  const [data, setData] = useState(initialWizardData);
  const [publishing, setPublishing] = useState(false);
  const [publishError, setPublishError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const currentStep = wizardSteps[stepIndex];
  const isLastStep = stepIndex === wizardSteps.length - 1;

  function goNext() {
    if (isLastStep) {
      handlePublish();
      return;
    }
    setStepIndex((i) => Math.min(i + 1, wizardSteps.length - 1));
  }

  function goBack() {
    if (stepIndex === 0) {
      router.push("/admin/avatars");
      return;
    }
    setStepIndex((i) => Math.max(i - 1, 0));
  }

  async function handlePublish() {
    setPublishing(true);
    setPublishError(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/admin/avatars/publish", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(data), // { avatar, persona, knowledge } — matches WizardData exactly
      });

      if (!res.ok) {
        const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(body.error ?? "Failed to publish avatar");
      }

      router.push("/admin/avatars");
    } catch (err) {
      setPublishError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to publish avatar");
      setPublishing(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <AdminPageHeader
        title="Create avatar"
        subtitle="Set the avatar, pick its persona, attach knowledge, then test it end to end."
      />

      <div className="mt-8">
        <WizardStepper activeIndex={stepIndex} />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-8">
        {currentStep.id === "avatar" ? (
          <StepAvatar data={data.avatar} onChange={(avatar) => setData({ ...data, avatar })} />
        ) : null}
        {currentStep.id === "persona" ? (
          <StepPersona data={data.persona} onChange={(persona) => setData({ ...data, persona })} />
        ) : null}
        {currentStep.id === "knowledge" ? (
          <StepKnowledge data={data.knowledge} onChange={(knowledge) => setData({ ...data, knowledge })} />
        ) : null}
        {currentStep.id === "test" ? <StepTest data={data} /> : null}
      </div>

      {publishError ? (
        <p className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">
          {publishError}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <div className="mt-8 flex items-center justify-between">
        <AdminSecondaryButton onClick={goBack}>{stepIndex === 0 ? "Cancel" : "Back"}</AdminSecondaryButton>
        <AdminPrimaryButton onClick={goNext} disabled={publishing}>
          {isLastStep ? (publishing ? "Publishing…" : "Publish avatar") : "Next"}
        </AdminPrimaryButton>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

        <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Voice</span>

              data.gender === "female" ? "bg-stone-900 text-white" : "border border-stone-200 text-stone-600"
            }`}
          >
            Female
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onChange({ ...data, gender: "male" })}
            className={`rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
              data.gender === "male" ? "bg-stone-900 text-white" : "border border-stone-200 text-stone-600"
            }`}
          >
            Male
          </button>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
