===== FILE START: components/admin/avatar-wizard/steps/StepAvatar.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useRef } from "react";
import { Upload } from "lucide-react";
import { AdminCard, AdminLivePanel } from "@/components/admin/AdminCard";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/admin/table/Table";
import { OWNER_TYPES, BODY_TYPES } from "@/lib/admin/mock-data";
import type { WizardData } from "../wizard-types";

const ownerTypeOptions: { id: WizardData["avatar"]["ownerType"]; label: string }[] = OWNER_TYPES.map((id) => ({
  id,
  label: id,
}));

const bodyTypeOptions: { id: WizardData["avatar"]["bodyType"]; label: string }[] = BODY_TYPES.map((id) => ({
  id,
  label: id,
}));

export function StepAvatar({
  data,
  onChange,
}: {
  data: WizardData["avatar"];
  onChange: (next: WizardData["avatar"]) => void;
}) {
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  function handleFileSelected(file: File | undefined) {
    if (!file) return;
    if (data.imagePreviewUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(data.imagePreviewUrl);
    onChange({ ...data, imagePreviewUrl: URL.createObjectURL(file) });
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 xl:grid-cols-[1fr_340px]">
      <AdminCard className="flex flex-col gap-5">
        <div>
          <h2 className="font-semibold text-stone-900">Basics</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">Image, name, and ownership for this avatar.</p>
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Image</span>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
              className="flex h-16 w-16 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-stone-300 bg-stone-50 text-stone-400 hover:border-accent hover:text-accent"
            >
              {data.imagePreviewUrl ? (
                // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                <img src={data.imagePreviewUrl} alt="" className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
              ) : (
                <Upload className="h-5 w-5" />
              )}
            </button>
            <input
              ref={fileInputRef}
              type="file"
              accept="image/*"
              className="hidden"
              onChange={(e) => handleFileSelected(e.target.files?.[0])}
            />
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-500">PNG or JPG, at least 512×512px.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <label className="block">
            <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Avatar name</span>
            <input
              value={data.name}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...data, name: e.target.value })}
              placeholder="e.g. Aura"
              className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
            />
          </label>
          <label className="block">
            <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Owner</span>
            <input
              value={data.owner}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...data, owner: e.target.value })}
              placeholder="e.g. Eya Ben Salem"
              className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
            />
          </label>
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Owner type</span>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {ownerTypeOptions.map((opt) => (
              <button
                key={opt.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onChange({ ...data, ownerType: opt.id })}
                className={`rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                  data.ownerType === opt.id
                    ? "bg-stone-900 text-white"
                    : "border border-stone-200 text-stone-600 hover:border-stone-300"
                }`}
              >
                {opt.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Body type</span>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {bodyTypeOptions.map((opt) => (
              <button
                key={opt.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onChange({ ...data, bodyType: opt.id })}
                className={`rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                  data.bodyType === opt.id
                    ? "bg-stone-900 text-white"
                    : "border border-stone-200 text-stone-600 hover:border-stone-300"
                }`}
              >
                {opt.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </AdminCard>

      <div className="xl:sticky xl:top-10 xl:self-start">
        <AdminLivePanel>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-white/40">Live preview</p>
          <div className="mt-4 flex h-32 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-white/5">
            {data.imagePreviewUrl ? (
              // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
              <img src={data.imagePreviewUrl} alt="" className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
            ) : (
              <div className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-accent/20 font-serif text-2xl font-semibold text-accent">
                {data.name ? data.name[0].toUpperCase() : "?"}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-2 text-sm">
            <Row label="Name" value={data.name || "Untitled avatar"} />
            <Row label="Owner" value={data.owner || "Not set"} />
            <Row label="Owner type" value={ownerTypeOptions.find((o) => o.id === data.ownerType)?.label ?? ""} />
            <Row label="Body type" value={bodyTypeOptions.find((b) => b.id === data.bodyType)?.label ?? ""} />
          </div>
          <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between rounded-2xl bg-white/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm">
            <span className="text-white/60">Status</span>
            <StatusBadge label={data.status} tone={data.status === "Private" ? "neutral" : "success"} />
          </div>
        </AdminLivePanel>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Row({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-white/10 pb-2">
      <span className="text-white/40">{label}</span>
      <span className="font-medium text-white">{value}</span>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE END: components/admin/avatar-wizard/steps/StepAvatar.tsx =====

===== FILE START: components/admin/avatar-wizard/steps/StepKnowledge.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Check, File as FileIcon, Upload, X } from "lucide-react";
import { AdminCard } from "@/components/admin/AdminCard";
import { Table, THead, Th, Tr, Td, StatusBadge } from "@/components/admin/table/Table";
import { ActionsMenu } from "@/components/admin/table/ActionsMenu";
import { knowledgeTone } from "@/lib/admin/mock-data";
import type { KnowledgeSource } from "@/lib/admin/types";
import type { WizardData, WizardKnowledgeFile } from "../wizard-types";

function formatBytes(bytes: number) {
  if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`;
  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`;
  return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`;
}

function filesToWizardFiles(fileList: FileList): WizardKnowledgeFile[] {
  return Array.from(fileList).map((file) => ({
    id: `${file.name}-${file.size}-${file.lastModified}`,
    name: file.name,
    sizeLabel: formatBytes(file.size),
  }));
}

export function StepKnowledge({
  data,
  onChange,
}: {
  data: WizardData["knowledge"];
  onChange: (next: WizardData["knowledge"]) => void;
}) {
  const [dragActive, setDragActive] = useState(false);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const [knowledgeSources, setKnowledgeSources] = useState<KnowledgeSource[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch("/api/admin/knowledge")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((rows: KnowledgeSource[]) => {
        if (!cancelled) setKnowledgeSources(rows);
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  function toggleExisting(id: string) {
    const selectedIds = data.selectedIds.includes(id)
      ? data.selectedIds.filter((s) => s !== id)
      : [...data.selectedIds, id];
    onChange({ ...data, selectedIds });
  }

  function addFiles(fileList: FileList | null) {
    if (!fileList || fileList.length === 0) return;
    const incoming = filesToWizardFiles(fileList);
    const existingIds = new Set(data.newSource.files.map((f) => f.id));
    const merged = [...data.newSource.files, ...incoming.filter((f) => !existingIds.has(f.id))];
    onChange({ ...data, newSource: { ...data.newSource, files: merged } });
  }

  function removeFile(id: string) {
    onChange({ ...data, newSource: { ...data.newSource, files: data.newSource.files.filter((f) => f.id !== id) } });
  }

  return (
    <AdminCard>
      <div className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onChange({ ...data, mode: "existing" })}
          className={`rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
            data.mode === "existing" ? "bg-stone-900 text-white" : "border border-stone-200 text-stone-600"
          }`}
        >
          Use existing knowledge
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onChange({ ...data, mode: "new" })}
          className={`rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
            data.mode === "new" ? "bg-stone-900 text-white" : "border border-stone-200 text-stone-600"
          }`}
        >
          Create a new knowledge source
        </button>
      </div>

      {data.mode === "existing" ? (
        <>
          <p className="mb-4 text-sm text-stone-500">Choose which sources this avatar can pull answers from.</p>
          {loading ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">Loading knowledge sources…</p>
          ) : knowledgeSources.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">No knowledge sources yet — create a new one instead.</p>
          ) : (
            <Table>
              <THead>
                <tr>
                  <Th>Select</Th>
                  <Th>Name</Th>
                  <Th>File count</Th>
                  <Th>Status</Th>
                  <Th className="min-w-[220px]">Description</Th>
                  <Th className="text-right">Actions</Th>
                </tr>
              </THead>
              <tbody>
                {knowledgeSources.map((source) => {
                  const selected = data.selectedIds.includes(source.id);
                  return (
                    <Tr key={source.id} className={selected ? "bg-accent/5" : ""}>
                      <Td>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => toggleExisting(source.id)}
                          aria-label={`${selected ? "Remove" : "Add"} ${source.name}`}
                          aria-pressed={selected}
                          className={`flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-md border-2 transition-colors ${
                            selected ? "border-accent bg-accent text-white" : "border-stone-300"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {selected ? <Check className="h-3 w-3" /> : null}
                        </button>
                      </Td>
                      <Td className="font-medium text-stone-900">{source.name}</Td>
                      <Td>{source.fileCount}</Td>
                      <Td>
                        <StatusBadge label={source.status} tone={knowledgeTone(source.status)} />
                      </Td>
                      <Td className="text-stone-500">{source.description}</Td>
                      <Td className="text-right">
                        <ActionsMenu name={source.name} variant="knowledge" />
                      </Td>
                    </Tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </Table>
          )}
        </>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <label className="block">
              <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Source name</span>
              <input
                value={data.newSource.name}
                onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...data, newSource: { ...data.newSource, name: e.target.value } })}
                placeholder="e.g. Onboarding guides"
                className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
              />
            </label>
            <label className="block">
              <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">File count</span>
              <div className="flex h-[46px] items-center rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 bg-stone-50 px-4 text-sm text-stone-500">
                {data.newSource.files.length} file{data.newSource.files.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}
              </div>
            </label>
          </div>

          <label className="block">
            <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Description</span>
            <textarea
              value={data.newSource.description}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...data, newSource: { ...data.newSource, description: e.target.value } })}
              rows={3}
              placeholder="What does this source cover?"
              className="w-full resize-none rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
            />
          </label>

          <div>
            <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Files</span>
            <div
              onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
              onDragOver={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                setDragActive(true);
              }}
              onDragLeave={() => setDragActive(false)}
              onDrop={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                setDragActive(false);
                addFiles(e.dataTransfer.files);
              }}
              className={`flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed px-6 py-8 text-center transition-colors ${
                dragActive ? "border-accent bg-accent/5" : "border-stone-300 hover:border-stone-400"
              }`}
            >
              <Upload className="h-6 w-6 text-stone-400" />
              <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">
                <span className="font-medium text-accent">Click to upload</span> or drag and drop
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-400">PDF, DOCX, TXT, CSV — up to 20 files</p>
              <input
                ref={fileInputRef}
                type="file"
                multiple
                accept=".pdf,.doc,.docx,.txt,.csv,.md"
                className="hidden"
                onChange={(e) => {
                  addFiles(e.target.files);
                  e.target.value = "";
                }}
              />
            </div>

            {data.newSource.files.length > 0 ? (
              <ul className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                {data.newSource.files.map((file) => (
                  <li
                    key={file.id}
                    className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-stone-100 bg-stone-50/60 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                  >
                    <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2 text-stone-700">
                      <FileIcon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-stone-400" />
                      <span className="truncate">{file.name}</span>
                    </span>
                    <span className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-3">
                      <span className="text-xs text-stone-400">{file.sizeLabel}</span>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => removeFile(file.id)}
                        aria-label={`Remove ${file.name}`}
                        className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-stone-400 hover:bg-stone-200 hover:text-stone-700"
                      >
                        <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      </button>
                    </span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            ) : null}
          </div>

          <div className="border-t border-stone-100 pt-6">
            <p className="mb-3 text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Preview</p>
            <Table>
              <THead>
                <tr>
                  <Th>Name</Th>
                  <Th>File count</Th>
                  <Th>Status</Th>
                  <Th className="min-w-[220px]">Description</Th>
                </tr>
              </THead>
              <tbody>
                <Tr>
                  <Td className="font-medium text-stone-900">{data.newSource.name || "Untitled source"}</Td>
                  <Td>{data.newSource.files.length}</Td>
                  <Td>
                    <StatusBadge label="Processing" tone={knowledgeTone("Processing")} />
                  </Td>
                  <Td className="text-stone-500">{data.newSource.description || "—"}</Td>
                </Tr>
              </tbody>
            </Table>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </AdminCard>
  );
}

===== FILE START: components/admin/avatar-wizard/steps/StepPersona.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Check } from "lucide-react";
import { AdminCard } from "@/components/admin/AdminCard";
import { Table, THead, Th, Tr, Td, StatusBadge } from "@/components/admin/table/Table";
import { ActionsMenu } from "@/components/admin/table/ActionsMenu";
import { publishTone } from "@/lib/admin/mock-data";
import type { Persona } from "@/lib/admin/types";
import type { WizardData } from "../wizard-types";
import { PersonaFieldsForm } from "./PersonaFieldsForm";

function CreativityBar({ value }: { value: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
      <div className="h-1.5 w-14 rounded-full bg-stone-100">
        <div className="h-1.5 rounded-full bg-accent" style={{ width: `${value}%` }} />
      </div>
      <span className="text-xs text-stone-500">{value}%</span>
    </div>
  );
}

export function StepPersona({
  data,
  onChange,
}: {
  data: WizardData["persona"];
  onChange: (next: WizardData["persona"]) => void;
}) {
  const [personas, setPersonas] = useState<Persona[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch("/api/admin/personas")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((rows: Persona[]) => {
        if (!cancelled) setPersonas(rows);
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
      <AdminCard>
        <div className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onChange({ ...data, mode: "existing" })}
            className={`rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
              data.mode === "existing" ? "bg-stone-900 text-white" : "border border-stone-200 text-stone-600"
            }`}
          >
            Use an existing persona
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onChange({ ...data, mode: "new" })}
            className={`rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
              data.mode === "new" ? "bg-stone-900 text-white" : "border border-stone-200 text-stone-600"
            }`}
          >
            Create a new persona
          </button>
        </div>

        {data.mode === "existing" ? (
          loading ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">Loading personas…</p>
          ) : personas.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">No personas yet — create a new one instead.</p>
          ) : (
            <Table>
              <THead>
                <tr>
                  <Th>Select</Th>
                  <Th>Type</Th>
                  <Th>Name</Th>
                  <Th className="min-w-[240px]">Prompt</Th>
                  <Th>Q&amp;A model</Th>
                  <Th>Creativity</Th>
                  <Th>Status</Th>
                  <Th>Version</Th>
                  <Th className="text-right">Actions</Th>
                </tr>
              </THead>
              <tbody>
                {personas.map((persona) => {
                  const selected = data.existingPersonaId === persona.id;
                  return (
                    <Tr key={persona.id} className={selected ? "bg-accent/5" : ""}>
                      <Td>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => onChange({ ...data, existingPersonaId: persona.id })}
                          aria-label={`Select ${persona.name}`}
                          aria-pressed={selected}
                          className={`flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 transition-colors ${
                            selected ? "border-accent bg-accent text-white" : "border-stone-300"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {selected ? <Check className="h-3 w-3" /> : null}
                        </button>
                      </Td>
                      <Td>{persona.type}</Td>
                      <Td className="font-medium text-stone-900">{persona.name}</Td>
                      <Td className="max-w-xs">
                        <span className="block truncate text-stone-500" title={persona.prompt}>
                          {persona.prompt}
                        </span>
                      </Td>
                      <Td>{persona.qnaModel}</Td>
                      <Td>
                        <CreativityBar value={persona.creativity} />
                      </Td>
                      <Td>
                        <StatusBadge label={persona.status} tone={publishTone(persona.status)} />
                      </Td>
                      <Td>{persona.version}</Td>
                      <Td className="text-right">
                        <ActionsMenu variant="persona" persona={persona} onChanged={() => {}} />
                      </Td>
                    </Tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </Table>
          )
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
            <PersonaFieldsForm
              data={{
                name: data.name,
                type: data.type,
                prompt: data.prompt,
                qnaModel: data.qnaModel,
                creativity: data.creativity,
                gender: data.gender,
              }}
              onChange={(next) => onChange({ ...data, ...next })}
            />

            <div className="border-t border-stone-100 pt-6">
              <p className="mb-3 text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Preview</p>
              <Table>
                <THead>
                  <tr>
                    <Th>Type</Th>
                    <Th>Name</Th>
                    <Th className="min-w-[240px]">Prompt</Th>
                    <Th>Q&amp;A model</Th>
                    <Th>Creativity</Th>
                    <Th>Status</Th>
                    <Th>Version</Th>
                  </tr>
                </THead>
                <tbody>
                  <Tr>
                    <Td>{data.type || "—"}</Td>
                    <Td className="font-medium text-stone-900">{data.name || "Untitled persona"}</Td>
                    <Td className="max-w-xs">
                      <span className="block truncate text-stone-500" title={data.prompt}>
                        {data.prompt || "—"}
                      </span>
                    </Td>
                    <Td>{data.qnaModel}</Td>
                    <Td>
                      <CreativityBar value={data.creativity} />
                    </Td>
                    <Td>
                      <StatusBadge label="Private" tone="neutral" />
                    </Td>
                    <Td>v1.0</Td>
                  </Tr>
                </tbody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </AdminCard>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE START: components/admin/avatar-wizard/steps/StepTest.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Send, AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import { AdminLivePanel } from "@/components/admin/AdminCard";
import { useChatStream } from "@/hooks/useChatStream";
import type { WizardData } from "../wizard-types";

export function StepTest({ data }: { data: WizardData }) {
  const avatarName = data.avatar.name || "Your avatar";
  const knowledgeSourceIds = data.knowledge.mode === "existing" ? data.knowledge.selectedIds : [];
  const knowledgeCount =
    data.knowledge.mode === "existing" ? data.knowledge.selectedIds.length : data.knowledge.newSource.files.length > 0 ? 1 : 0;

  // Preview mode: nothing about this avatar is persisted yet (Publish
  // hasn't been clicked), so there's no personaId to look up — the wizard's
  // in-memory draft (data.persona) is sent straight through as
  // `personaOverride` and /api/chat builds the system prompt from it
  // directly. Real, already-indexed knowledge sources (mode "existing")
  // are searched live; a brand-new, not-yet-ingested source has nothing to
  // search yet, so it's left out until Publish runs the ingestion.
  const { messages, streamingText, isStreaming, escalated, error, send } = useChatStream({
    mode: "preview",
    avatarName,
    personaOverride: data.persona,
    knowledgeSourceIds,
  });

  const [draft, setDraft] = useState("");

  function handleSend() {
    const text = draft.trim();
    if (!text || isStreaming) return;
    send(text);
    setDraft("");
  }

  const greeting = `Hi, I'm ${avatarName}. Ask me something from the ${
    knowledgeCount ? `${knowledgeCount} knowledge area${knowledgeCount > 1 ? "s" : ""} you picked` : "knowledge base"
  }.`;

  return (
    <AdminLivePanel>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-white/40">Live test session</p>
        {data.knowledge.mode === "new" ? (
          <span className="text-right text-[11px] text-white/40">
            New knowledge isn&rsquo;t indexed yet — answers won&rsquo;t use it until you publish.
          </span>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
        <div className="max-w-[92%] rounded-2xl rounded-tl-sm bg-white/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-white/90">
          {greeting}
        </div>

        {messages.map((m, i) => (
          <div
            key={i}
            className={`max-w-[85%] rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-relaxed ${
              m.role === "user"
                ? "ml-auto rounded-tr-sm bg-accent/20 text-white"
                : "rounded-tl-sm bg-white/10 text-white/90"
            }`}
          >
            {m.content}
          </div>
        ))}

        {isStreaming ? (
          <div className="max-w-[85%] rounded-2xl rounded-tl-sm bg-white/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-white/90">
            {streamingText || "…"}
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      {escalated ? (
        <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/15 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-amber-200">
          <AlertTriangle className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
          This reply tripped a guardrail — it would be flagged for escalation in a real session.
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {error ? <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-red-300">{error}</p> : null}

      <div className="mt-5 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-white/5 p-2">
        <input
          value={draft}
          onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && handleSend()}
          placeholder="Type a test message…"
          disabled={isStreaming}
          className="flex-1 bg-transparent px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-white/30 outline-none disabled:opacity-50"
        />
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleSend}
          disabled={isStreaming}
          aria-label="Send test message"
          className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-accent text-white transition-transform hover:scale-105 disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          <Send className="h-4 w-4" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </AdminLivePanel>
  );
}

      description: string;

  };
};

===== FILE START: components/admin/avatar-wizard/WizardStepper.tsx =====
"use client";

import { Check } from "lucide-react";

export type WizardStepId = "avatar" | "persona" | "knowledge" | "test";

export const wizardSteps: { id: WizardStepId; label: string }[] = [
  { id: "avatar", label: "Avatar" },
  { id: "persona", label: "Persona" },
  { id: "knowledge", label: "Knowledge" },
  { id: "test", label: "Test" },
];

export function WizardStepper({
  activeIndex,
  steps = wizardSteps,
}: {
  activeIndex: number;
  steps?: { id: string; label: string }[];
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center">
      {steps.map((step, i) => {
        const done = i < activeIndex;
        const active = i === activeIndex;

        return (
          <div key={step.id} className="flex flex-1 items-center last:flex-none">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <div
                className={`flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-sm font-semibold ${
                  done
                    ? "bg-accent text-white"
                    : active
                      ? "bg-accent/15 text-accent ring-2 ring-accent"
                      : "bg-stone-100 text-stone-400"
                }`}
              >
                {done ? <Check className="h-4 w-4" /> : i + 1}
              </div>
              <span className={`text-sm font-medium ${active ? "text-stone-900" : "text-stone-500"}`}>
                {step.label}
              </span>
            </div>
            {i < steps.length - 1 ? (
              <div className={`mx-4 h-px flex-1 ${done ? "bg-accent" : "bg-stone-200"}`} />
            ) : null}
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE START: components/admin/knowledge/knowledge-visuals.tsx =====
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { FileText, FileSpreadsheet, FileImage, FileCode2, File as FileIcon } from "lucide-react";
import type { KnowledgeStatus } from "@/lib/admin/types";

/**
 * Two-tone folder glyph, drawn entirely in `currentColor` so a single
 * Tailwind text-* class recolors it. Shared by the folder grid cards and the
 * modal header so a source's status reads visually, not just via badge text.
 */
export function FolderGlyph({
  toneClass,
  className = "h-12 w-16",
}: {
  toneClass: string;
  className?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 64 48" className={`${toneClass} ${className}`} xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
      <path d="M2 10a4 4 0 0 1 4-4h13l6 6h33a4 4 0 0 1 4 4v3H2v-9Z" fill="currentColor" opacity="0.35" />
      <path d="M2 15h60v25a4 4 0 0 1-4 4H6a4 4 0 0 1-4-4V15Z" fill="currentColor" />
    </svg>
  );
}

/** Maps a knowledge source's status to the tone its folder glyph + accents use. */
export function folderToneClass(status: KnowledgeStatus): string {
  if (status === "Indexed") return "text-emerald-500";
  if (status === "Processing") return "text-amber-500";
  return "text-red-500"; // Error
}

/** Icon + tint for a file, guessed from its extension. Purely cosmetic. */
export function fileVisual(name: string): { Icon: LucideIcon; toneClass: string } {
  const ext = name.split(".").pop()?.toLowerCase() ?? "";
  if (["png", "jpg", "jpeg", "gif", "webp", "svg"].includes(ext)) {
    return { Icon: FileImage, toneClass: "text-purple-400" };
  }
  if (["csv", "xls", "xlsx"].includes(ext)) {
    return { Icon: FileSpreadsheet, toneClass: "text-emerald-500" };
  }
  if (["json", "md", "yml", "yaml"].includes(ext)) {
    return { Icon: FileCode2, toneClass: "text-sky-500" };
  }
  if (["pdf", "doc", "docx", "txt"].includes(ext)) {
    return { Icon: FileText, toneClass: "text-accent" };
  }
  return { Icon: FileIcon, toneClass: "text-stone-400" };
}

export function formatBytes(bytes: number) {
  if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`;
  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`;
  return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`;
}

===== FILE START: components/admin/knowledge/KnowledgeFolderCard.tsx =====
"use client";

import { StatusBadge, formatTunisTime } from "@/components/admin/table/Table";
import { ActionsMenu } from "@/components/admin/table/ActionsMenu";
import { knowledgeTone } from "@/lib/admin/mock-data";
import type { KnowledgeSource } from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { FolderGlyph, folderToneClass } from "./knowledge-visuals";

/**
 * A single knowledge source rendered as a "folder" tile instead of a table
 * row. Clicking anywhere on the card opens the quick-look popup
 * (KnowledgeFolderModal); the kebab menu in the corner keeps the existing
 * View/Edit/Delete actions available without leaving the grid.
 */
export function KnowledgeFolderCard({
  source,
  onOpen,
  onChanged,
}: {
  source: KnowledgeSource;
  onOpen: () => void;
  onChanged: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      role="button"
      tabIndex={0}
      onClick={onOpen}
      onKeyDown={(e) => {
        if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
          e.preventDefault();
          onOpen();
        }
      }}
      className="group relative flex cursor-pointer flex-col gap-4 rounded-3xl border border-stone-200/70 bg-white p-6 text-left shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)] transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-stone-300 hover:shadow-lg focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent/50"
    >
      {/* Stops the click from bubbling up and opening the modal underneath the menu. */}
      <div className="absolute right-3 top-3" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <ActionsMenu variant="knowledge" source={source} onChanged={onChanged} />
      </div>

      <FolderGlyph toneClass={folderToneClass(source.status)} />

      <div className="min-w-0">
        <h3 className="truncate pr-8 text-base font-semibold text-stone-900">{source.name}</h3>
        <p className="mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-sm text-stone-500">{source.description || "No description yet."}</p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-auto flex items-center justify-between pt-1 text-xs text-stone-400">
        <StatusBadge label={source.status} tone={knowledgeTone(source.status)} />
        <span>
          {source.fileCount} file{source.fileCount === 1 ? "" : "s"} · {formatTunisTime(source.updatedAt)}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE START: components/admin/knowledge/KnowledgeFolderModal.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Upload, Pencil, X } from "lucide-react";
import { AdminSecondaryButton } from "@/components/admin/AdminPageHeader";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/admin/table/Table";
import { knowledgeTone } from "@/lib/admin/mock-data";
import type { KnowledgeSource, KnowledgeFile } from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { FolderGlyph, folderToneClass, fileVisual, formatBytes } from "./knowledge-visuals";

/**
 * Quick-look popup for a knowledge folder: a friendly grid of the files it
 * contains (icon + name + size, not a bare list), with drag-and-drop / a
 * button to add more and a hover-to-remove control on each tile.
 *
 * `source` is the currently-open KnowledgeSource, or null when closed — the
 * page passes its live copy from the `sources` list so the header (status,
 * file count) stays correct after edits, instead of a stale snapshot from
 * the moment the card was clicked.
 *
 * Renaming, changing status, or editing the description still happens on
 * the existing full-page editor (linked via "Edit details") — this popup is
 * deliberately just the fast, visual way to see and manage what's inside.
 */
export function KnowledgeFolderModal({
  source,
  onClose,
  onChanged,
}: {
  source: KnowledgeSource | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onChanged: () => void;
}) {
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<KnowledgeFile[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [dragActive, setDragActive] = useState(false);
  const [busyFileId, setBusyFileId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [uploadingCount, setUploadingCount] = useState(0);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const sourceId = source?.id ?? null;

  function load(id: string) {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    fetch(`/api/admin/knowledge/${id}/files`)
      .then(async (res) => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to load files");
        return res.json();
      })
      .then((rows: KnowledgeFile[]) => setFiles(rows))
      .catch((err) => setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load files"))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!sourceId) return;
    load(sourceId);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [sourceId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!sourceId) return;
    function onKeyDown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
    }
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    const previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
      document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
    };
  }, [sourceId, onClose]);

  if (!source) return null;

  // Uploads each selected file as a real multipart/form-data request — the
  // API route reads it with req.formData() and expects a `file` field
  // containing actual bytes, not a JSON description of the file. Do NOT set
  // a Content-Type header here: the browser has to generate it itself so it
  // includes the multipart boundary, which is what req.formData() needs.
  async function addFiles(fileList: FileList | null) {
    if (!fileList || fileList.length === 0 || !source) return;
    const filesToUpload = Array.from(fileList);
    setUploadingCount((n) => n + filesToUpload.length);

    for (const file of filesToUpload) {
      try {
        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append("file", file);

        const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/knowledge/${source.id}/files`, {
          method: "POST",
          body: formData, // no headers — let the browser set Content-Type + boundary
        });
        if (!res.ok) {
          const body = await res.json().catch(() => null);
          throw new Error(body?.error ?? `Failed to upload "${file.name}"`);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to upload file");
      } finally {
        setUploadingCount((n) => n - 1);
      }
    }
    load(source.id);
    onChanged();
  }

  async function removeFile(fileId: string) {
    if (!source) return;
    setBusyFileId(fileId);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/knowledge/${source.id}/files/${fileId}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to remove file");
      setFiles((prev) => prev.filter((f) => f.id !== fileId));
      onChanged();
    } catch (err) {
      alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to remove file");
    } finally {
      setBusyFileId(null);
    }
  }

  return createPortal(
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-ink/50 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm"
      onMouseDown={(e) => {
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
    >
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label={`${source.name} folder`}
        className="flex max-h-[85vh] w-full max-w-2xl flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-3xl bg-white shadow-2xl"
      >
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-stone-100 px-6 py-5">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
            <FolderGlyph toneClass={folderToneClass(source.status)} className="h-11 w-14 shrink-0" />
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <h2 className="truncate text-xl font-semibold text-stone-900">{source.name}</h2>
              <p className="mt-1 max-w-md text-sm text-stone-500">{source.description || "No description yet."}</p>
              <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2">
                <StatusBadge label={source.status} tone={knowledgeTone(source.status)} />
                <span className="text-xs text-stone-400">
                  {files.length} file{files.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close"
            className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-stone-400 transition-colors hover:bg-stone-100 hover:text-stone-700"
          >
            <X className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Body — the files, as a friendly icon grid rather than a list */}
        <div
          className={`flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-6 py-5 transition-colors ${dragActive ? "bg-accent/5" : ""}`}
          onDragOver={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            setDragActive(true);
          }}
          onDragLeave={() => setDragActive(false)}
          onDrop={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            setDragActive(false);
            void addFiles(e.dataTransfer.files);
          }}
        >
          {error ? (
            <p className="rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">{error}</p>
          ) : loading ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">Loading files…</p>
          ) : files.length === 0 && uploadingCount === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-stone-300 px-6 py-12 text-center">
              <Upload className="h-6 w-6 text-stone-400" />
              <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">This folder is empty.</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-400">Drag files in here, or use "Add files" below.</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
              {files.map((file) => {
                const { Icon, toneClass } = fileVisual(file.name);
                return (
                  <div
                    key={file.id}
                    className="group relative flex flex-col items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-stone-100 bg-stone-50/60 px-3 py-4 text-center"
                  >
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => removeFile(file.id)}
                      disabled={busyFileId === file.id}
                      aria-label={`Remove ${file.name}`}
                      className="absolute right-1.5 top-1.5 flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white text-stone-400 opacity-0 shadow-sm transition-opacity hover:text-red-600 group-hover:opacity-100 disabled:opacity-50"
                    >
                      <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    </button>
                    <Icon className={`h-8 w-8 ${toneClass}`} strokeWidth={1.5} />
                    <span className="line-clamp-2 w-full break-words text-xs font-medium text-stone-700">
                      {file.name}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-stone-400">{formatBytes(file.sizeBytes)}</span>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
              {uploadingCount > 0 &&
                Array.from({ length: uploadingCount }).map((_, i) => (
                  <div
                    key={`uploading-${i}`}
                    className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-stone-200 bg-stone-50/40 px-3 py-4 text-center"
                  >
                    <Upload className="h-8 w-8 animate-pulse text-stone-300" strokeWidth={1.5} />
                    <span className="text-xs text-stone-400">Uploading…</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 border-t border-stone-100 px-6 py-4">
          <input
            ref={fileInputRef}
            type="file"
            multiple
            accept=".pdf,.doc,.docx,.txt,.csv,.md"
            className="hidden"
            onChange={(e) => {
              void addFiles(e.target.files);
              e.target.value = "";
            }}
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-dashed border-stone-300 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-stone-600 transition-colors hover:border-accent hover:text-accent"
          >
            <Upload className="h-4 w-4" /> Add files
          </button>

          <Link href={`/admin/knowledge/${source.id}`} onClick={onClose}>
            <AdminSecondaryButton>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Pencil className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Edit details
              </span>
            </AdminSecondaryButton>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body
  );
}

import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  UserCircle2,
  Drama,
  MessageCircle,
  Library,
  Settings,
  Microscope,
  type LucideIcon,
} from "lucide-react";

export type AdminNavItem = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  href: string;
  icon: LucideIcon;
};

// Single source of truth for the admin sidebar. Order here is the visual
// top-to-bottom order in the mockups: Dashboard, Avatars, Conversations,
// Knowledge, Settings, Testing lab.
export const adminNavItems: AdminNavItem[] = [
  { id: "dashboard", label: "Dashboard", href: "/admin", icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { id: "avatars", label: "Avatars", href: "/admin/avatars", icon: UserCircle2 },
  { id: "personas", label: "Personas", href: "/admin/personas", icon: Drama },
  { id: "conversations", label: "Conversations", href: "/admin/conversations", icon: MessageCircle },
  { id: "knowledge", label: "Knowledge", href: "/admin/knowledge", icon: Library },
  { id: "testing", label: "Testing lab", href: "/admin/testing", icon: Microscope },
  { id: "settings", label: "Settings", href: "/admin/settings", icon: Settings },
];

/**
 * Returns true if `pathname` falls under `href` (used to light up the
 * matching sidebar icon for nested routes like /admin/avatars/new).
 */
export function isNavItemActive(pathname: string, href: string) {
  if (href === "/admin") return pathname === "/admin";
  return pathname === href || pathname.startsWith(`${href}/`);
}

===== FILE START: components/admin/persona/ChipList.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { X, Plus } from "lucide-react";

type ChipListProps = {
  chips: string[];
  onChange: (chips: string[]) => void;
  tone?: "neutral" | "warn";
  addLabel?: string;
};

export function ChipList({ chips, onChange, tone = "neutral", addLabel = "Add" }: ChipListProps) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState("");
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);

  const chipClass =
    tone === "warn"
      ? "border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-600"
      : "border-stone-200 bg-stone-100 text-stone-700";

  function commitDraft() {
    const value = draft.trim();
    if (value && !chips.includes(value)) onChange([...chips, value]);
    setDraft("");
    setAdding(false);
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 bg-white p-3">
      {chips.map((chip) => (
        <span
          key={chip}
          className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium ${chipClass}`}
        >
          {chip}
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label={`Remove ${chip}`}
            onClick={() => onChange(chips.filter((c) => c !== chip))}
            className="opacity-60 transition-opacity hover:opacity-100"
          >
            <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </button>
        </span>
      ))}

      {adding ? (
        <input
          autoFocus
          value={draft}
          onChange={(event) => setDraft(event.target.value)}
          onBlur={commitDraft}
          onKeyDown={(event) => {
            if (event.key === "Enter") commitDraft();
            if (event.key === "Escape") {
              setDraft("");
              setAdding(false);
            }
          }}
          placeholder="Type and press Enter"
          className="w-44 rounded-full border border-stone-200 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
        />
      ) : (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setAdding(true)}
          className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-dashed border-stone-300 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-stone-500 transition-colors hover:border-accent hover:text-accent"
        >
          <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> {addLabel}
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE START: components/admin/persona/PersonaEditor.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Briefcase, User } from "lucide-react";
import { AdminPageHeader, AdminPrimaryButton } from "@/components/admin/AdminPageHeader";
import { AdminCard, AdminLivePanel } from "@/components/admin/AdminCard";
import type { Persona, PersonaGender } from "@/lib/admin/types";

type PersonaEditorProps = {
  avatarId: string;
  avatarName: string;
};

// NOTE (2026-07-30): this editor previously also had Communication style
// sliders (formality/empathy/humour/confidence), an expertise chip list,
// and a Guardrails card (forbidden topics + escalation instructions).
// Those fields were never real columns on the `personas` table - select("*")
// always returned them as undefined, and the PATCH route never persisted
// them either - so the whole UI was silently a no-op cosmetic mockup. They
// were removed here to match the real schema (see
// supabase/migrations/0004_persona_gender.sql for the audit). If/when those
// get real columns + a matching PATCH allowlist, reintroduce the
// corresponding cards using the same pattern as Role & expertise below.
export function PersonaEditor({ avatarId, avatarName }: PersonaEditorProps) {
  const [personaId, setPersonaId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const [roleTitle, setRoleTitle] = useState("Senior HR Advisor");
  const [department, setDepartment] = useState("People & Culture");
  const [gender, setGender] = useState<PersonaGender>("female");

  const [saveState, setSaveState] = useState<"idle" | "saving" | "saved" | "error">("idle");
  const [saveError, setSaveError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    fetch("/api/admin/personas")
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((personas: Persona[]) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        const existing = personas.find((p) => p.avatarId === avatarId);
        if (!existing) return; // no persona yet - keep the defaults, handleSave will create one

        setPersonaId(existing.id);
        setRoleTitle(existing.roleTitle || "");
        setDepartment(existing.department || "");
        setGender(existing.gender || "female");
      })
      .catch(() => {
        // Non-fatal - editor just falls back to defaults for a brand-new avatar.
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [avatarId]);

  async function handleSave() {
    setSaveState("saving");
    setSaveError(null);

    const payload = { avatarId, roleTitle, department, gender };

    try {
      const res = personaId
        ? await fetch(`/api/admin/personas/${personaId}`, {
            method: "PATCH",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify(payload),
          })
        : await fetch("/api/admin/personas", {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify({ ...payload, name: avatarName, type: roleTitle || "Assistant" }),
          });

      if (!res.ok) {
        const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(body.error ?? "Failed to save persona");
      }

      const saved: Persona = await res.json();
      setPersonaId(saved.id);
      setSaveState("saved");
      setTimeout(() => setSaveState("idle"), 1800);
    } catch (err) {
      setSaveError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save persona");
      setSaveState("error");
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <AdminPageHeader
        title={`Define ${avatarName}'s personality`}
        subtitle="Shape how she thinks, speaks, and represents PwC."
        breadcrumb={
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Link href="/admin/avatars" className="hover:text-stone-700">
              Avatars
            </Link>
            <span>/</span>
            <span>{avatarName}</span>
            <span>/</span>
            <span className="font-medium text-stone-700">Persona</span>
          </span>
        }
        action={
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            {saveState === "error" && saveError ? <span className="text-sm text-red-600">{saveError}</span> : null}
            <AdminPrimaryButton onClick={handleSave} disabled={loading || saveState === "saving"}>
              {saveState === "saved" ? "Saved" : saveState === "saving" ? "Saving…" : "Save persona"}
            </AdminPrimaryButton>
          </div>
        }
      />

      <div className="mt-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 xl:grid-cols-[1fr_360px]">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
          <AdminCard>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-accent/10 text-accent">
                <Briefcase className="h-4 w-4" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h2 className="font-semibold text-stone-900">Role</h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">What {avatarName} does and who she serves.</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Role title</span>
                <input
                  value={roleTitle}
                  onChange={(e) => setRoleTitle(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                />
              </label>
              <label className="block">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-stone-700">Department</span>
                <input
                  value={department}
                  onChange={(e) => setDepartment(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-accent"
                />
              </label>
            </div>
          </AdminCard>

          <AdminCard>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-accent/10 text-accent">
                <User className="h-4 w-4" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h2 className="font-semibold text-stone-900">Voice</h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">Picks the text-to-speech voice used in live conversations.</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-6 flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setGender("female")}
                className={`rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                  gender === "female" ? "bg-stone-900 text-white" : "border border-stone-200 text-stone-600"
                }`}
              >
                Female
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setGender("male")}
                className={`rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                  gender === "male" ? "bg-stone-900 text-white" : "border border-stone-200 text-stone-600"
                }`}
              >
                Male
              </button>
            </div>
          </AdminCard>
        </div>

        <div className="xl:sticky xl:top-10 xl:self-start">
          <AdminLivePanel>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-white/40">Behaviour preview</p>

            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
              <div className="ml-auto max-w-[85%] rounded-2xl rounded-tr-sm bg-accent/20 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white">
                Can I get more parental leave than the policy states?
              </div>
              <div className="max-w-[92%] rounded-2xl rounded-tl-sm bg-white/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-white/90">
                That&rsquo;s a fair question. Standard policy covers 16 weeks — for exceptions, I&rsquo;ll
                connect you with an HR partner who can review your case personally.
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <PreviewTile label="Role" value={roleTitle || "—"} />
              <PreviewTile label="Department" value={department || "—"} />
              <PreviewTile label="Voice" value={gender === "male" ? "Male" : "Female"} />
              <PreviewTile label="Mission" value="Support & guide" />
            </div>
          </AdminLivePanel>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

function PreviewTile({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white/5 p-3.5">
      <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-white/40">{label}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-white">{value}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

type PersonaSliderProps = {
  label: string;
  valueLabel: string;
  minLabel: string;
  maxLabel: string;
  value: number;
  onChange: (value: number) => void;
};

export function PersonaSlider({
  label,
  valueLabel,
  minLabel,
  maxLabel,
  value,
  onChange,
}: PersonaSliderProps) {
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
        <span className="font-semibold text-stone-900">{label}</span>
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-accent">{valueLabel}</span>
      </div>

      <input
        type="range"
        min={0}
        max={100}
        value={value}
        onChange={(event) => onChange(Number(event.target.value))}
        aria-label={label}
        className="mt-3 h-1.5 w-full cursor-pointer appearance-none rounded-full
          [&::-webkit-slider-thumb]:h-5 [&::-webkit-slider-thumb]:w-5 [&::-webkit-slider-thumb]:appearance-none

          [&::-webkit-slider-thumb]:border-accent [&::-webkit-slider-thumb]:bg-white
          [&::-webkit-slider-thumb]:shadow-[0_1px_4px_rgba(0,0,0,0.25)]
          [&::-moz-range-thumb]:h-5 [&::-moz-range-thumb]:w-5 [&::-moz-range-thumb]:rounded-full
          [&::-moz-range-thumb]:border-2 [&::-moz-range-thumb]:border-accent [&::-moz-range-thumb]:bg-white
          [&::-moz-range-thumb]:cursor-pointer"
        style={{
          background: `linear-gradient(to right, var(--color-accent) ${value}%, #e7e5e4 ${value}%)`,
        }}
      />

      <div className="mt-1.5 flex justify-between text-xs text-stone-400">
        <span>{minLabel}</span>
        <span>{maxLabel}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE START: components/admin/table/ActionsMenu.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import {
  MoreHorizontal,
  Eye,
  History,
  FlaskConical,
  Pencil,
  Copy,
  Sparkles,
  Users,
  Archive,
  Trash2,
  Rocket,
  type LucideIcon,
} from "lucide-react";
import type { Avatar, Persona, KnowledgeSource, ResolvedCombination } from "@/lib/admin/types";


type ActionItem = { label: string; icon: LucideIcon; onClick: () => void; destructive?: boolean };

type Router = ReturnType<typeof useRouter>;

async function withBusy(setBusy: (b: boolean) => void, fn: () => Promise<void>) {
  setBusy(true);
  try {
    await fn();
  } catch (err) {
    alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Something went wrong");
  } finally {
    setBusy(false);
  }
}
function combinationItems(
  combo: ResolvedCombination,
  router: Router,
  onChanged: () => void,
  setBusy: (b: boolean) => void
): ActionItem[] {
  const { avatar, persona, knowledgeSources } = combo;
  const isPublic = avatar.status === "Public" && persona.status === "Public";

  return [
    {
    label: "Edit",
    icon: Pencil,
    onClick: () => router.push(`/admin/combinations/${combo.id}`),
    },
    { label: "View tests", icon: FlaskConical, onClick: () => router.push("/admin/testing") },
    // Publishing a combination means both the avatar AND the persona need to
    // be "Public" — /api/experience/combinations filters on both. Toggling
    // just one from here would leave the pair in a half-published state, so
    // this PATCHes both records together.
    {
      label: isPublic ? "Make private" : "Publish",
      icon: Rocket,
      onClick: () =>
        withBusy(setBusy, async () => {
          const nextStatus = isPublic ? "Private" : "Public";
          const [avatarRes, personaRes] = await Promise.all([
            fetch(`/api/admin/avatars/${avatar.id}`, {
              method: "PATCH",
              headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
              body: JSON.stringify({ status: nextStatus }),
            }),
            fetch(`/api/admin/personas/${persona.id}`, {
              method: "PATCH",
              headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
              body: JSON.stringify({ status: nextStatus }),
            }),
          ]);
          if (!avatarRes.ok) throw new Error((await avatarRes.json()).error ?? "Failed to update avatar status");
          if (!personaRes.ok) throw new Error((await personaRes.json()).error ?? "Failed to update persona status");
          onChanged();
        }),
    },
    {
      label: "Duplicate",
      icon: Copy,
      onClick: () =>
        withBusy(setBusy, async () => {
          const res = await fetch("/api/admin/combinations", {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              avatar: `${combo.avatar} (copy)`,
              persona: combo.persona,
              knowledgeSources: combo.knowledgeSources,
              status: "Private",
              
            }),
          });
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to duplicate persona");
          onChanged();
        }),
    },
    {
      label: "Archive",
      icon: Archive,
      onClick: () =>
        withBusy(setBusy, async () => {
          const [avatarRes, personaRes] = await Promise.all([
            fetch(`/api/admin/avatars/${avatar.id}`, {
              method: "PATCH",
              headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
              body: JSON.stringify({ status: "Archived" }),
            }),
            fetch(`/api/admin/personas/${persona.id}`, {
              method: "PATCH",
              headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
              body: JSON.stringify({ status: "Archived" }),
            }),
          ]);
          if (!avatarRes.ok) throw new Error((await avatarRes.json()).error ?? "Failed to archive avatar");
          if (!personaRes.ok) throw new Error((await personaRes.json()).error ?? "Failed to archive persona");
          onChanged();
        }),
    },
    // Deliberately calls /api/admin/combinations/:id, NOT the avatar or
    // persona delete routes — this unpairs the row only. The avatar,
    // persona, and knowledge source all remain intact and reusable.
    {
      label: "Remove pairing",
      icon: Trash2,
      destructive: true,
      onClick: () => {
        if (!confirm(`Remove this combination (${avatar.name} + ${persona.name})? The avatar and persona themselves won't be deleted.`)) return;
        withBusy(setBusy, async () => {
          const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/combinations/${combo.id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to remove combination");
          onChanged();
        });
      },
    },
  ];
}
function personaItems(persona: Persona, router: Router, onChanged: () => void, setBusy: (b: boolean) => void): ActionItem[] {
  return [
    { label: "View", icon: Eye, onClick: () => router.push(`/admin/personas/${persona.id}`) },
    {
      label: "Versions",
      icon: History,
      onClick: () => alert(`Version history isn't tracked yet — "${persona.name}" is currently at ${persona.version}.`),
    },
    { label: "View tests", icon: FlaskConical, onClick: () => router.push("/admin/testing") },
    { label: "Edit", icon: Pencil, onClick: () => router.push(`/admin/personas/${persona.id}`) },
    {
      label: "Duplicate",
      icon: Copy,
      onClick: () =>
        withBusy(setBusy, async () => {
          const res = await fetch("/api/admin/personas", {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              name: `${persona.name} (copy)`,
              type: persona.type,
              prompt: persona.prompt,
              roleTitle: persona.roleTitle,
              department: persona.department,
              qnaModel: persona.qnaModel,
              creativity: persona.creativity,
              status: "Private",
              // Deliberately not carrying avatarId over — a duplicate starts
              // unlinked so you don't end up with two personas both claiming
              // to be "the" persona for the same avatar.
            }),
          });
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to duplicate persona");
          onChanged();
        }),
    },
    { label: "Smart edit", icon: Sparkles, onClick: () => console.log("smart edit", persona.name) },
    {
      label: "Creators",
      icon: Users,
      onClick: () => alert("Creator/audit tracking isn't built yet — there's no user or auth system wired up."),
    },
    // Publish is what actually surfaces a persona (paired with a Published
    // avatar) on the /experience avatar picker — see
    // app/api/experience/combinations/route.ts, which filters to
    // status === "Published" on BOTH sides. There was previously no menu
    // item that could ever set a persona to "Published" at all.
    {
      label: persona.status === "Public" ? "Private" : "Public",
      icon: Rocket,
      onClick: () =>
        withBusy(setBusy, async () => {
          const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/personas/${persona.id}`, {
            method: "PATCH",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify({ status: persona.status === "Public" ? "Private" : "Public" }),
          });
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to update persona status");
          onChanged();
        }),
    },
    {
      label: "Archive",
      icon: Archive,
      onClick: () =>
        withBusy(setBusy, async () => {
          const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/personas/${persona.id}`, {
            method: "PATCH",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify({ status: "Archived" }),
          });
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to archive persona");
          onChanged();
        }),
    },
    {
      label: "Delete",
      icon: Trash2,
      destructive: true,
      onClick: () => {
        if (!confirm(`Delete "${persona.name}"? This can't be undone.`)) return;
        withBusy(setBusy, async () => {
          const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/personas/${persona.id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to delete persona");
          onChanged();
        });
      },
    },
  ];
}

function avatarItems(avatar: Avatar, router: Router, onChanged: () => void, setBusy: (b: boolean) => void): ActionItem[] {
  return [
    { label: "View", icon: Eye, onClick: () => router.push(`/admin/avatars/${avatar.id}/persona`) },
    { label: "Edit", icon: Pencil, onClick: () => router.push(`/admin/avatars/${avatar.id}/persona`) },
    // Same reasoning as personaItems' Publish action: the avatars list had
    // no row actions at all, so an avatar could never leave "Draft" either.
    // Both this AND the linked persona need status "Published" before a
    // combination shows up on /experience.
    {
      label: avatar.status === "Public" ? "Private" : "Public",
      icon: Rocket,
      onClick: () =>
        withBusy(setBusy, async () => {
          const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/avatars/${avatar.id}`, {
            method: "PATCH",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify({ status: avatar.status === "Public" ? "Private" : "Public" }),
          });
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to update avatar status");
          onChanged();
        }),
    },
    {
      label: "Archive",
      icon: Archive,
      onClick: () =>
        withBusy(setBusy, async () => {
          const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/avatars/${avatar.id}`, {
            method: "PATCH",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify({ status: "Archived" }),
          });
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to archive avatar");
          onChanged();
        }),
    },
    {
      label: "Delete",
      icon: Trash2,
      destructive: true,
      onClick: () => {
        if (!confirm(`Delete "${avatar.name}"? This also removes its linked persona and can't be undone.`)) return;
        withBusy(setBusy, async () => {
          const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/avatars/${avatar.id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to delete avatar");
          onChanged();
        });
      },
    },
  ];
}

function knowledgeItems(
  source: KnowledgeSource,
  router: Router,
  onChanged: () => void,
  setBusy: (b: boolean) => void
): ActionItem[] {
  return [
    { label: "View", icon: Eye, onClick: () => router.push(`/admin/knowledge/${source.id}`) },
    { label: "Edit", icon: Pencil, onClick: () => router.push(`/admin/knowledge/${source.id}`) },
    {
      label: "Delete",
      icon: Trash2,
      destructive: true,
      onClick: () => {
        if (!confirm(`Delete "${source.name}"? This removes its files too and can't be undone.`)) return;
        withBusy(setBusy, async () => {
          const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/knowledge/${source.id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error((await res.json()).error ?? "Failed to delete knowledge source");
          onChanged();
        });
      },
    },
  ];
}

type PersonaVariantProps = { variant: "persona"; persona: Persona; onChanged: () => void };
type AvatarVariantProps = { variant: "avatar"; avatar: Avatar; onChanged: () => void };
type KnowledgeVariantProps = { variant: "knowledge"; source: KnowledgeSource; onChanged: () => void };
// Conversations/tests aren't wired to real actions yet — same stub behaviour

type CombinationVariantProps = { variant: "combination"; combination: ResolvedCombination; onChanged: () => void };

type ActionsMenuProps =
  | PersonaVariantProps
  | AvatarVariantProps
  | KnowledgeVariantProps
  | CombinationVariantProps
  | StandardVariantProps;
type StandardVariantProps = { variant?: "standard"; name: string };



const MENU_WIDTH = 192; // w-48
const GAP = 6;

type Coords = { top: number; left: number };

export function ActionsMenu(props: ActionsMenuProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [coords, setCoords] = useState<Coords | null>(null);
  const triggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);



  const items: ActionItem[] =
    props.variant === "persona"
      ? personaItems(props.persona, router, props.onChanged, setBusy)
      : props.variant === "avatar"
        ? avatarItems(props.avatar, router, props.onChanged, setBusy)
        : props.variant === "knowledge"
          ? knowledgeItems(props.source, router, props.onChanged, setBusy)
          : props.variant === "combination"
            ? combinationItems(props.combination, router, props.onChanged, setBusy)
            : [
                { label: "View", icon: Eye, onClick: () => console.log("view", props.name) },
                { label: "Versions", icon: History, onClick: () => console.log("versions", props.name) },
                { label: "View tests", icon: FlaskConical, onClick: () => console.log("view tests", props.name) },
                { label: "Edit", icon: Pencil, onClick: () => console.log("edit", props.name) },
                { label: "Duplicate", icon: Copy, onClick: () => console.log("duplicate", props.name) },
                { label: "Smart edit", icon: Sparkles, onClick: () => console.log("smart edit", props.name) },
                { label: "Creators", icon: Users, onClick: () => console.log("creators", props.name) },
                { label: "Archive", icon: Archive, onClick: () => console.log("archive", props.name) },
                { label: "Delete", icon: Trash2, onClick: () => console.log("delete", props.name), destructive: true },
              ];

  // Computed from the trigger's real screen position every time the menu
  // opens (and on scroll/resize while it's open) — this is what lets the
  // menu render into document.body via a portal instead of the table's own
  // overflow-x-auto wrapper, so no ancestor's overflow can ever clip it.
  const updateCoords = useCallback(() => {
    const trigger = triggerRef.current;
    if (!trigger) return;
    const rect = trigger.getBoundingClientRect();
    const menuHeight = menuRef.current?.offsetHeight ?? items.length * 40 + 12;
    const spaceBelow = window.innerHeight - rect.bottom;
    const openUpward = spaceBelow < menuHeight + GAP && rect.top > menuHeight + GAP;

    setCoords({
      top: openUpward ? rect.top - menuHeight - GAP : rect.bottom + GAP,
      left: Math.min(Math.max(rect.right - MENU_WIDTH, GAP), window.innerWidth - MENU_WIDTH - GAP),
    });
  }, [items.length]);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    updateCoords();
  }, [open, updateCoords]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;

    function onPointerDown(e: MouseEvent) {
      const target = e.target as Node;
      if (triggerRef.current?.contains(target)) return;
      if (menuRef.current?.contains(target)) return;
      setOpen(false);
    }
    function onKeyDown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (e.key === "Escape") setOpen(false);
    }
    window.addEventListener("scroll", updateCoords, true);
    window.addEventListener("resize", updateCoords);
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onPointerDown);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("scroll", updateCoords, true);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateCoords);
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onPointerDown);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    };
  }, [open, updateCoords]);

  return (
    <>
      <button
        ref={triggerRef}
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
        disabled={busy}
        aria-label="Row actions"
        aria-haspopup="menu"
        aria-expanded={open}
        className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-stone-400 transition-colors hover:bg-stone-100 hover:text-stone-700 disabled:opacity-40"
      >
        <MoreHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" />
      </button>

      {open && coords
        ? createPortal(
            <div
              ref={menuRef}
              role="menu"
              style={{ position: "fixed", top: coords.top, left: coords.left, width: MENU_WIDTH }}
              className="z-50 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 bg-white py-1.5 shadow-lg"
            >
              {items.map((item) => (
                <button
                  key={item.label}
                  type="button"
                  role="menuitem"
                  onClick={() => {
                    item.onClick();
                    setOpen(false);
                  }}
                  className={`flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 px-3.5 py-2 text-left text-sm transition-colors ${
                    item.destructive ? "text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50" : "text-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-50"
                  }`}
                >
                  <item.icon className="h-4 w-4" />
                  {item.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>,
            document.body
          )
        : null}
    </>
  );
}
