===== FILE START: lib/ingestion/pipeline.ts =====
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import type { KnowledgeFileRow, KnowledgeStatus } from "@/lib/admin/types";
import { extractText } from "./extract";
import { chunkText } from "./chunk";
import { embedTexts } from "./embed";

const STORAGE_BUCKET = "knowledge";

async function setSourceStatus(sourceId: string, status: KnowledgeStatus) {
  const { error } = await supabaseAdmin.from("knowledge_sources").update({ status }).eq("id", sourceId);
  if (error) console.error(`Failed to set status=${status} on source ${sourceId}:`, error);
}

/**
 * Ingests a single knowledge_file: download its bytes from Storage, extract
 * text, chunk it, embed each chunk, and upsert into knowledge_chunks tagged
 * with both knowledge_source_id (retrieval filter) and knowledge_file_id
 * (traceability). Idempotent — reruns replace that file's previous chunks.
 */
export async function ingestKnowledgeFile(fileId: string): Promise<void> {
  const { data: fileData, error: fileError } = await supabaseAdmin
    .from("knowledge_files")
    .select("*")
    .eq("id", fileId)
    .maybeSingle();
  if (fileError) throw fileError;
  if (!fileData) throw new Error(`Knowledge file ${fileId} not found`);

  const file = fileData as KnowledgeFileRow;
  if (!file.storage_path) throw new Error(`Knowledge file ${fileId} has no storage_path to ingest`);

  await setSourceStatus(file.knowledge_source_id, "Processing");

  try {
    const { data: blob, error: downloadError } = await supabaseAdmin.storage
      .from(STORAGE_BUCKET)
      .download(file.storage_path);
    if (downloadError) throw downloadError;

    const buffer = Buffer.from(await blob.arrayBuffer());
    const text = await extractText(buffer, file.storage_path);
    const chunks = chunkText(text);

    const { error: deleteError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("knowledge_chunks")
      .delete()
      .eq("knowledge_file_id", fileId);
    if (deleteError) throw deleteError;

    if (chunks.length > 0) {
      const embeddings = await embedTexts(chunks, "document");

      const rows = chunks.map((content, i) => ({
        knowledge_source_id: file.knowledge_source_id,
        knowledge_file_id: fileId,
        chunk_index: i,
        content,
        embedding: embeddings[i],
      }));

      const { error: insertError } = await supabaseAdmin.from("knowledge_chunks").insert(rows);
      if (insertError) throw insertError;
    }

    await setSourceStatus(file.knowledge_source_id, "Indexed");
  } catch (err) {
    await setSourceStatus(file.knowledge_source_id, "Error");
    throw err;
  }
}

/** Re-ingests every file registered under a knowledge_source (bulk reindex). */
export async function ingestKnowledgeSource(sourceId: string): Promise<void> {
  const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
    .from("knowledge_files")
    .select("id")
    .eq("knowledge_source_id", sourceId);
  if (error) throw error;

  const files = (data ?? []) as { id: string }[];
  if (files.length === 0) {
    await setSourceStatus(sourceId, "Indexed");
    return;
  }

  let hadError = false;
  for (const f of files) {
    try {
      await ingestKnowledgeFile(f.id);
    } catch (err) {
      hadError = true;
      console.error(`Ingestion failed for file ${f.id}:`, err);
    }
  }
  await setSourceStatus(sourceId, hadError ? "Error" : "Indexed");
}

===== FILE END: lib/ingestion/pipeline.ts =====

===== FILE START: lib/ingestion/retrieve.ts =====
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { embedTexts } from "./embed";

export type RetrievedChunk = {
  id: string;
  knowledgeFileId: string;
  content: string;
  similarity: number;
};

type MatchRow = { id: string; knowledge_file_id: string; content: string; similarity: number };

/**
 * Vector search scoped to a single knowledge_source_id — the chatbot passes
 * the source tied to the active persona/avatar combination. The scoping is
 * enforced inside the match_knowledge_chunks SQL function itself (see
 * 0002_knowledge_chunks.sql), so results can never cross into another
 * source's chunks even if a caller omits the filter.
 */
export async function searchKnowledgeChunks(
  query: string,
  knowledgeSourceId: string,
  matchCount = 6
): Promise<RetrievedChunk[]> {
  const [queryEmbedding] = await embedTexts([query], "query");

  const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin.rpc("match_knowledge_chunks", {
    query_embedding: queryEmbedding,
    filter_source_id: knowledgeSourceId,
    match_count: matchCount,
  });
  if (error) throw error;

  return ((data ?? []) as MatchRow[]).map((row) => ({
    id: row.id,
    knowledgeFileId: row.knowledge_file_id,
    content: row.content,
    similarity: row.similarity,
  }));
}

===== FILE END: lib/ingestion/retrieve.ts =====

===== FILE START: lib/llm/chatClient.ts =====
// lib/llm/chatClient.ts
// Replaces lib/llm/realtimeClient.ts (deleted - see git history for the
// full gpt-realtime-2 debugging saga: wrong WS path, temperature rejected
// at both session.update and response.create, ~10-1000x pricier per token
// than the Claude models we now use). This is a plain HTTP + SSE client
// against this gateway's standard /chat/completions route - same
// debuggability as lib/ingestion/embed.ts (curl-testable, clear status
// codes/JSON error bodies) instead of opaque WebSocket handshake failures.
import "server-only";
import { DEFAULT_MAX_TOKENS } from "./models";

export type ChatMessage = { role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string };
export type ChatUsage = { promptTokens: number; completionTokens: number };

type StreamArgs = {
  model: string;
  system: string;
  messages: ChatMessage[];
  maxTokens?: number;
  /** 0-1, same range chatPipeline.ts already computes from persona.creativity */
  temperature?: number;
  usageOut?: { current: ChatUsage | null };
  signal?: AbortSignal;
};

// Same Azure resource/endpoint pattern as embed.ts - reuses
// AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT / AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY, flat "/chat/completions"
// path, and the "api-key" header. This works for ALL model prefixes on
// this gateway (azure.*, openai.*, bedrock.*, vertex_ai.*) since it's a
// LiteLLM proxy (confirmed via its /redoc page) that routes purely off the
// `model` string in the request body - same host/key regardless of which
// upstream provider (Azure, OpenAI, AWS Bedrock, GCP Vertex) a given model
// string maps to.
function getEndpoint(): string {
  const base = process.env.AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT;
  if (!base) throw new Error("Missing AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT in .env.local");
  return `${base.replace(/\/+$/, "")}/chat/completions`;
}

// chatPipeline.ts's persona.creativity is already normalized to 0-1 before
// reaching this function. We pass that value through unchanged (no *2
// scaling onto OpenAI's 0-2 range) because Anthropic Claude's native
// `temperature` range is 0-1, and a value >1 would be rejected. 0-1 is
// safe for every model this gateway proxies (OpenAI, Claude via Bedrock,
// Gemini via Vertex).
function toChatTemperature(t: number | undefined): number {
  return Number(Math.min(1, Math.max(0, t ?? 0.5)).toFixed(2));
}

/**
 * Streams a text reply via standard OpenAI-compatible Chat Completions
 * (SSE). Works against any model this gateway proxies since they all
 * share the same normalized request/response schema through LiteLLM.
 */
export async function* streamChatMessage({
  model,
  system,
  messages,
  maxTokens = DEFAULT_MAX_TOKENS,
  temperature,
  usageOut,
  signal,
}: StreamArgs): AsyncGenerator<string, void, unknown> {
  const apiKey = process.env.AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY;
  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("Missing AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY in .env.local");

  const url = getEndpoint();

  const body = {
    model,
    messages: [{ role: "system", content: system }, ...messages],
    max_tokens: maxTokens,
    temperature: toChatTemperature(temperature),
    stream: true,
    stream_options: { include_usage: true },
  };

  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      "api-key": apiKey,
    },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
    signal,
  });

  if (!res.ok || !res.body) {
    const text = await res.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`Chat completions request failed (${res.status}) URL=${url} Model=${model} Response=${text}`);
  }

  const reader = res.body.getReader();
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  let buffer = "";

  try {
    while (true) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;

      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
      const lines = buffer.split("\n");
      buffer = lines.pop() ?? "";

      for (const line of lines) {
        const trimmed = line.trim();
        if (!trimmed.startsWith("data:")) continue;
        const payload = trimmed.slice(5).trim();
        if (payload === "[DONE]") return;

        let json: any;
        try {
          json = JSON.parse(payload);
        } catch {
          continue; // partial/malformed chunk - skip rather than crash the stream
        }

        const delta = json.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content;
        if (delta) yield delta;

        if (json.usage && usageOut) {
          usageOut.current = {
            promptTokens: json.usage.prompt_tokens ?? 0,
            completionTokens: json.usage.completion_tokens ?? 0,
          };
        }
      }
    }
  } finally {
    reader.releaseLock();
  }
}

===== FILE END: lib/llm/chatClient.ts =====

===== FILE START: lib/llm/chatPipeline.ts =====
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { searchKnowledgeChunks, type RetrievedChunk } from "@/lib/ingestion/retrieve";
import { buildSystemPrompt, type PersonaPromptInput } from "./systemPrompt";
import { streamChatMessage, type ChatMessage, type ChatUsage } from "./chatClient";
import { resolveModelId } from "./models";
import { logChatMetrics, persistChatMetrics, type ChatTurnMetrics, type RetrievalMetrics } from "./metrics";

export type ChatPipelinePersona = PersonaPromptInput & {
  qnaModel?: string;
  creativity?: number;
};

export type ChatPipelineInput = {
  avatarName: string;
  persona: ChatPipelinePersona;
  knowledgeSourceIds: string[];
  message: string;
  history: ChatMessage[];
  /** When set, the turn gets logged to conversation_messages and can flag the conversation as Escalated. */
  conversationId?: string | null;
};

const MATCHES_PER_SOURCE = 5;
const MAX_CONTEXT_CHUNKS = 6;

/**
 * Vector search across every knowledge source tied to this persona/avatar
 * combination, merged and re-ranked - timed per source so a single slow
 * or retrying source shows up by id instead of just inflating one opaque
 * total.
 */
async function retrieveContext(
  query: string,
  knowledgeSourceIds: string[]
): Promise<{ chunks: RetrievedChunk[]; metrics: RetrievalMetrics }> {
  const start = Date.now();

  if (knowledgeSourceIds.length === 0) {
    return {
      chunks: [],
      metrics: {
        knowledgeSourceIds,
        perSourceMs: {},
        totalMs: 0,
        chunksReturned: 0,
        chunksUsed: 0,
        topSimilarity: null,
        avgSimilarity: null,
      },
    };
  }

  const perSourceMs: Record<string, number> = {};

  const results = await Promise.allSettled(
    knowledgeSourceIds.map(async (id) => {
      const sourceStart = Date.now();
      try {
        const found = await searchKnowledgeChunks(query, id, MATCHES_PER_SOURCE);
        perSourceMs[id] = Date.now() - sourceStart;
        return found;
      } catch (err) {
        perSourceMs[id] = Date.now() - sourceStart;
        throw err;
      }
    })
  );

  const allChunks: RetrievedChunk[] = [];
  for (const result of results) {
    if (result.status === "fulfilled") {
      allChunks.push(...result.value);
    } else {
      console.error("Knowledge retrieval failed for one source:", result.reason);
    }
  }

  const chunksReturned = allChunks.length;
  const topChunks = allChunks.sort((a, b) => b.similarity - a.similarity).slice(0, MAX_CONTEXT_CHUNKS);
  const similarities = topChunks.map((c) => c.similarity);

  return {
    chunks: topChunks,
    metrics: {
      knowledgeSourceIds,
      perSourceMs,
      totalMs: Date.now() - start,
      chunksReturned,
      chunksUsed: topChunks.length,
      topSimilarity: similarities[0] ?? null,
      avgSimilarity:
        similarities.length > 0
          ? Number((similarities.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / similarities.length).toFixed(4))
          : null,
    },
  };
}

/** Best-effort transcript logging - a missing conversation_messages table shouldn't break the live chat. */
async function logMessages(conversationId: string | null | undefined, userMessage: string, assistantMessage: string) {
  if (!conversationId) return;
  try {
    const { error } = await supabaseAdmin.from("conversation_messages").insert([
      { conversation_id: conversationId, role: "user", content: userMessage },
      { conversation_id: conversationId, role: "assistant", content: assistantMessage },
    ]);
    if (error) throw error;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Failed to log conversation messages (run supabase/migrations/0003_conversation_messages.sql?):", err);
  }
}

function sseFrame(data: unknown) {
  return `data: ${JSON.stringify(data)}\n\n`;
}

/**
 * RAG retrieval -> persona-shaped system prompt -> streamed chat completion
 * (Anthropic Claude via this gateway's Bedrock route) -> conversation
 * logging -> metrics.
 *
 * NOTE: the guardrail/escalation step (forbidden-topic keyword matching,
 * see lib/llm/guardrail.ts) was removed from this pipeline on 2026-07-30 -
 * it depended on persona.forbiddenTopics, which was never a real column on
 * the personas table (select("*") always returned undefined for it), so it
 * was already a permanent no-op. guardrail.ts itself is left in place,
 * unused, in case forbidden_topics gets a real column + admin UI later -
 * re-wire it here (and flagEscalation, also removed) at that point.
 *
 * Frame types sent to the client:
 *   {type:"sources", sources:[{id, similarity}]}                - once, after retrieval
 *   {type:"delta", text}                                        - many, as tokens arrive
 *   {type:"done", fullText, provider, metrics}                  - once, at the end
 *   {type:"error", message}                                      - instead of "done" on failure
 */
export function runChatPipeline(input: ChatPipelineInput): ReadableStream<Uint8Array> {
  const encoder = new TextEncoder();

  return new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
    async start(controller) {
      function send(data: unknown) {
        controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(sseFrame(data)));
      }

      const turnStart = Date.now();
      let fullText = "";
      let genStart = Date.now();

      try {
        const { chunks: contextChunks, metrics: retrievalMetrics } = await retrieveContext(
          input.message,
          input.knowledgeSourceIds
        );
        send({ type: "sources", sources: contextChunks.map((c) => ({ id: c.id, similarity: c.similarity })) });

        const system = buildSystemPrompt({
          avatarName: input.avatarName,
          persona: input.persona,
          contextChunks,
        });

        const model = resolveModelId(input.persona.qnaModel);
        const temperature =
          typeof input.persona.creativity === "number" ? Math.min(1, Math.max(0, input.persona.creativity / 100)) : 0.5;

        const messages: ChatMessage[] = [...input.history, { role: "user", content: input.message }];

        const usageOut: { current: ChatUsage | null } = { current: null };
        genStart = Date.now();
        let firstTokenAt: number | null = null;

        for await (const delta of streamChatMessage({ model, system, messages, temperature, usageOut })) {
          if (firstTokenAt === null) firstTokenAt = Date.now();
          fullText += delta;
          send({ type: "delta", text: delta });
        }
        const genEnd = Date.now();

        await logMessages(input.conversationId, input.message, fullText);

        const generationTotalMs = genEnd - genStart;
        const usage = usageOut.current;

        const metrics: ChatTurnMetrics = {
          conversationId: input.conversationId ?? null,
          personaName: input.persona.name || input.avatarName,
          message: input.message,
          retrieval: retrievalMetrics,
          generation: {
            provider: "anthropic-claude",
            model,
            temperature,
            timeToFirstTokenMs: firstTokenAt !== null ? firstTokenAt - genStart : null,
            totalGenerationMs: generationTotalMs,
            outputChars: fullText.length,
            promptTokens: usage?.promptTokens ?? null,
            completionTokens: usage?.completionTokens ?? null,
            tokensPerSecond:
              usage && generationTotalMs > 0
                ? Number((usage.completionTokens / (generationTotalMs / 1000)).toFixed(1))
                : null,
          },
          guardrail: { escalate: false, reason: null },
          totalTurnMs: Date.now() - turnStart,
        };

        logChatMetrics(metrics);
        await persistChatMetrics(metrics);

        send({ type: "done", escalate: false, reason: null, fullText, provider: "anthropic-claude", metrics });
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Chat pipeline error:", err);
        send({ type: "error", message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unexpected error" });
      } finally {
        controller.close();
      }
    },
  });
}

===== FILE END: lib/llm/chatPipeline.ts =====

===== FILE START: lib/llm/groqClient.ts =====
// lib/llm/groqClient.ts
import { DEFAULT_MAX_TOKENS } from "./models";

export type ChatMessage = { role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string };

const GROQ_API_URL = "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions";

type StreamArgs = {
  model: string;
  system: string;
  messages: ChatMessage[];
  maxTokens?: number;
  temperature?: number;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
};

type GroqSSEChunk = {
  choices?: { delta?: { content?: string }; finish_reason?: string | null }[];
};

/**
 * Streams a chat completion from Groq's OpenAI-compatible endpoint
 * (https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions) — raw fetch, no SDK,
 * same approach lib/ingestion/embed.ts already uses for Voyage. Now the
 * only LLM client in the pipeline (anthropicClient.ts removed).
 *
 * Unlike Anthropic's Messages API, the OpenAI-compatible shape puts the
 * system prompt inside the `messages` array rather than a separate
 * top-level field.
 */
export async function* streamGroqMessage({
  model,
  system,
  messages,
  maxTokens = DEFAULT_MAX_TOKENS,
  temperature = 0.5,
  signal,
}: StreamArgs): AsyncGenerator<string, void, unknown> {
  const apiKey = process.env.GROQ_API_KEY;
  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("Missing GROQ_API_KEY in .env.local");

  const res = await fetch(GROQ_API_URL, {
    method: "POST",
    signal,
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
      "content-type": "application/json",
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      model,
      messages: [{ role: "system", content: system }, ...messages],
      max_tokens: maxTokens,
      temperature,
      stream: true,
    }),
  });

  if (!res.ok || !res.body) {
    const body = await res.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`Groq API request failed (${res.status}): ${body}`);
  }

  const reader = res.body.getReader();
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  let buffer = "";

  try {
    while (true) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });

      const frames = buffer.split("\n\n");
      buffer = frames.pop() ?? "";

      for (const frame of frames) {
        const dataLine = frame.split("\n").find((line) => line.startsWith("data:"));
        if (!dataLine) continue;

        const jsonStr = dataLine.slice(5).trim();
        if (!jsonStr || jsonStr === "[DONE]") continue;

        let chunk: GroqSSEChunk;
        try {
          chunk = JSON.parse(jsonStr);
        } catch {
          continue; // malformed/partial frame — skip rather than crash the stream
        }

        const text = chunk.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content;
        if (text) yield text;
      }
    }
  } finally {
    reader.releaseLock();
  }
}

===== FILE END: lib/llm/groqClient.ts =====

===== FILE START: lib/llm/metrics.ts =====
// lib/llm/metrics.ts

export type RetrievalMetrics = {
  knowledgeSourceIds: string[];
  perSourceMs: Record<string, number>;
  totalMs: number;
  chunksReturned: number;
  chunksUsed: number;
  topSimilarity: number | null;
  avgSimilarity: number | null;
};

export type ChatTurnMetrics = {
  conversationId: string | null;
  personaName: string;
  message: string;
  retrieval: RetrievalMetrics;
  generation: {
    provider: string;
    model: string;
    temperature: number;
    timeToFirstTokenMs: number | null;
    totalGenerationMs: number;
    outputChars: number;
    promptTokens: number | null;
    completionTokens: number | null;
    tokensPerSecond: number | null;
  };
  guardrail: {
    escalate: boolean;
    reason: string | null;
  };
  totalTurnMs: number;
};

/** Console-only metrics logging. No persistence — swap in a real store later if needed. */
export function logChatMetrics(metrics: ChatTurnMetrics): void {
  console.log(
    `[chat-metrics] persona="${metrics.personaName}" turn=${metrics.totalTurnMs}ms ` +
      `retrieval=${metrics.retrieval.totalMs}ms (${metrics.retrieval.chunksUsed}/${metrics.retrieval.chunksReturned} chunks, ` +
      `top=${metrics.retrieval.topSimilarity ?? "n/a"}, avg=${metrics.retrieval.avgSimilarity ?? "n/a"}) ` +
      `gen=${metrics.generation.totalGenerationMs}ms ttft=${metrics.generation.timeToFirstTokenMs ?? "n/a"}ms ` +
      `tok/s=${metrics.generation.tokensPerSecond ?? "n/a"} (${metrics.generation.promptTokens ?? "?"}→${metrics.generation.completionTokens ?? "?"} tok) ` +
      `escalate=${metrics.guardrail.escalate}${metrics.guardrail.reason ? ` (${metrics.guardrail.reason})` : ""}`
  );

  if (Object.keys(metrics.retrieval.perSourceMs).length > 0) {
    console.log("[chat-metrics] per-source retrieval:", metrics.retrieval.perSourceMs);
  }
}

/** No-op for now — persistence disabled until a chat_metrics table exists. */
export async function persistChatMetrics(_metrics: ChatTurnMetrics): Promise<void> {
  return;
}

// lib/llm/models.ts
import type { QnaModel } from "@/lib/admin/types";

// Conversational replies should stay short (see buildSystemPrompt) - this
// is a ceiling, not a target, mostly there to bound latency/cost per turn.
export const DEFAULT_MAX_TOKENS = 1024;

/**
 * Maps the persona editor's Q&A model picker (qna_model column on
 * personas - "Claude Opus" / "Claude Sonnet" / "Claude Haiku") onto REAL
 * Anthropic Claude models, proxied through this gateway's AWS Bedrock
 * route (bedrock.anthropic.* - see Models_List_EMEA.xlsx, audited
 * 2026-07-30). These were previously mapped to gpt-realtime-* models
 * under the same three labels - see chatClient.ts (replacing
 * realtimeClient.ts) for why Realtime was dropped (no temperature
 * support on this gateway, WebSocket complexity, ~10-1000x more
 * expensive per token).
 *
 * Cost per ~1500in/300out token turn (from the same catalog):
 *   claude-opus-4-5:   $5.00 / $25.00 per 1M  -> ~$0.0150/turn
 *   claude-sonnet-4-5: $3.30 / $16.50 per 1M  -> ~$0.0099/turn
 *   claude-haiku-4-5:  $1.00 / $5.00  per 1M  -> ~$0.0030/turn
 */
const CHAT_MODEL_MAP: Record<QnaModel, string> = {
  "Claude Opus": process.env.CHAT_MODEL_QUALITY || "bedrock.anthropic.claude-opus-4-5",
  "Claude Sonnet": process.env.CHAT_MODEL_BALANCED || "bedrock.anthropic.claude-sonnet-4-5",
  "Claude Haiku": process.env.CHAT_MODEL_FAST || "bedrock.anthropic.claude-haiku-4-5",
};

const DEFAULT_QNA_MODEL: QnaModel = "Claude Sonnet";

export function resolveModelId(qnaModel: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (qnaModel && qnaModel in CHAT_MODEL_MAP) return CHAT_MODEL_MAP[qnaModel as QnaModel];
  return CHAT_MODEL_MAP[DEFAULT_QNA_MODEL];
}

// ---- Audio models (STT / TTS) -------------------------------------------
// Whisper is free on this gateway ($0/$0 per 1M) - no reason to use a
// pricier transcription model for a text-first chatbot with occasional
// voice input.
export const STT_MODEL = process.env.STT_MODEL || "openai.global.whisper-1";

// Only speech/TTS model present anywhere in the current model list.
export const TTS_MODEL = process.env.TTS_MODEL || "openai.gpt-4o-mini-tts";

// Fallback voice when a persona has no gender set for some reason - see
// app/api/conversations/route.ts, which normally resolves this per-persona
// from personas.gender ("female" -> "nova", "male" -> "onyx").
export const TTS_VOICE_FEMALE = process.env.TTS_VOICE_FEMALE || "nova";
export const TTS_VOICE_MALE = process.env.TTS_VOICE_MALE || "onyx";
export const TTS_VOICE = process.env.TTS_VOICE || TTS_VOICE_FEMALE;

===== FILE START: lib/llm/resolveContext.ts =====
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import {
  toPersonaDTO,
  type AvatarCombinationRow,
  type AvatarRow,
  type Persona,
  type PersonaRow,
} from "@/lib/admin/types";

export type ResolvedChatContext = {
  avatarName: string;
  persona: Persona;
  knowledgeSourceIds: string[];
};

/**
 * Resolves everything /api/chat needs for a *persisted* avatar/persona pair
 * — the real employee-facing flow (/experience -> /conversation). A
 * persona can in principle be reused across avatars, so it's the
 * avatar_combinations row (not the persona alone) that says which
 * knowledge source(s) apply to this particular pairing.
 *
 * Accepts any one of combinationId, personaId, or avatarId+personaId —
 * whatever the caller already has on hand.
 */
export async function resolveChatContext({
  personaId,
  avatarId,
  combinationId,
}: {
  personaId?: string;
  avatarId?: string;
  combinationId?: string;
}): Promise<ResolvedChatContext> {
  let resolvedPersonaId = personaId ?? null;
  let resolvedAvatarId = avatarId ?? null;
  let knowledgeSourceIds: string[] = [];

  if (combinationId) {
    const { data: combo, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("avatar_combinations")
      .select("*")
      .eq("id", combinationId)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (error) throw error;
    if (!combo) throw new Error(`Combination ${combinationId} not found`);

    const comboRow = combo as AvatarCombinationRow;
    resolvedPersonaId = resolvedPersonaId ?? comboRow.persona_id;
    resolvedAvatarId = comboRow.avatar_id;
    knowledgeSourceIds = comboRow.knowledge_source_ids ?? [];
  }

  if (!resolvedPersonaId) {
    throw new Error("personaId or combinationId is required to resolve a chat context");
  }

  const { data: persona, error: personaError } = await supabaseAdmin
    .from("personas")
    .select("*")
    .eq("id", resolvedPersonaId)
    .maybeSingle();
  if (personaError) throw personaError;
  if (!persona) throw new Error(`Persona ${resolvedPersonaId} not found`);

  const personaRow = persona as PersonaRow;
  resolvedAvatarId = resolvedAvatarId ?? personaRow.avatar_id;

  // No combination row looked up yet (caller passed personaId/avatarId
  // directly, e.g. from the /conversation?avatar=&persona= query params) —
  // find the one linking this exact avatar+persona pairing so the right
  // knowledge source(s) get searched.
  if (knowledgeSourceIds.length === 0 && resolvedAvatarId) {
    const { data: combo, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("avatar_combinations")
      .select("*")
      .eq("avatar_id", resolvedAvatarId)
      .eq("persona_id", resolvedPersonaId)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (error) throw error;
    if (combo) knowledgeSourceIds = (combo as AvatarCombinationRow).knowledge_source_ids ?? [];
  }

  let avatarName = personaRow.name;
  if (resolvedAvatarId) {
    const { data: avatar, error: avatarError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("avatars")
      .select("*")
      .eq("id", resolvedAvatarId)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (avatarError) throw avatarError;
    if (avatar) avatarName = (avatar as AvatarRow).name;
  }

  return {
    avatarName,
    persona: toPersonaDTO(personaRow),
    knowledgeSourceIds,
  };
}

===== FILE END: lib/llm/resolveContext.ts =====

===== FILE START: lib/llm/sttClient.ts =====
import "server-only";
import { STT_MODEL } from "./models";

function getEndpoint(): string {
  const base = process.env.AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT;
  if (!base) throw new Error("Missing AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT in .env.local");
  return `${base.replace(/\/+$/, "")}/audio/transcriptions`;
}

/**
 * Transcribes an audio buffer (recorded user voice message) to text using
 * Whisper. openai.global.whisper-1 is free on this gateway - see models.ts.
 */
export async function transcribeAudio(
  audioBuffer: Buffer,
  filename = "audio.webm",
  mimeType = "audio/webm"
): Promise<string> {
  const apiKey = process.env.AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY;
  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("Missing AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY in .env.local");

  const url = getEndpoint();

  // Node's Buffer type isn't directly assignable to the DOM BlobPart type
  // (TS2322 - Buffer<ArrayBufferLike> vs ArrayBufferView<ArrayBuffer>).
  // Wrapping in a fresh Uint8Array normalizes it to a plain
  // ArrayBuffer-backed view that Blob's constructor accepts cleanly.
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(audioBuffer);
  const form = new FormData();
  form.append("file", new Blob([bytes], { type: mimeType }), filename);
  form.append("model", STT_MODEL);

  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "api-key": apiKey },
    body: form,
  });

  if (!res.ok) {
    const text = await res.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`Transcription request failed (${res.status}) URL=${url} Response=${text}`);
  }

  const json = (await res.json()) as { text: string };
  return json.text;
}

===== FILE END: lib/llm/sttClient.ts =====

===== FILE START: lib/llm/systemPrompt.ts =====
import type { RetrievedChunk } from "@/lib/ingestion/retrieve";

// Deliberately a superset of both shapes /api/chat can receive a persona
// as: the full DB `Persona` DTO (lib/admin/types.ts) for the real
// employee-facing /conversation flow, and the avatar wizard's in-memory
// draft (WizardData["persona"]) for the pre-publish "Test" step.
//
// expertise/formality/empathy/humour/confidence/forbiddenTopics/
// escalationInstructions were removed on 2026-07-30 - they were read here
// previously but never existed as columns on the real `personas` table
// (see supabase/migrations/0004_persona_gender.sql's comment for the full
// audit), so `select("*")` always returned them as undefined and this
// entire tone/guardrail paragraph was silently a no-op. If/when those
// fields get real columns + admin UI support again, reintroduce the
// corresponding paragraphs below using the same pattern.
export type PersonaPromptInput = {
  name?: string;
  prompt?: string;
  roleTitle?: string;
  department?: string;
};

export function buildSystemPrompt({
  avatarName,
  persona,
  contextChunks,
}: {
  avatarName: string;
  persona: PersonaPromptInput;
  contextChunks: RetrievedChunk[];
}): string {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  const displayName = persona.name || avatarName;

  lines.push(`You are ${displayName}, a digital human avatar built by PwC Tunisia for internal employees.`);

  if (persona.roleTitle) {
    lines.push(`Role: ${persona.roleTitle}${persona.department ? ` — ${persona.department}` : ""}.`);
  }

  if (persona.prompt?.trim()) {
    lines.push("");
    lines.push("Persona instructions from the admin who configured you:");
    lines.push(persona.prompt.trim());
  }

  lines.push("");
  if (contextChunks.length > 0) {
    lines.push(
      "Use the following knowledge-base excerpts to ground your answer when they're relevant. Don't invent " +
        "policy details that aren't supported by them — if the excerpts don't cover the question, say you're " +
        "not certain and offer to escalate instead of guessing."
    );
    contextChunks.forEach((chunk, i) => {
      lines.push(`\n[Source ${i + 1}]\n${chunk.content}`);
    });
  } else {
    lines.push(
      "No knowledge-base excerpts were retrieved for this question (either nothing matched, or no knowledge " +
        "source is attached yet). Answer from general reasoning and be upfront that you don't have a specific " +
        "document to point to."
    );
  }

  lines.push("");
  lines.push(
    "Keep replies conversational and concise — two to four sentences unless the question genuinely needs a " +
      "list or step-by-step instructions. This is a spoken/voice conversation, not a written report."
  );

  return lines.join("\n");
}

===== FILE END: lib/llm/systemPrompt.ts =====

===== FILE START: lib/llm/ttsClient.ts =====
import "server-only";
import { TTS_MODEL, TTS_VOICE } from "./models";

function getEndpoint(): string {
  const base = process.env.AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT;
  if (!base) throw new Error("Missing AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT in .env.local");
  return `${base.replace(/\/+$/, "")}/audio/speech`;
}

/**
 * Converts text to speech using gpt-4o-mini-tts. Language is auto-detected
 * from the input text - no manual language param needed, unlike the old
 * browser SpeechSynthesis approach (see hooks/useTextToSpeech.ts).
 */
export async function synthesizeSpeech(
  text: string,
  opts?: { voice?: string; format?: "mp3" | "opus" | "aac" | "flac" | "wav" | "pcm" }
): Promise<Buffer> {
  const apiKey = process.env.AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY;
  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("Missing AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY in .env.local");
  console.log("TTS model being sent:", opts?.voice || TTS_VOICE, "| model:", TTS_MODEL);
  const url = getEndpoint();

  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      "api-key": apiKey,
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      model: TTS_MODEL,
      voice: opts?.voice || TTS_VOICE,
      input: text,
      response_format: opts?.format || "mp3",
    }),
  });

  if (!res.ok) {
    const errText = await res.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`TTS request failed (${res.status}) URL=${url} Response=${errText}`);
  }

  return Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
}

===== FILE START: README.md =====
# Avatar Chatbot — PwC Internal Digital Human Platform

A Next.js application that lets employees have voice or text conversations
with AI-powered digital human avatars. Each avatar is backed by a
configurable persona (name, role, tone, voice, knowledge base) and a RAG
pipeline over internal documents. Admins build and publish avatars through a
step-by-step wizard; employees pick an avatar and talk to it via text or
voice.

---

## Tech Stack

| Layer                | Technology                                                |
|----------------------|------------------------------------------------------------|
| Framework            | Next.js 16 (App Router, Route Handlers)                    |
| UI                   | React 19, Tailwind CSS 4, Framer Motion, Lucide icons       |
| Language             | TypeScript                                                  |
| Database             | Supabase (Postgres + pgvector)                              |
| Chat LLM             | Anthropic Claude — Opus / Sonnet / Haiku (via internal gateway, AWS Bedrock route) |
| Embeddings           | Azure OpenAI `text-embedding-3-small`                       |
| Speech-to-text       | Whisper (`openai.global.whisper-1`)                         |
| Text-to-speech       | `gpt-4o-mini-tts`                                           |
| LLM Gateway          | Internal PwC shared GenAI service (LiteLLM-based proxy)     |
| Document ingestion   | `pdf-parse`, `unpdf`, `mammoth` (.docx), `@mozilla/readability` + `jsdom` (web pages) |
| Realtime transport   | `ws` (used internally by the ingestion/utility layer)       |

---

## Requirements

- **Node.js 20+**
- **npm** (or a compatible package manager)
- A **Supabase** project (Postgres with the `pgvector` extension available)
- An API key for the internal PwC GenAI gateway, with access to:
  - Chat completions (Anthropic Claude models via Bedrock route)
  - Embeddings (Azure OpenAI)
  - Audio transcription (Whisper)
  - Audio speech / TTS (`gpt-4o-mini-tts`)

---

## Environment Variables

Create a `.env.local` file in the project root:

```env
# --- Supabase ---
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your-anon-key
SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY=your-service-role-key

# --- Internal GenAI Gateway ---
# Single endpoint + key used for chat, embeddings, STT, and TTS. The
# gateway routes by the `model` string in each request body.
AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT=https://genai-sharedservice-emea.pwcinternal.com
AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY=your-gateway-api-key

# --- Optional: override individual model tiers without touching code ---
# CHAT_MODEL_QUALITY=bedrock.anthropic.claude-opus-4-5
# CHAT_MODEL_BALANCED=bedrock.anthropic.claude-sonnet-4-5
# CHAT_MODEL_FAST=bedrock.anthropic.claude-haiku-4-5
# STT_MODEL=openai.global.whisper-1
# TTS_MODEL=openai.gpt-4o-mini-tts
# TTS_VOICE_FEMALE=nova
# TTS_VOICE_MALE=onyx

# --- Optional: embeddings tuning ---
# AZURE_OPENAI_EMBEDDING_MODEL=azure.text-embedding-3-small
# AZURE_OPENAI_EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS=512
```

**Do not commit `.env.local`.**

---

## Models Used

| Purpose        | Model                                   | Notes                                      |
|-----------------|------------------------------------------|---------------------------------------------|
| Chat — Quality tier  | `bedrock.anthropic.claude-opus-4-5`   | Persona `qna_model = "Claude Opus"`         |
| Chat — Balanced tier | `bedrock.anthropic.claude-sonnet-4-5` | Persona `qna_model = "Claude Sonnet"` (default) |
| Chat — Fast tier     | `bedrock.anthropic.claude-haiku-4-5`  | Persona `qna_model = "Claude Haiku"`        |
| Embeddings           | `azure.text-embedding-3-small`        | 512 dimensions, used for RAG vector search  |
| Speech-to-text (STT) | `openai.global.whisper-1`             | Free tier on the gateway                    |
| Text-to-speech (TTS) | `openai.gpt-4o-mini-tts`              | Voice picked per persona: `nova` (female) / `onyx` (male) |

All models are routed through the internal GenAI gateway using a single
`AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT` + `AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY` pair — the gateway
dispatches to the correct upstream provider (Azure, OpenAI, AWS Bedrock)
based on the `model` string sent in each request.

---

## Database Setup

The full schema lives in a single migration:

```
supabase/migrations/0001_init.sql
```

Run it once against a fresh Supabase project (SQL editor or CLI). It creates:

- `avatars` — avatar identity (name, image, owner, body type, publish status)
- `personas` — persona configuration (prompt, Q&A model tier, creativity,
  role/department, **gender** for TTS voice selection)
- `knowledge_sources` / `knowledge_files` / `knowledge_chunks` — RAG
  document storage and pgvector embeddings
- `avatar_combinations` — links an avatar + persona + set of knowledge
  sources into a publishable combination
- `conversations` / `conversation_messages` — session and transcript logging
- `tests` — test-run tracking (Scenario/Regression/Smoke)
- `profiles` — links to Supabase `auth.users`, with an `employee`/`admin`
  role enum

The migration also sets up required Postgres extensions (`pgcrypto` for
UUID generation, `vector` for embeddings), the `user_role` enum, and
indexes on every foreign key + the `ivfflat` vector index needed for
performant similarity search.

---

## Getting Started

```bash
# 1. Install dependencies
npm install

# 2. Apply the database schema (Supabase SQL editor or CLI)
#    Run supabase/migrations/0001_init.sql

# 3. Create .env.local (see Environment Variables above)

# 4. Run the dev server
npm run dev
```

Open [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000).

### Other scripts

```bash
npm run build       # Production build
npm run start        # Run the production build
npm run lint          # Lint the codebase
npm run dev:https     # Dev server over HTTPS (needed for mic access on some setups)
```

---

## Core Flows

**Employee — talking to an avatar**
1. `/select-role` → `/experience` → pick an avatar → `/conversation`
2. `ConversationChat.tsx` starts a session (`POST /api/conversations`),
   which resolves the persona's `gender` into a TTS voice and returns
   avatar/persona display info.
3. Each turn: `useChatStream` streams a reply from `POST /api/chat`
   (RAG retrieval → system prompt → Claude → SSE deltas); `useTextToSpeech`
   sends the finished reply to `POST /api/tts` and plays back the audio.
4. Voice input (optional) is captured via `useSpeechToText` (browser Web
   Speech API) and sent as a normal chat message once finalized.

**Admin — building an avatar**
1. `/admin/avatars/new` launches the wizard (`AvatarWizard.tsx`).
2. Steps: avatar identity → persona (existing or new, incl. gender) →
   knowledge sources → live test chat (`StepTest.tsx`, calls `/api/chat`
   in preview mode with an in-memory persona draft).
3. Publishing (`POST /api/admin/avatars/publish`) creates the avatar,
   persona, knowledge source(s), and an `avatar_combinations` row in one
   transaction-like flow (with manual rollback on failure).

---

## RAG Pipeline

`lib/ingestion/pipeline.ts` handles turning uploaded files or URLs into
searchable knowledge:
1. **Fetch** — download the file or scrape the URL (`fetchUrl.ts`, using
   `@mozilla/readability` + `jsdom` for clean article extraction).
2. **Extract** — pull raw text from PDFs (`pdf-parse` / `unpdf`) or `.docx`
   files (`mammoth`).
3. **Chunk** — split into overlapping text chunks (`chunk.ts`).
4. **Embed** — call the gateway's embeddings endpoint (`embed.ts`).
5. **Store** — persist chunks + vectors into `knowledge_chunks`.

At chat time, `chatPipeline.ts` runs similarity search across every
knowledge source attached to the active persona in parallel, merges and
re-ranks the results, and injects the top matches into the system prompt
built by `systemPrompt.ts`.

===== FILE END: README.md =====

===== FILE START: scripts/test-retrieval.ts =====
/**
 * Manual RAG retrieval sanity check.
 *
 * Run with: npx tsx scripts/test-retrieval.ts "<question>" "<knowledge_source_id>" [matchCount] ["<substring to find>"]
 *
 * .env.local is loaded directly inside this file (see dotenv.config below) —
 * no dotenv-cli wrapper needed, which avoids Windows issues with nested
 * npx processes and extensionless shell-script binaries in node_modules/.bin.
 */
import dotenv from "dotenv";
dotenv.config({ path: ".env.local" });

import dns from "node:dns";
dns.setDefaultResultOrder("ipv4first");

async function main() {
  // Dynamic import, not static — static imports are hoisted and run before
  // dotenv.config() above, which would make retrieve.ts's transitive import
  // of lib/supabase/admin.ts throw on missing env vars before they're loaded.
  const { searchKnowledgeChunks } = await import("../lib/ingestion/retrieve");

  const question = process.argv[2];
  const sourceId = process.argv[3];
  const matchCount = process.argv[4] ? parseInt(process.argv[4], 10) : 6;
  const findSubstring = process.argv[5];

  if (!question || !sourceId) {
    console.error(
      'Usage: tsx scripts/test-retrieval.ts "<question>" "<knowledge_source_id>" [matchCount] ["<substring to find>"]'
    );
    process.exit(1);
  }

  console.log(`\nQuery: "${question}"  (matchCount=${matchCount})\n`);

  const results = await searchKnowledgeChunks(question, sourceId, matchCount);

  if (results.length === 0) {
    console.log("No chunks returned at all — check filter_source_id matches an indexed source.");
    return;
  }

  for (const [i, r] of results.entries()) {
    const isTarget = findSubstring && r.content.includes(findSubstring);
    console.log(
      `#${i + 1}${isTarget ? "  <-- MATCH" : ""}  similarity=${r.similarity.toFixed(4)}  chunk_id=${r.id}`
    );
    console.log(`    ${r.content.slice(0, 200).replace(/\n/g, " ")}...`);
    console.log();
  }

  if (findSubstring) {
    const rank = results.findIndex((r) => r.content.includes(findSubstring));
    console.log(
      rank === -1
        ? `"${findSubstring}" not found in top ${matchCount} results at all — try increasing matchCount further.`
        : `"${findSubstring}" found at rank #${rank + 1} with similarity ${results[rank].similarity.toFixed(4)}.`
    );
  }

  const top = results[0].similarity;
  if (top < 0.3) {
    console.log(
      "⚠️  Top similarity is quite low — either the question isn't covered by this source, or retrieval quality needs attention."
    );
  }
}

main().catch((err) => {
  console.error("Retrieval test failed:", err);
  process.exit(1);
});

===== FILE END: scripts/test-retrieval.ts =====

===== FILE START: supabase/migrations/0001_init.sql =====
-- supabase/migrations/0001_init.sql
-- Consolidated baseline schema for the Avatar Chatbot platform.
-- Supersedes all previous incremental migrations (0001-0005) - this is the
-- single source of truth for a fresh database. Run this once on an empty
-- Supabase project.
--
-- Order matters: tables are created in dependency order (referenced tables
-- before the tables that FK to them).

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Extensions
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- gen_random_uuid() - used as the default for every table's `id` column.
create extension if not exists pgcrypto;

-- vector type - used by knowledge_chunks.embedding for similarity search
-- (see lib/ingestion/retrieve.ts). 512 dimensions matches
-- AZURE_OPENAI_EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS / azure.text-embedding-3-small's
-- configured output size (see lib/ingestion/embed.ts) - adjust the
-- dimension below if that env var is ever changed.
create extension if not exists vector;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Enums
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Used by public.profiles.role. Referenced as "USER-DEFINED" in Supabase's
-- schema export - this is the actual type definition.
do $$
begin
  if not exists (select 1 from pg_type where typname = 'user_role') then
    create type user_role as enum ('employee', 'admin');
  end if;
end $$;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- avatars
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

create table public.avatars (
  id uuid not null default gen_random_uuid(),
  name text not null,
  image_url text,
  owner text not null,
  owner_type text not null check (owner_type = any (array['Organisation'::text, 'Individual'::text])),
  body_type text not null check (body_type = any (array['Full body'::text, 'Half body'::text, 'Head only'::text])),
  status text not null default 'Private'::text check (status = any (array['Private'::text, 'Public'::text, 'Archived'::text])),
  created_at timestamp with time zone not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamp with time zone not null default now(),
  constraint avatars_pkey primary key (id)
);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- personas
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

create table public.personas (
  id uuid not null default gen_random_uuid(),
  avatar_id uuid,
  type text not null default ''::text,
  name text not null,
  prompt text not null default ''::text,
  qna_model text not null default 'Claude Sonnet'::text
    check (qna_model = any (array['Claude Opus'::text, 'Claude Sonnet'::text, 'Claude Haiku'::text])),
  creativity integer not null default 35 check (creativity >= 0 and creativity <= 100),
  status text not null default 'Private'::text check (status = any (array['Private'::text, 'Public'::text, 'Archived'::text])),
  version text not null default 'v1.0'::text,
  role_title text not null default ''::text,
  department text not null default ''::text,
  -- Drives TTS voice selection - see app/api/conversations/route.ts (maps
  -- to "nova"/"onyx") and lib/llm/ttsClient.ts.
  gender text not null default 'female'::text check (gender = any (array['female'::text, 'male'::text])),
  created_at timestamp with time zone not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamp with time zone not null default now(),
  constraint personas_pkey primary key (id),
  constraint personas_avatar_id_fkey foreign key (avatar_id) references public.avatars(id)
);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- knowledge_sources
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

create table public.knowledge_sources (
  id uuid not null default gen_random_uuid(),
  name text not null,
  description text not null default ''::text,
  status text not null default 'Processing'::text check (status = any (array['Indexed'::text, 'Processing'::text, 'Error'::text])),
  created_at timestamp with time zone not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamp with time zone not null default now(),
  constraint knowledge_sources_pkey primary key (id)
);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- knowledge_files
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

create table public.knowledge_files (
  id uuid not null default gen_random_uuid(),
  knowledge_source_id uuid not null,
  name text not null,
  size_bytes bigint not null default 0,
  storage_path text,
  created_at timestamp with time zone not null default now(),
  source_url text,
  constraint knowledge_files_pkey primary key (id),
  constraint knowledge_files_knowledge_source_id_fkey foreign key (knowledge_source_id) references public.knowledge_sources(id)
);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- avatar_combinations
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

create table public.avatar_combinations (
  id uuid not null default gen_random_uuid(),
  avatar_id uuid not null,
  persona_id uuid not null,
  knowledge_source_ids uuid[] not null default '{}'::uuid[],
  created_at timestamp with time zone not null default now(),
  constraint avatar_combinations_pkey primary key (id),
  constraint avatar_combinations_avatar_id_fkey foreign key (avatar_id) references public.avatars(id),
  constraint avatar_combinations_persona_id_fkey foreign key (persona_id) references public.personas(id)
);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- conversations
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

create table public.conversations (
  id uuid not null default gen_random_uuid(),
  name text not null,
  is_public boolean not null default false,
  status text not null default 'Active'::text check (status = any (array['Active'::text, 'Resolved'::text, 'Escalated'::text])),
  personal_avatar_request boolean not null default false,
  active_avatar_id uuid,
  persona_id uuid,
  conversation_type text not null default 'Voice'::text check (conversation_type = any (array['Voice'::text, 'Text'::text])),
  language text not null default 'English'::text,
  creator_user text not null default 'anonymous'::text,
  started_at timestamp with time zone not null default now(),
  ended_at timestamp with time zone,
  duration_seconds integer,
  combination_id uuid,
  constraint conversations_pkey primary key (id),
  constraint conversations_active_avatar_id_fkey foreign key (active_avatar_id) references public.avatars(id),
  constraint conversations_persona_id_fkey foreign key (persona_id) references public.personas(id),
  constraint conversations_combination_id_fkey foreign key (combination_id) references public.avatar_combinations(id)
);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- tests
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

create table public.tests (
  id uuid not null default gen_random_uuid(),
  name text not null,
  conversation_id uuid,
  type text not null check (type = any (array['Scenario'::text, 'Regression'::text, 'Smoke'::text])),
  total_questions integer not null default 0,
  status text not null default 'Running'::text check (status = any (array['Pass'::text, 'Running'::text, 'Review'::text, 'Fail'::text])),
  created_at timestamp with time zone not null default now(),
  constraint tests_pkey primary key (id),
  constraint tests_conversation_id_fkey foreign key (conversation_id) references public.conversations(id)
);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- profiles (links to Supabase auth.users)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

create table public.profiles (
  id uuid not null,
  email text not null,
  role user_role not null default 'employee'::user_role,
  created_at timestamp with time zone not null default now(),
  constraint profiles_pkey primary key (id),
  constraint profiles_id_fkey foreign key (id) references auth.users(id)
);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- knowledge_chunks (RAG vector store)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

create table public.knowledge_chunks (
  id uuid not null default gen_random_uuid(),
  knowledge_source_id uuid not null,
  knowledge_file_id uuid not null,
  chunk_index integer not null,
  content text not null,
  -- Dimension must match AZURE_OPENAI_EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS (see
  -- lib/ingestion/embed.ts) - default is 512 for azure.text-embedding-3-small.
  embedding vector(512) not null,
  created_at timestamp with time zone not null default now(),
  constraint knowledge_chunks_pkey primary key (id),
  constraint knowledge_chunks_knowledge_source_id_fkey foreign key (knowledge_source_id) references public.knowledge_sources(id),
  constraint knowledge_chunks_knowledge_file_id_fkey foreign key (knowledge_file_id) references public.knowledge_files(id)
);

-- Vector similarity index - without this, searchKnowledgeChunks()
-- (lib/ingestion/retrieve.ts) does a full sequential scan per query, which
-- gets slow as knowledge_chunks grows. ivfflat is a good default for
-- moderate-sized collections; switch to hnsw if pgvector version supports
-- it and recall/latency tuning is needed later.
create index knowledge_chunks_embedding_idx
  on public.knowledge_chunks
  using ivfflat (embedding vector_cosine_ops)
  with (lists = 100);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- conversation_messages (per-turn transcript log)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

create table public.conversation_messages (
  id uuid not null default gen_random_uuid(),
  conversation_id uuid not null,
  role text not null check (role = any (array['user'::text, 'assistant'::text])),
  content text not null,
  created_at timestamp with time zone not null default now(),
  constraint conversation_messages_pkey primary key (id),
  constraint conversation_messages_conversation_id_fkey foreign key (conversation_id) references public.conversations(id)
);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Helpful indexes for common lookups (not in the original export, but
-- worth having from day one - foreign keys are NOT automatically indexed
-- in Postgres, and these columns are queried constantly by the app).
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

create index personas_avatar_id_idx on public.personas(avatar_id);
create index avatar_combinations_avatar_id_idx on public.avatar_combinations(avatar_id);
create index avatar_combinations_persona_id_idx on public.avatar_combinations(persona_id);
create index conversations_persona_id_idx on public.conversations(persona_id);
create index conversations_combination_id_idx on public.conversations(combination_id);
create index conversation_messages_conversation_id_idx on public.conversation_messages(conversation_id);
create index knowledge_files_knowledge_source_id_idx on public.knowledge_files(knowledge_source_id);
create index knowledge_chunks_knowledge_source_id_idx on public.knowledge_chunks(knowledge_source_id);
create index knowledge_chunks_knowledge_file_id_idx on public.knowledge_chunks(knowledge_file_id);

===== FILE END: supabase/migrations/0001_init.sql =====

===== FILE START: test-realtime.mjs =====
import WebSocket from "ws";

const API_KEY = "sk-aRt4ew2R_UM4ewX9TMpMpw"; // paste your real key
const MODEL = "openai/gpt-realtime-2";

const urlsToTry = [
  `wss://genai-sharedservice-emea.pwcinternal.com/v1/realtime?model=${encodeURIComponent(MODEL)}`,
  `wss://genai-sharedservice-emea.pwcinternal.com/realtime?model=${encodeURIComponent(MODEL)}`,
];

const headerVariants = [
  { name: "Authorization Bearer", headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${API_KEY}` } },
  { name: "api-key", headers: { "api-key": API_KEY } },
];

async function testConnection(url, headerVariant) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    console.log(`\n--- Trying: ${url}`);
    console.log(`--- Headers: ${headerVariant.name}`);

    const ws = new WebSocket(url, { headers: headerVariant.headers });

    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      console.log("TIMEOUT — no response after 8s");
      ws.terminate();
      resolve();
    }, 8000);

    ws.on("open", () => {
      console.log("✅ SUCCESS — connection opened!");
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      ws.close();
      resolve();
    });

    ws.on("unexpected-response", (req, res) => {
      console.log(`❌ Unexpected response: ${res.statusCode} ${res.statusMessage}`);
      let body = "";
      res.on("data", (chunk) => (body += chunk));
      res.on("end", () => {
        console.log("Body:", body);
        clearTimeout(timeout);
        resolve();
      });
    });

    ws.on("error", (err) => {
      console.log("❌ Error:", err.message);
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      resolve();
    });
  });
}

for (const url of urlsToTry) {
  for (const headerVariant of headerVariants) {
    await testConnection(url, headerVariant);
  }
}

===== FILE END: test-realtime.mjs =====

===== FILE START: unbundle.js =====
// unbundle.js — reverses bundle.js: reads project-bundle.txt and recreates
// every file at its original relative path, making directories as needed.
//
// Usage (on the NEW laptop):
//   1. Put project-bundle.txt next to this script (or pass a path as argv[2])
//   2. node unbundle.js
//   3. npm install   (regenerates node_modules — wasn't in the bundle)

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

const bundlePath = process.argv[2] || path.join(process.cwd(), "project-bundle.txt");
const OUT_ROOT = process.cwd();

if (!fs.existsSync(bundlePath)) {
  console.error(`Can't find ${bundlePath}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

const text = fs.readFileSync(bundlePath, "utf8");
const fileRegex = /===== FILE START: (.+?) =====\n([\s\S]*?)\n===== FILE END: \1 =====/g;

let match;
let count = 0;

while ((match = fileRegex.exec(text)) !== null) {
  const relPath = match[1].trim();
  const content = match[2];
  const fullPath = path.join(OUT_ROOT, relPath);

  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(fullPath), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(fullPath, content, "utf8");
  count++;
  console.log("wrote", relPath);
}

console.log(`\nRestored ${count} files into ${OUT_ROOT}`);
console.log("Next: npm install, then npm run dev");

===== FILE END: unbundle.js =====
